Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.3

Chapter 2592
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
The Method to Recover the Li Clan
After a Saint was conferred as a Saint, the other Saints would usually welcome them at the Saint Hall.
It was a new circle for the newly promoted Saints, even though there were only a few dozen people in it.
However, the situation was different this time. Too many things had happened in the Divine Realm.
The Aristocrat Alliance was essentially formed by the Xuanyuan Guard. Previously, they had been at odds with Luo Zheng and the others. Now that Luo Zheng had single-handedly overthrown the entire Xuanyuan Guard, no one in the entire Divine Dominion dared to oppose them.
Especially the scene of Qing Emperor being killed, it left a deep impression in the minds of all the Saints.
Luo Zheng was not restricted by the rules of the Governing Slate. He could easily destroy the other party's universe. It could be said that the fate of the Saints was in Luo Zheng's hands. For a moment, the Saints had placed themselves in a troublesome position.
If Luo Zheng really pursued the Saints of the Wealthy Clan Alliance, what should they do?
"Revise the rules?"
"It's extremely difficult to change even one of the rules on the Governing Slate. Can he revise it at will?"
"Luo Zheng summoned us. Let's go …"
Leng Wuyu and the other newly promoted Saints did not know what had happened outside the Saint Mountain. Hearing that Luo Zheng had summoned the Saints for an audience? He felt that it was rather strange.
Luo Zheng was powerful, but it was not to the extent that he could command all the Saints at will, right?
Especially the Saint Emperor of the Leng Clan, Tang Lun, Mu Haiji, and the others. It was impossible for them to listen to this guy's orders …
However, the next scene made Leng Wuyu and the others dumbfounded.
The Saints came one after another. The moment they appeared, they cupped their hands and bowed to Luo Zheng.
"How can this guy make the Saints submit …" Leng Wuyu's heart was in turmoil.
Tang Lun, Leng Yao, Fang Shixing, and the other Saints' expressions were extremely unnatural.
They were extremely unwilling to come to the Saint Hall to meet Luo Zheng, but they had no choice but to come!
These people had attacked Luo Zheng many times in the past. Now that Luo Zheng wanted to settle the score, none of them could escape.
Luo Zheng's gaze swept past the Saints' faces. He did not reveal any emotions. Instead, he said faintly, "There's one person short. Why is Mu Haiji not here?"
"Mu Haiji …" Tang Lun also had some doubts.
During this period of time, Tang, Fang, Leng, Qin, and the other Saints of the original Aristocratic Alliance had gathered together to discuss countermeasures, but they had never seen Mu Haiji.
As soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, a spatial fluctuation spread out, and a figure quietly appeared. It was Mu Haiji.
Mu Haiji and Luo Zheng looked at each other. Then, Mu Haiji braced himself and said, "I found something in the Great Extreme Space. I think it will be of great use to you, Luo Zheng."
As Mu Haiji spoke, he released a flying boat from his spatial ring. This was the High Firmament Flying Boat used by the Xuan Yuan Guards.
Seeing the flying boat, Luo Zheng says with a faint smile: "You need so many days to figure out a flying boat? Or have you thought about using this flying boat to escape to the Outer Realm? "
Hearing this, Mu Haiji's expression changed slightly. He immediately said, "The God's Domain is the foundation of us Saints. I have no intention of escaping!"
"Unfortunately, you are a Saint. Your inner world has fused with the universe, so you can't escape. If you were a Perfection Saint or a Quasi-Saint, I'm afraid the situation would be different, right?" Luo Zheng questioned.
Mu Haiji's expression changed slightly. He didn't dare to argue with Luo Zheng.
When Mu Haiji found this flying boat, he did have such an idea. If he could use this flying boat to escape to the mother world, it might be a way out. However, he was a Saint after all, and the Great Extreme Universe was a place that made him successful, but it also bound him.
After thinking about it, he could only obediently hand over the flying boat.
Mu Haiji didn't know that everything in the God's Domain was under Luo Zheng's control.
From time to time, powerful lightning bolts would burst out from the top of the Sea of Time. These lightning bolts shot out in all directions.
It didn't matter if they were hiding deep underground or in space, they couldn't escape the Lightning Tribulation.
Of the remaining five Xuanyuan Guards, three had already died. Only Wei Xian and another Xuanyuan Guard were left. Each of them cast an extremely powerful barrier and struggled to hold on. Wei Xian even drilled into the depths of the Black Fire Torrent.
However, these two Xuanyuan Guards were already at the end of their rope under the baptism of the lightning bolts. In less than three to five days, they would be completely struck by the lightning bolts.
Luo Zheng knew the movements of these Xuanyuan Guards anytime and anywhere. Of course, he also knew about the existence of this flying boat.
If Mu Haiji wanted to escape with the flying boat, Luo Zheng would immediately stop him. Moreover, he didn't dare to do so.
Luo Zheng didn't continue to pursue this issue. Instead, he said to all the Saints, "Today, I summoned all the Saints here to tell you that the rules of the God's Domain will be revised."
"The rules will be revised?"
"Then can the rules on the Governing Slate be changed at will?"
"…"
The Saints immediately discussed spiritedly. This was their biggest doubt.
Those rules not only limited the power of the Saints, but also protected them from killing each other.
"All the rules on the Governing Slate have already been erased by me," Luo Zheng replied.
Hearing this, the Saints all opened their mouths wide.
Especially the newly advanced Saints. Leng Wuyu stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
To the Saints, the Governing Slate was an ironclad rule that could not be violated. Yet, this fellow had actually flattened all the rules?
"In the future, there won't be any rich and powerful families. The only existence in the God's Domain that can surpass the sea of time will be my Li Clan," Luo Zheng announced.
When Luo Zheng said this, the Saints were also somewhat dumbfounded.
They more or less knew some of the legends of the Sacred Clan. That was the legendary Li Clan.
But hadn't the Sacred Clan been completely wiped out?
Just as the Saints were puzzled, Luo Zheng continued, "The God's Domain originally belonged to the Li Clan. I'm only doing this to return what belongs to them."
Li Luoshui's biggest dream was to awaken all the Li Clan members.
Although these Li Clan members had fallen countless years ago, their memories still existed. Once Li Luoshui completely controlled the reincarnation soul artifact, she could indeed do it.
The only problem was the bloodline. After reincarnating, the Li Clan members would only have their memories, but their bloodline no longer existed.
However, the problem of bloodline could be left to Elder Jin and the others to solve.
Actually, Li Luoshui's idea was more radical. In her eyes, everything in the God's Domain belonged to the Li Clan, including the Gods and the True Gods.
According to her idea, she could "awaken" all the Li Clan members. This was equivalent to forcefully stuffing the memories of the Li Clan members into everyone's minds. After all, these people were the reincarnations of the Li Clan members.
However, Luo Zheng, Luo Xiao, and the others were opposed to this. After all, this was too unreasonable. It was equivalent to erasing the memories of everyone in the God's Domain.
Thus, they chose a compromise, which was to let the newly born Gods in the God's Domain inherit the memories of the Li Clan members.

Chapter 2593
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Mu Ning
When Luo Zheng told them about the Li Clan's revival plan, the Sages fell silent.
No matter which side they stood on, they could not support Luo Zheng.
If the Li Clan really returned to the divine territory, they would occupy a large amount of the divine territory's resources.
These rich and powerful clans would even face competition with the ubiquitous Gods in the divine territory. How could they compete with the race that created this world?
But so what if they did not support him? Who could oppose Luo Zheng?
Moreover, what Luo Zheng said was not without reason. The divine territory originally belonged to the Li Clan. What they did was just returning it to its rightful owner.
Sage Bai Zixing coughed twice and asked, "But when the Li Clan grows stronger in the future, how can we coexist peacefully with them?"
"I will revise the rules. In the future, when fighting for the position of Sage, we will not fight with our lives. Instead, we will fight for a ranking in the Divine Jade Wall. Only the strongest few will be allowed to enter the Saint Mountain," Luo Zheng replied.
The process of fighting for the Holy Spring in the Saint Mountain was too tragic. Many True Gods and Perfected Sovereigns would die every time they fought. Luo Zheng's goal was to change this situation.
This was a good suggestion. The death of every Perfected True God and Quasi-Sage was a huge loss to the rich and powerful clans.
Actually, the Sages had discussed before that they would confer the title of Sage according to the ranking in the Divine Jade Wall.
However, the position of Sage was a fatal temptation to True Gods. No one was willing to give in. Therefore, every time the Saint Mountain descended, the rich and powerful clans would still fall into a frenzy of fighting.
If Luo Zheng could end this situation, they would naturally be willing.
"But the Sacred Clan … No, the Li Clan came from the Mother World. They have the bloodline of the Li Clan and are bound to be more outstanding than us. How can we compete with them?" the Sage of the Qin Clan asked worriedly.
Although the Sacred Clan had disappeared for countless eras, their influence was still there. These Sages more or less knew some inside information.
Before Luo Zheng could speak, Li Luoshui suddenly sneered. "You mean that because the Li Clan is outstanding, we have to give in to you? Or perhaps … none of you understand who the master of the entire God's Domain is. "
Li Luoshui's attitude was much more radical than Luo Zheng's. After all, in her eyes, the God's Domain was the last home of the Jiuli tribe. It was only after Pang Miao massacred them that the God's Domain appeared.
The Qin Clan's Saint opened his mouth but did not refute.
In the end, Li Luoshui was the one with reason, and her strength was also on her side …
Luo Zheng said, "Our God's Domain is not a peaceful place. In fact, the master who created the God's Domain has a great enemy. Those Xuan Yuan Guards were attracted by this enemy. No one can guarantee that this enemy will not invade again. Therefore, we have to become stronger to be able to resist. There is no need to discuss the issue of racial strife."
The winner was king. This was the truth even in the entire Chaos.
If they did not want to be eliminated, they could only force themselves to become stronger.
He tossed these questions aside. After all, he did not gather the Sages here to discuss with them.
Then Luo Zheng's gaze shifted back to Mu Haiji. He said to his father, "Father, how should we deal with the grudge between you and Mu Haiji?"
It's finally my turn? Mu Haiji's heart trembled.
However, since he did not run away, he planned to face Luo Xiao.
A cold light flashed through Li Luoshui's eyes. She had no sympathy for someone who had almost killed her husband.
Luo Xiao looked at Mu Haiji in silence for a while.
Back then, Gu Bei and Saint Jiu had set up all kinds of measures to deal with Mu Haiji's Universal War.
However, Luo Xiao's true enemy was still Dongfang Chunjun. Mu Haiji was just a pawn.
If it was not for Dongfang Chunjun holding the fort, Luo Xiao might not have taken Mu Haiji seriously.
"Although my Dayan Realm was almost destroyed by you, you still lost the Universal War. Moreover, you were only acting for Dongfang Chunjun. Now that Dongfang Chunjun has been executed, the hatred of the past has faded. I will not take your life, but only your position as a Saint. How does that sound?" Luo Xiao asked.
The Soul Lodging Ground could be removed, but the method was a bit troublesome. With Luo Zheng's current abilities, it was not difficult.
Hearing Luo Xiao's words, Mu Haiji's body trembled slightly. The other Saints also looked at him with sympathy.
Taking away Mu Haiji's position as a Saint was worse than killing him. After all, Mu Haiji's current strength, prestige, and the rise and fall of the Noble Clans were tied to his position as a Saint. If he lost the Dayan Realm, his cultivation would fall back to Great Perfection. It was equivalent to taking away everything that belonged to Mu Haiji.
However, it was still better than death. As long as he was alive, he could wait for the next Saint Mountain to descend and become a Saint again.
Mu Haiji was conflicted for a long time. After realizing that there was no other way out, he said, "This punishment is light. Mu Haiji is willing to accept it."
Tang Lun, Leng Yao, Fang Shixing, and the others all became nervous when Mu Haiji was stripped of his position as a Saint.
As members of the Noble Alliance, they had made many moves against Luo Zheng. If they were stripped of their positions as Saints, it would be terrible.
Luo Zheng naturally noticed these Saints' expressions.
As a former member of the Noble Alliance, he would naturally punish them, but that was a matter for the future. At this time, he said, "Today's summons ends here. Everyone can leave. I'm going to the Mu family's floating island."
Mu Haiji was stunned and asked, "Why are you going to the Mu family's floating island?"
"Take me to see Mu Ning." When Mu Ning was mentioned, Luo Zheng's gaze became gentler.
This little girl had been locked up by Mu Xuerong because of him. She should still be on the Mu family's floating island.
Thus, under Mu Haiji's lead, Luo Zheng went to the Mu family's floating island.
Luo Xiao, Li Luoshui, and the others took Chen Huangyi back to the Han family. The other Saints also left one after another.
Leng Wuyu and the other newly promoted Saints experienced this scene in the Holy Hall. They all felt like they had spilled sauce, and they all had mixed feelings.
They had once viewed becoming a Saint as the pinnacle and the ultimate dream of their lives. Now, they saw the Saints all submissive in front of Luo Zheng.
Especially Mu Haiji. He didn't even dare to resist when he was stripped of his position as a Saint.
They suddenly realized that becoming a Saint wasn't as powerful as they thought …
…
…
There was a cliff on the west side of the Mu family's floating island. This cliff was as smooth as a mirror. Standing in front of it, one could see one's own shadow.
"Mu Ning was locked up there. The one who ordered it was Mu Xuerong," Mu Haiji explained.
"I know …"
Mu Xuerong had personally told Luo Zheng, so he naturally knew.

Chapter 2594
Birth
During the years Mu Ning had been locked up, she had focused on cultivating.
Her talent wasn't bad, and she was now an upper True God.
Mu Xuerong had issued a ban that no one could release Mu Ning, so Mu Ning knew nothing about the outside world.
The sea had not been peaceful these days, and the Mu family's floating island had been bobbing up and down. It was inevitable that Mu Ning would be suspicious. Even a rich family's floating island had almost fallen into the Sea of Time. Something big must have happened in the Divine Realm.
Her heart was full of worry, and she couldn't help but think of Luo Zheng. Was he well all these years?
These thoughts spun in her mind, making it difficult for her to calm down.
But under the restraint of this God Forbidding Wall, it was impossible for her to escape, unless she could become a Perfect True God.
In just a few decades, cultivating from a lower True God to an upper True God was the limit. If she continued to advance, it would be difficult to break through unless she had a great opportunity.
Just as Mu Ning was thinking, she suddenly heard footsteps behind her.
"Who is it?"
Mu Ning turned her head, and her ponytail swayed along with it.
When she saw Luo Zheng, a strange look immediately appeared in her lively and sharp eyes. She seemed to be stunned as she said, "Luo Zheng! You actually came! "
After she blurted it out, her expression became vigilant and she hurriedly covered her mouth.
"What are you afraid of?" Seeing Mu Ning's cute action, Luo Zheng smiled.
Mu Ning lowered her voice and asked, "Did you sneak into the Mu family to take me away?"
This girl's imagination was too rich. Luo Zheng smiled and said, "I came to take you away, but not secretly."
"But my sister …" Mu Ning said worriedly.
She didn't understand Luo Zheng's meaning.
Take her away in broad daylight? This was the Mu family's floating island!
"She won't stop you," Luo Zheng said seriously.
"Why?" Mu Ning asked curiously.
"Mu Xuerong died in the Abyss Demon Realm," Luo Zheng replied.
Hearing this, Mu Ning's expression changed greatly.
Although she wasn't on good terms with Mu Xuerong, they were still sisters in the end. Even if Mu Xuerong had imprisoned Mu Ning, that was only for the sake of her little sister.
Now that she heard the bad news brought by Luo Zheng, she found it hard to accept.
"Why is it like this …" Mu Ning was already on the verge of tears.
Luo Zheng gently sighed. He didn't want to see Mu Ning sad, but there were some things that she would eventually learn. That was why he had told her about how Mu Xuerong had perished within the Yin Yang furnace.
Before Mu Xuerong died, she still cared about her little sister. She urged Luo Zheng to release Mu Ning no matter what.
After hearing this, Mu Ning became even sadder. She lay on Luo Zheng's shoulder and sobbed for a long time, as if she couldn't control herself.
Logically speaking, Mu Xuerong only had herself to blame, not to mention that this woman had repeatedly tried to kill her.
But seeing Mu Ning so sad, Luo Zheng couldn't bear it. He gently stroked Mu Ning's soft black hair and said, "Actually, it's not impossible to bring Mu Xuerong back to life …"
Mu Ning suddenly raised her head and stared at Luo Zheng in a daze. Her reddened eyes were filled with hope. "Luo Zheng, are you saying that you can bring my big sister back to life?"
Luo Zheng nodded. "I can do it."
Implanting the flames of memories and reincarnating wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng.
Mu Ning suddenly grabbed Luo Zheng's arm. "I beg you, Luo Zheng! Bring my big sister back to life! "
Of course, she knew about the enmity between Luo Zheng and her big sister. In the Sea of Time forbidden area, her big sister had almost killed Luo Zheng.
Although she wasn't too clear about what had happened afterwards, she understood Mu Xuerong's personality. She definitely wouldn't let Luo Zheng go.
Luo Zheng gazed at Mu Ning's white jade-like face. Her delicate and pitiful appearance made one's heart ache. He faintly smiled and said, "Reviving her is impossible. I can only let your big sister reincarnate. She can retain her memories of this life."
With everything in the Realm of the Gods under his control, Luo Zheng didn't fear a single Mu Xuerong. Not to mention, before her death, Mu Xuerong's temperament had undergone a significant change. Because of Mu Ning's face, Luo Zheng agreed to this matter.
… …
… …
The Governing Stone Tablet constantly released thunder punishment. It lasted for a full month before it subsided.
When the thunder punishment subsided, it meant that the Xuan Yuan Guards hidden in the Realm of the Gods had all fallen. Then, Luo Zheng informed Chi Yi and the others of where the Xuan Yuan Guards had fallen.
These Xuan Yuan Guards came from the Origin World, so they must have brought many things with them. Elder Jin and the others placed great importance on them, so they naturally wouldn't allow them to be scattered in the depths of the Realm of the Gods. Chi Yi and the others went to where these people had fallen to find them.
Another eight months passed … …
Luo Zheng and Dongfang Yingqing appeared in the Phaseless Zone, south of the Realm of the Gods. Below them was a Central God City, which was protected by a Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet.
"It's right below us," Luo Zheng said.
Dongfang Yingqing's face was filled with anticipation, and she said, "Then let's go down and take a look!"
Luo Zheng nodded, and then descended towards the Central God City with Dongfang Yingqing.
The instant they entered the Central God City, the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet guarding the Central God City suddenly activated. However, just as the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet was about to launch an attack, Luo Zheng thought, and the light of the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet dissipated.
Even the Governing Stone Tablet was under Luo Zheng's control, so a mere Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet was naturally nothing.
After the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet calmed down, he appeared in the City Lord's Mansion with Dongfang Yingqing.
At the same time the two of them entered the mansion, the loud and clear sound of a baby crying came from the rear courtyard of the City Lord's Mansion.
A hint of joy appeared on Dongfang Yingqing's face. "Is it him?"
"En," Luo Zheng replied.
Although he knew that the newborn baby was Hua Tianming's reincarnation, Luo Zheng still felt an indescribable sense of strangeness in his heart.
"Who is it! How dare you trespass into the City Lord's Mansion! "
"How dare you!"
The two of them brazenly barging into the mansion instantly attracted the attention of the guards.
The master of the Central God City was the first-tier Ye family, and the family head, Ye Zhengqi, was quite famous and powerful in the northern part of the Divine Realm.
When the Ye family's guards saw that outsiders had infiltrated the City Lord's Mansion, they naturally surrounded them.
At this moment, Ye Zhengqi was also guarding the rear courtyard's entrance, waiting for his wife to give birth.
When Ye Zhengqi heard the guards' shouts, his expression darkened, and he hurried over. However, when he sensed Luo Zheng's cultivation level, his expression changed slightly, and he asked in a low voice, "May I know who you are, and why are you here in the Ye family?"
Hearing Ye Zhengqi's question, Luo Zheng was thinking about how to answer, but Dongfang Yingqing beat him to it and said, "I'm here to find my husband!"
This answer confused Ye Zhengqi. "Who's your husband? Why are you here in the Ye family? "
Dongfang Yingqing replied bluntly, "It's that newborn baby. He's my husband …"
Dongfang Yingqing probably also realized that her words were too strange, and the more she spoke, the less confident she became.
Ye Zhengqi looked at Dongfang Yingqing as if he was looking at a lunatic. This woman had a beautiful face, an extraordinary temperament, and an extremely high cultivation level. She couldn't be crazy, right?

Chapter 2595
A Man Destined to Be a Legend
The people of the God Realm had very limited knowledge of reincarnation.
If it weren't for Luo Zheng, a Perfection True God, standing in front of him, Ye Zhengqi would have chased them away already.
However, his expression was extremely unpleasant as he said, "My Ye Family's Lin 'er has just been born, how could he be your husband? Isn't this the biggest joke in the world? "
Dongfang Yingqing wanted to say something but was stopped by Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng said, "Friend, have you heard of 'reincarnation'?"
Ye Zhengqi immediately shook his head. "How can those rumors of reincarnation be taken seriously?"
"Whether they can be taken seriously or not, we'll know when the baby grows up. Before that, I only have one request. Give the baby the name 'Tianming'." Luo Zheng tapped his spatial ring as he spoke. Following a flash of light, a longsword appeared in Luo Zheng's hand.
To Hua Tianming, the Sword of Tengshe was a sword that claimed his life. That was the fate that Luo Xiao had set for Hua Tianming. Now that Hua Tianming had reincarnated, it was naturally unsuitable for him to use this sword.
The sword in Luo Zheng's hand was also a top-grade grandmist treasure. Although it couldn't be compared to the treasures of faith possessed by Saints, it was still very rare among the rich and powerful families.
To the first-tier and second-tier families, this kind of treasured sword was an extremely rare existence.
"This sword is for Brother Tianming. Other than that, there's also these …" Luo Zheng took out dozens of medicinal pills as he spoke.
When he took out these medicinal pills, the Ye Family's guards were stunned. They might not recognize these medicinal pills, but the unique aura emitted by these medicinal pills was enough to know that these medicinal pills were extraordinary.
Even with Ye Zhengqi's experience, he panicked when he saw these things.
Although the Ye Family was only a first-tier family, these medicinal pills and that treasured sword were equivalent to half of the Ye Family's assets. Therefore, Ye Zhengqi was quite shocked at this moment. He stammered, "This, this … How can my Lin 'er … how can he …"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "Because he once saved my life. These things are only a part of it. In the future, there will be people sending him cultivation resources every year in hopes that he can step into the True God Realm before he turns ten."
Ye Zhengqi's mouth opened wide. He already understood that Luo Zheng wasn't joking.
"Also, your Ye Family belongs to the Leng Family, right?" Luo Zheng's Ye Zhengqi.
Ye Zhengqi's family's Leng Family's Ye Family.
"I will ask Leng Yao to deploy people to protect this Divine City," Luo Zheng continued.
Hearing Luo Zheng directly call the Leng Family's Saint Emperor by his name, Ye Zhengqi was even more terrified. The origins of this man and woman were even more mysterious. However, they didn't ask for anything from the Ye Family. They just wanted his son to be named "Tianming". This was simply a gift from the heavens …
Luo Zheng turned his head and looked at Dongfang Yingqing. "I've already arranged everything. Are you sure you don't want to return to the floating island and stay here?"
Dongfang Yingqing nodded without hesitation and said with a smile, "I'll stay in this Divine City. I want to watch him grow up."
This was Dongfang Yingqing's choice. Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't interfere. However, he still reminded her. Before he left, Luo Zheng said to Ye Zhengqi, "Take good care of Dongfang Yingqing and Brother Tianming. I can guarantee that Brother Tianming will become a Saint by the age of thirty and your Ye Family will become a Noble Family."
"Saint …"
"Noble Family …"
Even after Luo Zheng left, Ye Zhengqi was still in a bit of a daze.
Ye Zhengqi had managed the Ye Family for so long and brought it into the top tier. He himself was only a peak Upper True God and didn't have the qualifications to enter the Saint Mountain.
Ye Zhengqi didn't even dare to think about making his family a Noble Family.
Who exactly was this guy? How dare he make such a big promise?
Now that Luo Zheng had left, Ye Zhengqi could only ask Dongfang Yingqing. Because he didn't know her background, he still very politely cupped his hands and asked, "How should I address you?"
"I'm Dongfang Yingqing." Dongfang Yingqing smiled gently.
"Dongfang …" Ye Zhengqi was stunned. "Could it be the Dongfang Family from the Fusang Divine Tree?"
The news of the Dongfang Family's destruction hadn't spread yet. Ye Zhengqi only knew that the Dongfang Family was the number one Noble Family in the Divine Realm. Naturally, he would ask this question.
"Yes." Dongfang Yingqing nodded.
Ye Zhengqi was speechless again. His attitude became even more cautious. "Then who was that person just now? Can he really command the Leng Family's Holy Emperor? "
"His name is Luo Zheng. He's my husband's brother. I'm not sure if he can command the Leng Family's Holy Emperor, but he drove all the Noble Families out of the Sea of Time … Can I see Tianming?" Dongfang Yingqing clearly wasn't interested in these questions. She only wanted to see Hua Tianming.
Driving all the Noble Families out of the Sea of Time … Ye Zhengqi was once again shocked speechless when he saw this girl's sincere attitude.
Ye Zhengqi quickly wondered if he should ask about the situation in the Sea of Time to verify Dongfang Yingqing's words. However, half a month later, all his doubts were no longer important. Because Leng Yao personally came to the Ye Family to see "Ye Tianming."
The Leng Family's Holy Emperor was very sincere and promised to never collect shenwu coins from the Ye Family again. Not only that, but he also promised to take in the Ye Family's True Gods into the floating island every year.
The fate of this remote, first-tier family changed because of a baby.
As for Hua Tianming, he grew up in the Ye Family. In the next few decades, he would shine in the Divine Realm and create another legend.
… …
… …
In the center of the Sea of Time.
A huge island floated in the Sea of Time.
This floating island was tens of thousands of times larger than an ordinary floating island. This colossus was equivalent to thousands of Divine Cities.
Luo Zheng didn't remove the other Noble Families' floating islands. Instead, he moved them to the periphery of the Sea of Time. The Saints of the Noble Families no longer had the right to modify the Governing Slate.
This new floating island would become the center of the entire Divine Realm and attract all the geniuses in the Divine Realm. Of course, this included the newly born Li Clan members. These Li Clan members inherited the memories and talents of their previous lives. Their cultivation speed was so fast that it made one's hair stand on end. In the future, the God Citizens and the Li Clan members would form a healthy competition.
After dealing with these matters, Luo Zheng planned to bring Mu Ning, Han Liusu, and Chuyue to the Great Evolution Universe.
Mu Ning was very interested in the place where Luo Zheng was born. However, before they set off, she still faintly revealed a worried expression. "Luo Zheng, I don't know if I can get along with them."
Mu Ning had been with Luo Zheng for a period of time, so she knew about their existence.
"Don't worry. I believe you can get along well." Luo Zheng gently patted Mu Ning's head.

Chapter 2596
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Back to the Cloud Hall
Han Chuyue, this girl's words choked Luo Zheng until he could not say a word for a long time.
Han Liusu smiled sweetly. "At least he didn't say that to the first one …"
Luo Zheng widened his eyes and made a gesture of surrender. He waved his hand in the air, and a space passage leading to the Dayan Universe appeared.
Before entering the Saint Mountain, Luo Xiao had settled Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, and the others in the Dayan Universe. Even the Path-Chosen had returned.
These Path-Chosen had become upper-level True Gods in a short time. Their return to the Dayan Universe had brought great surprise to their respective forces and families.
Back then, Xingwei was only a lower-level True God, but he could dominate the universe. Upper-Level True Gods were almost unimaginable to those Celestial Venerables.

After entering the Dayan Universe, Luo Zheng waved his hand and arrived in the Rising Sky Realm.
In the Rising Sky Realm, the Demi-Immortal Clan was already waiting.
The old clan leader came personally to welcome Luo Zheng's return. None of the Celestial Venerables were absent.

The Celestial Venerables could freely go to the Divine Realm to pursue a higher level of power.
The Crown of Destiny was somewhat similar to the position of Saint. After being conferred the title of Celestial Venerables, their strength would far surpass the realm master, but at the same time, they would be limited.
In order to prepare for the Universal War, Luo Xiao could only force the Crown of Celestial Venerables on these Celestial Venerables. The passage to the Divine Realm was actually half closed. After all, at that time, they still needed the Jiang Family to go to the Divine Realm.
The only exception was Chen Huang Yijian. The Mandate he was conferred was originally different from the other Celestial Venerables, so he could sneak to the Divine Realm while the other Words, The Celestial Venerables, they could not.
Now that the threat in the Divine Realm no longer existed, Luo Xiao naturally did not need the Empyreans to risk their lives for him, allowing them to return the Crown of Destiny.
Upon hearing the news brought back by Luo Zheng, the Celestial Venerables in the universe were also extremely happy.
In a part of the universe, they were indeed the top existences.
But to the Celestial Lords, the pursuit of a higher level of power was their faith. This faith turned into blood that flowed through their bodies.
Without the Crown of Heaven's Will, they would fall back to being ordinary Sector Lords. Even if they went to the Divine Realm, they would still be mere Divine People. These drawbacks contained many dangers and troubles, but also endless challenges …
Therefore, after Luo Zheng made the announcement, nearly half of the Celestial Venerables wanted to remove their Crown of Destiny and head to the Divine Realm.
Since Star Tail had already left the Great Yan Universe, Luo Xiao was trying to find a way to restore his inner world. After Luo Zheng agreed to their requests one by one, he asked the old patriarch to record this matter and report it to Luo Xiao when the time came.
After staying in the Rising Sky Realm for three days, Luo Zheng made a trip to the Demon Night Clan with Xun before he set his sights on the lower dimensions.
This time, Luo Zheng brought even more people with him on his return to the Central Domain.

Chapter 2597
The Rivalry Between Acquaintances

When the Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors looked into Luo Zheng's eyes, they could feel an indescribable magical power radiating from Luo Zheng's eyes.
Although Luo Zheng didn't have any hostility or disdain, they subconsciously felt that they were as insignificant as dust and ants …
King Yan was also extremely excited. Compared to Luo Zheng, the conflict between him and the Red Maple was almost negligible.

King Yan naturally didn't know that even if he ascended to the Higher Domain, he wouldn't be able to find Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng had already left for the Divine Dominion.

King Yan and the other Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors knew that Luo Zheng would ask about this matter. Hence, they explained the conflict between the two continents in detail.
There was no right or wrong in this matter. It was all for one's own benefit.

Ning Yudie no longer had any desires in this world. However, the Cloud Hall was her homeland. There was no doubt that she had a deep relationship with the Cloud Hall. Hence, she was naturally protective of her people.
Her words stunned the four Battle Emperors and Battle Monarchs.

Hide

Bottom Whirlpool
The initial speed of a colossus like the Divine Realm must be extremely slow.
Just like a running elephant, it might turn its head slowly and sluggishly, but once it started sprinting, its speed would become faster and faster.
When Luo Zheng pushed the two bracelets forward, the entire God's Domain shook even more violently.

Back then, Li Luoshui had used the Black Flame Torrent to kill several Saints.
However, the Divine Realm had no records of the Black Flame Torrent's function and origin.
After all, even a Saint could enter a place like the Sea of Time to investigate, but who could go deep into the Black Flame Torrent?
"Rumble …"
As Luo Zheng pushed down the rings in his hands, the Divine Realm moved faster and faster. Luo Zheng also felt that the Black Flame Torrent was boiling more and more fiercely!
"This speed should be enough," Luo Zheng murmured.
Even if the You Xiong Clan knew about the death of Wei Xian and the others, it would still take a long time for them to send someone to investigate.
By that time, the Divine Realm should have already reached an extremely high speed and escaped from this place.
Since the Divine Realm had been activated, Luo Zheng wouldn't need to control it for a short period of time.
After breaking free from the rings, Luo Zheng could still clearly feel the boiling Black Flame Torrent through the newborn will.
"This Black Flame Torrent can't be born out of thin air. If Chi You wanted to drive such a huge Divine Realm, he must have placed something deep underground. Logically speaking, since I have inherited the newborn will Chi You, I should know everything in the Divine Realm. So why can't I find the source of this Black Flame Torrent?"
Once this curiosity arose, Luo Zheng couldn't suppress it.
He sat in the middle of the hall and used his nascent will to spread out his Divine Sense.
This spiritual sense crossed the sun and shot down from the sky. It drilled into the rock layer, then the gangue layer, the flint layer, and the tendon layer. Finally, it sank into the Black Fire Torrent.
If it was in the past, when one's consciousness entered the Black Flame Torrent, one's consciousness would be annihilated.
But after fusing with the nascent will, this Burning Fire Style could do nothing to Luo Zheng's spiritual consciousness.
Luo Zheng's Divine Sense constantly shuttled back and forth in the Black Flame Torrent. He quickly discovered that this green liquid was almost everywhere at the bottom of the Divine Domain. There was so much of it that it exceeded Luo Zheng's imagination.
The underground Black Flame Torrent was like a massive ocean. It was wrapped up by the upper layer of rock, and the surface area of this ocean was nearly as large as the Divine Realm!
In such a huge green sea, Luo Zheng wouldn't be too surprised even if some strange creatures appeared.
But soon, Luo Zheng realized that he was thinking too much.
Black fire was a forbidden area for living creatures after all. Even in the essence sun fire, fire spirits had been born and evolved into all kinds of strange creatures. However, Black fire was deathly silent.
"Gululu, gululu …"
As Luo Zheng's spiritual consciousness shuttled back and forth, he found that the Black Flame Torrent around him was boiling even more fiercely.
The originally viscous green liquid was like boiling water, boiling crazily.
"The commotion should be coming from the front!"
After confirming the direction, Luo Zheng's Divine Sense sped up a bit. Not long after, he saw an unbelievable scene.
In the center of the Black Flame Torrent, there was a huge green whirlpool!
The whirlpool was spinning at high speed, causing the Black Flame Torrent in the entire Divine Domain!
"Let's go in and take a look!"
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all. He injected his Divine Sense into the whirlpool. But the moment his Divine Sense entered, it disappeared!
His Divine Sense had lost connection with himself …
Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. His face was full of confusion.
It was very common for Divine Sense to be cut off. Even now, if Luo Zheng felt that other True Gods were investigating him, he could turn his soul power into a blade and cut off the opponent's Divine Sense by force.
To put it bluntly, Divine Sense was just an extension of the soul. It was just that the soul power contained in Divine Sense was relatively small.
Luo Zheng couldn't suppress the curiosity in his heart. He released his Divine Sense again.
This time, he had already confirmed the direction. Naturally, his speed was extremely fast. In the blink of an eye, he found the whirlpool. But the situation was exactly the same as before. The moment his Divine Sense entered, it was sucked away.
Luo Zheng gave up on his third attempt. He reckoned that the result would be exactly the same.
After pondering for a while, Luo Zheng suddenly came to a conclusion. This whirlpool should have something to do with him activating the Divine Domain.
Thinking of this, he once again put the seven rings on his body. As Luo Zheng pulled back the rings in his hands, the entire Divine Domain slowly came to a stop. Not long after, it floated in a state of absolute stillness in the chaos.
On the floating island, Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others naturally felt this movement. Their faces were full of puzzlement.
"Luo Zheng just activated the Divine Domain. Why did he stop it?"
"Did he discover something?"
While stopping the Divine Domain, Luo Zheng once again injected his Divine Sense into the ground.
Sure enough, just as he had deduced, the huge whirlpool in the Black Fire Torrent disappeared. The originally boiling Black Fire Torrent also gradually calmed down.
"It seems that this huge whirlpool should be the power source of the Divine Domain. But what exactly is in the whirlpool?"
Although Luo Zheng had proven his conjecture, he was even more curious about this whirlpool.
After Luo Zheng activated the Divine Domain again, he left the Sun Palace and returned to the floating island.
"Luo Zheng, why did you stop the Divine Domain just now?" Elder Jin asked his doubts. He was afraid that Luo Zheng had encountered some thorny problem.
Luo Zheng thought for a while and answered, "I discovered something strange at the bottom of the Divine Domain. However, my Divine Sense is unable to enter it. So, I plan to make a trip there myself."
"The bottom of the Divine Domain?" Chi Yi raised his eyebrows slightly.
They knew that the bottom of the Divine Domain was filled with Black Fire Torrents. However, even the experienced and knowledgeable Chi Yi and Elder Jin were unable to determine the source of such things.

Chapter 2601
Sucking in the Maelstrom
Luo Zheng told them what he had seen with his consciousness.
This piqued the interest of Chi Yi and the others.
Even in the Mother World, there were very few people who could build such a huge ship.
This Soaring Firmament Flying Ship was only fifteen meters long, and it would consume a lot of divine crystals to travel through the Primal Chaos.
And for a massive world like the Divine Dominion to travel through the Primal Chaos, the consumption of divine crystals would be astronomical. Chi Yi and Fu Er had discussed this problem when they were outside the Divine Dominion. They couldn't figure out what method the Great Tribal Chief used to drive this ship.
Elder Jin told Fu Er and Chi Yi to stay here and inspect the flying ship, but the two were more interested in exploring the bottom of the Primal Chaos, so they decided to accompany Luo Zheng.
The Great Maelstrom was located at the back of the Divine Realm, and it was to the west of the Sea of Time.
With Luo Zheng's current ability, he naturally couldn't drill down layer by layer like before. He waved his hand and constructed a spatial passage. At the same time, he said to Ming Wei and the others, "The other end of the spatial passage is the Black Fire Torrent. Before you go in, I'm afraid you'll have to take precautions."
Ming Wei and the others naturally understood Luo Zheng's meaning.
If they didn't take any precautions, their bodies would be burned to a crisp in the Black Fire Torrent.
Ming Wei gently spread her hands, and a colorful halo surrounded her.
Fu Er took out a yellow talisman paper and stuck it on his chest. This talisman paper constructed a golden halo.
After they made some preparations, Luo Zheng was the first to enter the spatial passage, followed by Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others.
The spatial passage compressed the distance to an extremely close distance. They only took four or five steps before a heatwave hit them in the face, and they fell into the sea of Black Fire Torrent.
"Zi zi zi …"
As soon as they fell in, the golden halo protecting Fu Er was continuously eroded by the Black Fire Torrent, and the yellow talisman paper on Fu Er's chest quickly burned to ashes.
If the golden halo collapsed and the Black Fire Torrent rushed in, Fu Er's body would probably be burned to charcoal in an instant.
At this time, Ming Wei gently waved her hand, and another colorful halo enveloped Fu Er.
When Chi Yi saw this scene, he chuckled, "Can you do it, Brother Fu Er …"
Fu Er, on the other hand, calmly took out another piece of yellow paper and muttered, "I underestimated the power of this thing. It can actually break my Yi Bing Protective Talisman so easily. I'll just have to use Armour Protective Talisman later."
The few Nirvana Realm cultivators needed to use their methods to resist the Black Fire Torrent, but Luo Zheng didn't need to. It was as if he was wandering in an ordinary lake, allowing the green liquid to burn him. There was nothing wrong with his body.
"The temperature of this Black Fire Torrent is extremely high. It's strange that it can actually boil." Ming Wei looked around.
The Black Fire Torrents that surrounded them bubbled like boiling water, and fist-sized bubbles kept flying past them.
"Where is the huge whirlpool that Luo Zheng mentioned?" Elder Jin asked.
"It's not far ahead. Follow me," Luo Zheng replied.
Then, Luo Zheng led the way in front, while the others followed behind Luo Zheng and swam in the Black Fire Torrent.
After swimming for dozens of miles in the Black Fire Torrent, everyone suddenly felt that the fluctuations of the Black Fire Torrent intensified. A huge force kept stirring the Black Fire Torrent, forming turbulent flows.
Although the turbulent flows were powerful, they were still unable to shake the group, and they continued to move forward in a straight line.
As they continued to move forward, the turbulent flows became more and more intense. The Nirvana Realm cultivators also needed to expend a certain amount of energy to resist. At this time, Luo Zheng suddenly stopped, then pointed to a place not far ahead and said, "It should be there!"
The Black Fire Torrent was a kind of green liquid that couldn't be seen through. How could they see anything standing here?
Under such circumstances, they could only use their divine sense to investigate. Elder Jin, Ming Wei, and the others extended their divine sense and extended it forward. However, their divine sense didn't extend far before Ming Wei suddenly frowned and said, "My divine sense has been cut off!"
"You've noticed it too." Luo Zheng had already expected this situation.
Elder Jin nodded. "This Great Whirlpool seems to be rejecting our divine sense."
However, Chi Yi frowned and said, "I feel that my divine sense hasn't been cut off, but directly sucked into the Great Whirlpool and lost contact with our souls."
This point of view was quickly supported by Fu Er. "I feel the same way."
Similarly, divine sense lost contact with one's body, but there were many kinds of situations. Some were cut off, some were annihilated, and some were sucked away by some power.
"If divine sense is sucked away, doesn't that mean that there's another layer of space in the Great Whirlpool?" Ming Wei quickly made another judgment.
"It's indeed possible." Fu Er touched his chin and said, "Or there's a magic treasure in the center of the Great Whirlpool that can absorb souls."
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I stopped the Divine Realm earlier to check if there was anything, but after the Divine Realm stopped, the Great Whirlpool also disappeared. This area is empty and there's nothing."
"In other words, there's another layer of space in the center of the Great Whirlpool," Chi Yi said.
"If we want to know what's in the Great Whirlpool, I'm afraid we'll have to go in," Luo Zheng said, staring ahead.
"But …" Ming Wei still wanted to discuss the possible dangers.
At this moment, Luo Zheng lightly pushed off with his feet, and his whole body moved forward like a swordfish.
A vast force immediately wrapped around Luo Zheng. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he could resist this force, but he didn't do so. Instead, he let the Great Whirlpool pull him in.
Ming Wei frowned. She didn't have time to reprimand Luo Zheng for his recklessness. Unexpectedly, she followed Luo Zheng into the whirlpool.
Elder Jin smiled bitterly.
They, the Chiyou Guard, had been through countless dangerous situations and had to be very careful every time. They naturally shouldn't act so recklessly. However, at this moment, they had no other choice but to follow.
As the Black Flame Current stirred, Luo Zheng's speed became faster and faster. Soon, he was pulled to the core of the Black Flame Current.
At this core, Luo Zheng felt a trace of spatial fluctuation!
"Chi Yi's judgment was right. There's really a different world!"
As soon as this thought flashed through his mind, his whole body was sucked into the core of the Great Whirlpool. Behind him, Ming Wei and Elder Jin were also sucked in.
As Luo Zheng was enveloped by the spatial fluctuation, everything around him spun.

Chapter 2602

Mysterious Meteorite Cauldron
Luo Zheng had seen this white crystal before. After Chi Yi brought back the corpse of a Xuanyuan Guard from the Divine Realm, he found one in the Xuanyuan Guard's spatial ring.
It was the "Divine Crystal" that they spoke of.
Elder Jin and the rest deduced that the Divine Crystals in Wei Xian's Xuanyuan Guard's possession were all used up in the Moonlight Slaying Formation.
According to them, the Lingxiao Flying Ship brought by the Xuanyuan Guards also required Divine Crystals to operate.
According to Luo Zheng's understanding, Divine Crystals were similar to the "True Origin Stones" in the universe to the Mother World.
True Origin Stones did not exist in the Divine Realm because the power of a True God came from the Power of Faith in the Inner World. True Gods could not directly use the "Shenwu Coins" that were used as a currency like True Origin Stones. After collecting them, they could only rely on the Saints of various wealthy families to change the rules …
Now that he looked over and saw the Divine Crystals that covered the sky, Luo Zheng's mouth was wide open.
He did not expect that there would be such a huge amount of Divine Crystals in this space!
Buzz, buzz …
Following the spatial fluctuation behind Luo Zheng, Ming Wei, Fu Er, Elder Jin, and Chi Yi were all sucked into this space.
When they saw this scene, their expressions were ten times more exaggerated than Luo Zheng's.
Luo Zheng was not from the Mother World. Although he had heard from Fu Er and the rest that Divine Crystals were the main currency in the Mother World and the only way for Nirvana Realm experts to replenish their energy, he did not have a clear understanding of the value of Divine Crystals.
Therefore, no matter how many Divine Crystals there were, the impact on Luo Zheng was rather limited.
However, it was different for Elder Jin, Ming Wei, and the rest!
This time, it was not Elder Jin, Ming Wei, and the rest who were fighting against Wei Xian and the rest.
After all, in terms of the number of Nirvana Realm experts, the Chiyou Guards and Nuwa Clan had the advantage. The biggest problem was that Elder Jin, Ming Wei, and the rest lacked resources. Among them, they lacked Divine Crystals the most! The Divine Crystals that they had brought with them had been used up not long after they were exiled in that prison.
If they were given sufficient divine crystals to fully nourish the Nirvana Token, perhaps there was no need for Luo Zheng to make a move. Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and Fu Er would be able to wipe out the group of people led by Wei Xian.
"These … are all Divine Crystals!"
"Am I dreaming? There are so many Divine Crystals!"
"These were all left behind by the Great Chief!"
Elder Jin, Chi Yi, and Fu Er's jaws dropped as they stared blankly at the scene before them.
Ming Wei's eyes were also filled with excitement, but overall, she was much calmer than them.
Ming Wei was indeed shocked by the Divine Crystals here. However, they did not belong to the Nuwa Clan. They belonged to the entire Li Clan and did not have much to do with her.
Even in the Mother World, it was rare to see so many divine crystals gathered together.
"Today, I was still thinking about what to use to drive the Cloudsoar Vessel. I never imagined that I would be able to find so many Divine Crystals here. Truly …" Chi Yi couldn't help but sigh with emotion.
Luo Zheng squatted down and took a divine crystal in his hand. He felt a unique coldness from his fingertips.
The appearance of this divine crystal was like a stone that had been cut. There seemed to be some kind of unique energy contained within the crystal.
However, in Luo Zheng's eyes, the energy within this divine crystal was not considered strong. It was just that the energy was exceptionally stable and gentle.
"Are these divine crystals really that important?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Hearing Luo Zheng's childish question, Ming Wei smiled gently. "Of course! The operation of everything in the Mother World is inseparable from the divine crystals. "
"But the energy within is not considered strong," Luo Zheng said directly.
"However, the energy contained within the divine crystals can nourish the Nirvana Token and allow it to erupt with even more power." Ming Wei shrugged.
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. "So it has the same effect as the Soul Elixir?"
During this period of time, Luo Zheng had gained a little understanding of the Nirvana Realm.
To enter the Nirvana Realm, one needed to accommodate the Nirvana Token. However, the power of the Nirvana Realm powerhouses no longer relied on the Inner World.
All the power of a True God relied on the Power of Faith to transform into Taoist Connotation through the Godhead. However, after the Nirvana Realm powerhouses evolved their Divine Path to the extreme, the power they pursued came from the Nirvana Token.
"It's not the same." Ming Wei shook her head. "The two major currencies in the Mother World are the Soul Elixir and the Divine Crystal. The Soul Elixir can be used in the Sea of Truth and the Nirvana Realm to strengthen the power of the soul. The Divine Crystal is used in the Mother World to nourish the Nirvana Token."
Such an explanation made Luo Zheng clear all of a sudden.
However, his thoughts turned slightly and he asked in confusion, "Since the Divine Crystal is used to nourish the Nirvana Token, why does the Lingxiao Flying Boat need the Divine Crystal to drive it? The Lingxiao Flying Boat does not belong to a Nirvana Realm powerhouse. Could it be that there is also a Nirvana Token in the flying boat? "
"Your speculation is correct." Ming Wei pursed her lips and smiled. "The Lingxiao Flying Boat was forged by some special Nirvana Realm powerhouses. Their ability is to project the Nirvana Token onto certain objects. Therefore, only the Divine Crystal is needed to drive the flying boat."
Projecting the Nirvana Token onto certain objects … Luo Zheng became more and more curious as he listened.
It was Luo Zheng's fault that he had not stepped into the Nirvana Realm yet. If he could achieve it in the future, it would be extremely easy to understand.
"I'm afraid this is the biggest legacy that the Great Chief has left for us …" Elder Jin was shocked for a long time before he finally calmed down and murmured to Luo Zheng.
Fu Er nodded and said, "I think I understand how the Black Fire Dregs outside came about."
"How did they come about?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Follow me." Fu Er took the lead and walked forward along the road paved with Divine Crystals.
Elder Jin and the others followed closely behind. They seemed to have made the same speculation and looked confident.
In the beginning, the Divine Crystals were all laid flat on the ground. However, as the group continued to move forward, the number of Divine Crystals increased and turned into small hills that piled up on both sides of the road.
After the group passed through the hills, the terrain began to become steep. The road formed by the Divine Crystals formed a downward slope that formed a huge whirlpool.
In the center of the whirlpool formed by the Divine Crystals sat a simple and old cauldron.
In this large cauldron, the green Black Fire Dregs rose and fell within it. The Divine Crystals around the whirlpool were continuously rolling into the cauldron!
The moment Luo Zheng looked at this large cauldron, he faintly felt a sense of illusion. This cauldron did not seem to exist in this world. However, Luo Zheng also felt an inexplicable mysterious aura from this cauldron that firmly attracted his eyes.
"This large cauldron …" Luo Zheng looked at it for a while and thought of what Ming Wei had said before. He asked, "Is it a Nirvana Token?"
"Correct!" A trace of admiration appeared in Ming Wei's eyes. At the same time, she said, "This is the Xuanyun Cauldron that came from the 18th Imperial Cliff."
Fu Er nodded and added, "The Black Fire Dregs were originally not called Black Fire Dregs, but Xuanyun Fire …"
"Then this Xuanyun Cauldron is the Great Chief's Nirvana Token?"

Chapter 2603
Green Wood Puppet Soldier
Elder Jin smiled faintly at Luo Zheng's deduction. "This Mysterious Fall Cauldron is indeed a good item for eighteen levels of heaven, but there's no way Great Tribal Chief would use it as a token."
Ming Wei also agreed. "For an existence like Chiyou, his Nirvana token would be at least at the thirtieth heaven and above. An eighteenth-heaven Mysterious Fall Cauldron is not qualified to be Chiyou's Nirvana token."
Although she didn't know what Chiyou's token was, she still had this bit of common sense.
"But if this Mysterious Fall Cauldron isn't Grandfather's token, why would it appear here?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Fu Er chuckled. "There's also a Nirvana Realm token in Wei Xian's Lingxiao Speedboat, but it's not Wei Xian's Nirvana Realm token. By the same logic, this Xuanyun Cauldron should be something that the Great Chieftain invited others to invest in this place as motivation for the Divine Realm."
"I see …" Luo Zheng stared at the Mysterious Fall Cauldron and murmured.
The energy of heaven and earth in the divine realm came from the chaos energy outside the realm converted by the four divine trees. However, this energy wasn't enough to drive the entire divine realm.
Only by relying on a token could one drive a massive ship like the divine realm.
"However, there are too many divine crystals here. I'm afraid most of the divine crystals accumulated by the Li Clan over countless years are here. It would be too wasteful to just use them to drive the divine realm. I wonder what Great Tribal Chief's intentions are?" Fu Er said as he looked at the divine crystals around them.
Chi Yi was currently groping around the Mysterious Fall Cauldron. His gaze circled to the back of the cauldron and he saw several black shadows appear on the shiny divine crystal hill.
Chi Yi felt that these black shadows were somewhat familiar. When he focused his gaze, his pupils suddenly shrank. He immediately said, "I don't know what Great Tribal Chief's intentions are, but we're definitely in trouble right now …"
As soon as he finished speaking, the black shadows rushed down the divine crystal hill.
Ming Wei's gaze swept over them and her willowy eyebrows raised slightly. "What's that?"
The black thing was only half the height of a human. Its body had a black metallic luster, and it had four legs. There was a unique rune on its torso, and it had a triangular head with fist-sized eyes embedded in its head.
"It's a Green Wood Puppet Soldier, and it's the most powerful kind." Elder Jin's expression turned ugly.
"How powerful?" Ming Wei asked.
"You and I will be able to take down a few Cyanwood Puppet Soldiers. However, we are definitely no match for so many of them," said Old Jin.
The Green Wood Puppet Soldier was a kind of war puppet unique to Jiuli.
The lowest level Green Wood Puppet Soldier was equivalent to the True God Realm. There were many of them, and one Li Clan member could control thousands or even tens of thousands of them.
However, the truly powerful ones among the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers were the ones in front of him. This type of Green Wood Puppet Soldier was also embedded with a Nirvana Realm token. Although they were puppets, their strength was comparable to a Nirvana Realm powerhouse.
When Ming Wei heard Elder Jin's words, her expression instantly turned ugly. There were two to three hundred Green Wood Puppets in front of her!
"Since there are such powerful Green Wood Puppets in the Domain of the Gods, why doesn't Grandfather use them to protect the Domain of the Gods?" Luo Zheng asked in confusion as he looked at the black shadows that were constantly surging over.
"It's very simple. These Green Wood Puppets can only move within a large formation. The Great Tribal Chief doesn't have the ability to cover the entire Domain of the Gods with this formation, so he can only use these Green Wood Puppets to protect this place. Now that we have intruded, we will naturally be attacked by the Green Wood Puppets," Fu Er analyzed to Luo Zheng.
"What's there to analyze! Let's quickly retreat. These Green Wood Puppets have come with ill intentions. If we are surrounded by them, I'm afraid we will all die here! "Elder Jin shouted.
Everyone quickly retreated along the path paved with Divine Crystals. However, these Green Wood Puppets were extremely fast. Their four feet made 'sha sha' sounds as they walked on the Divine Crystals. In just five to six breaths of time, they had already caught up to Chi Yi, who was bringing up the rear.
"Weng!"
The rune on the chest of the Green Wood Puppet Soldier at the front suddenly rotated, and a pure white cone of light suddenly shot out from it.
"Xiu!"
Chi Yi's complexion changed. He leapt up and twisted his body, his entire body struggling like a loach in water as he forcibly avoided this cone of light.
But as he dodged this cone of light, he was delayed for a moment, and several more Green Wood Puppets rushed up.
"Xiu xiu xiu …"
Pure white cones of light shot out from these Green Wood Puppets once more.
Seeing these cones of light about to pierce through Chi Yi, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flashed and blocked in front of Chi Yi.
"Puff puff puff …"
The power contained within these cones of light was so great that it exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations.
Before this, Luo Zheng had forcefully withstood the might of the Moon Slaughtering Sword. These cones of light were only slightly weaker than the Moon Slaughtering Sword!
"I'll block them. You guys hurry up and retreat!"
No matter how powerful these cones of light were, in the end, they couldn't harm Luo Zheng in the slightest. He could completely divert the strength of the cones of light away from his body.
Chi Yi and the others naturally understood Luo Zheng's ability. Without the slightest hesitation, they quickly retreated back the way they came.
These Green Wood Puppets weren't very intelligent. They didn't know how to split up to chase after Elder Jin and the others. Two to three hundred Green Wood Puppets went straight for Luo Zheng.
"Buzz buzz …"
All the Green Wood Puppets spread out in a fan shape. The runes on their chests rotated and released pure white cones of light, which shot towards Luo Zheng one after another.
If it were Ming Wei or Elder Jin, facing these cones of light, they would probably be torn to shreds in an instant.
Luo Zheng withstood the attacks of these cones of light and kept retreating. His complexion gradually turned grave.
The combined attack of so many Green Wood Puppets was roughly equivalent to the might of ten Moon Slaughtering Sword.
Although Luo Zheng could divert all these attacks to the surface of the Saint Mountain, even the indestructible Saint Mountain might not be able to withstand such a powerful force!
As this thought flashed through his mind, he discovered that the silver light on the surface of the Saint Mountain hidden underground was surging wildly, faintly showing signs of collapse.
The Saint Mountain was extremely important to the Realm of the Gods. If the Saint Mountain collapsed, it would probably cause unpredictable trouble.
In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng could only divert a portion of his power to some desolate and remote regions of the Realm of the Gods.
"Rumble …"
In the north of the Realm of the Gods, an incredible force suddenly descended from some desolate regions.
Under the frantic cutting of these forces, mountain ranges were directly cut in half. When the invisible force chopped on the plains, it immediately opened up gullies that were tens of thousands of feet deep. This force directly split open the rock layer, and black flames crazily gushed out from the bottom of the gullies.
Although there were few people in these large regions, there were still some divine citizens living in them. When these divine citizens saw this apocalyptic scene, their faces turned pale with fright.

A movable statue.

Ming Wei and the others' faces were as white as paper. Just as they were about to be smashed to pieces by the light cones, a light flashed in Luo Zheng's mind. With a light shake of his right hand, the Sword of Tengshe appeared in his hand.
The red light in the Green Wood Puppet Soldier's eyes dissipated, and the attack it had been preparing also stopped.
"Huu …"
Ming Wei let out a long sigh of relief. Her cheeks were still flushed red, and she had a different kind of beauty.
She had been scared half to death just now, but now that she saw Luo Zheng's actions, she naturally understood.
"It seems that the token used to enter this place is the Great Tribal Chief's Sword of Tengshe." Elder Jin had also been scared out of his wits, and it took him a while to recover.
Luo Zheng looked at the Sword of Tengshe in his hand, and smiled helplessly. "If I had known, I would have pulled out this sword from the beginning …"
After the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers stopped their attack, they returned the way they had come.
After the danger had been resolved, they were even more curious about the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers.
The Great Tribal Chief must have used the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers to protect something. Since the Sword of Tengshe could protect them from the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers' attacks, they were naturally happy to go and investigate.
However, they had learned from their mistakes. They were afraid that this damned place had some sort of trap, so they didn't dare to wander around. They could only slowly follow behind the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers.
After the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers passed by the small mountain of divine crystals, they discovered a small bridge behind the mountain of divine crystals.
Just below this small bridge, Luo Zheng discovered the large formation that Fu Er had mentioned.
There were two to three hundred formation eyes embedded in this large formation. The Green Wood Puppet Soldiers lined up neatly and entered this large formation. The light in their eyes was completely extinguished, and they no longer made any movements.
Fu Er's words made sense. Although the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers were powerful, their range of movement was limited. They could only be used to guard this space.
If the Realm of the Gods was guarded by this group of Green Wood Puppet Soldiers, those Nirvana Realm experts wouldn't be able to wreak havoc.
After observing for a while, Luo Zheng held the Sword of Tengshe and led Ming Wei and the others across the small bridge. They didn't walk far before reaching the end. At the end, Luo Zheng once again saw a lifelike statue of Chiyou.
"Plop!"
"Plop!"
"…"
Without saying a word, Elder Jin, Chi Yi, and Fu Er knelt on the ground and worshipped the Great Tribal Chief's statue.
This was a tradition of the Li Tribe. Even in the bone pagoda, they still followed this tradition. Moreover, the significance of this statue before them was even more different.
"This statue …"
Luo Zheng sized up this Chiyou statue.
The Chiyou statues he had seen before all had three heads and six arms, but this statue was somewhat unique. Each of his five arms held a weapon.
Axe, spear, halberd, saber, crossbow … Only the topmost right hand was empty.
Luo Zheng looked at the Sword of Tengshe in his hand. Without hesitation, he placed the Tengshe sword on the statue's right hand.
The moment the Sword of Tengshe left Luo Zheng's hand, the large formation under the small bridge once again bloomed with light. The Green Wood Puppet Soldiers had actually awakened. One by one, the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers swarmed up from both sides of the small bridge with red eyes, heading straight for Luo Zheng's group.
"Oh no!"
"These Green Wood Puppet Soldiers only recognize the Sword of Tengshe!"
Elder Jin and the others paled in fright.
Luo Zheng frowned. He wanted to pull the Sword of Tengshe out of the statue's hand.
But when he tried to pull, the Sword of Tengshe did not budge at all!
Luo Zheng grimaced. Circulating the Origin of Strength in his body, he once more tried to pull it out. Although he used all his strength, he still could not pull it out.
"This …"
He also did not expect such a thing to happen.
While everyone was at a loss, the Chiyou statue slightly moved. The statue raised the Sword of Tengshe.

Chapter 2605
Meeting
While Luo Zheng and the others were still recovering from their shock, Chiyou's statue opened its eyes, and a voice full of vicissitudes came out of its mouth.
"Who are you? Why do you have my sword? "
Elder Jin, Chi Yi, and the others were surprised and delighted when they heard this voice. This was Chiyou's voice!
"Great Tribal Chief!"
"We are the Chiyou Guard! Great Tribal Chief! "
Elder Jin and Chi Yi responded. Their eyes were full of excitement, and their faces were sincere and solemn.
In the mother world, they were not qualified to meet the Great Tribal Chief. Now that they could face Chiyou alone, they naturally felt extremely honored.
"Chiyou Guard?"
There was no joy or sorrow in Chiyou's voice, and no expression on his carved face. He looked around and asked, "Tengshe's sword was given to my daughter, Luo Shui, for safekeeping. Where is Luo Shui?"
"Reporting to the Great Tribal Chief! Her Highness is in the Divine Domain. This is … "Elder Jin thought for a moment, then introduced," This is your eldest grandson! "
Strictly speaking, Luo Zheng was Chiyou's grandson.
However, Elder Jin's thoughts turned very quickly. After all, Luo Xiao was a divine citizen without a bloodline inheritance.
Surnames were meaningless to existences like them. The only way to verify blood relations was through bloodline. Therefore, it was not wrong to regard Luo Zheng as the Great Tribal Chief's eldest grandson.
"Eldest grandson?"
The two-zhang-tall statue looked down at Luo Zheng. He stretched out one of his hands and placed it in front of Luo Zheng. "Young man, let me see your bloodline …"
Luo Zheng had been sizing up this Chiyou statue. He had roughly understood that this Chiyou statue only contained a remnant soul. However, this remnant soul still gave Luo Zheng great pressure.
"Luo Zheng! Quickly offer a drop of blood, "Elder Jin hurriedly ordered.
Luo Zheng nodded. He stretched out his finger, and a drop of golden blood shot out from his fingertip and landed on the stone hand of the Chiyou statue.
Chi You placed his finger that was stained with Luo Zheng's blood in front of his eyes and examined it. Indifferently, he said, "You are indeed the flesh and blood of Luo Shui. You stay … the others, please wait outside the small bridge."
Elder Jin and the others didn't dare to disobey. They took Ming Wei and quickly retreated to the other end of the bridge.
"Why is your bloodline no longer pure? I smelled the scent of 'Ji' in it. Tell me why. "Chi You's voice was still indifferent.
"Ji?" Luo Zheng was taken aback and asked, "You mean that Primal Chaos Ancient God?"
"You're really sharp. You found the problem with the bloodline just like that. My name is Ji." 9527's voice came from Luo Zheng's chest.
Just as 9527 was communicating with Luo Zheng, Chi You pointed at Luo Zheng's chest again. Under an invisible force, a light green soul was pulled out. It was 9527's soul.
"You're not Ji himself. You're just a spiritual puppet he created with his soul!" Chi You's voice became cold.
Golden threads flashed on his right hand, which was made of stone. These threads quickly turned into a mini cage, trapping 9527 within it.
9527 was very calm and replied, "I naturally can't represent my master."
"Speak. Why are you parasitizing my descendant!" Chi You's voice became stern again.
Although Chi You didn't show any concern for Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, as the son of Luo Shui, could bring Tengshe's sword into this area. The meaning was obvious. But after smelling the bloodline of Ji, Chi You became suspicious.
9527 said unhurriedly, "Very simple. Luo Zheng was chosen by me."
"What was chosen?" Chi You asked again.
"I hope he becomes the Man of Destiny," 9527 replied.
"He becomes the Man of Destiny?"
Chi You's emotionless voice also showed a trace of surprise. He didn't expect to get such an answer.
"Otherwise? I treasure my feather clothes so much. Why would I casually pass on my bloodline to a commoner? Do you think the bloodline of Jiuli is nobler than me? "9527 asked in response.
9527 didn't change his expression. After all, there was a chaos ancient god standing behind 9527.
"Why did you choose him …" Chi You stared at Luo Zheng and murmured.
"You created the world yourself and cultivated the seed. Why do you have to ask me? I chose him because he's the most suitable one! " 9527 said. "Also, Luo Zheng was chosen as the Man of Destiny. I will also help you. Why not cooperate?"
Chi You was silent. Of course, he understood 9527's meaning.
After a long while, Chi You said, "You're Luo Zheng?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied.
"I want to see your mother," Chi You said his request.
It wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng to invite Li Luoshui here. He said, "But I can't get out of this space."
Chi You stretched out his hand and pointed upwards. A spatial crack split open. Through this spatial crack, the surging green liquid outside could be seen.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate. With a thought, he used greater teleportation and went straight to the crack.
In the blink of an eye, a spatial tunnel extended from the depths of the ground to a palace on the floating island.
During this time, Li Luoshui and Luo Xiao had been traveling around. They only returned to the floating island occasionally. The husband and wife enjoyed the peace and leisure in the divine territory. The relationship between Li Luoshui and Aunt Hanjiu also eased a lot.
Luo Zheng's sudden appearance in the palace puzzled Li Luoshui and Luo Xiao. Li Luoshui asked, "Zheng 'er, what happened?"
"Someone wants to see you, Mother," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Zheng didn't have any feelings for his grandfather. But Li Luoshui and Chi You were different. She was his daughter.
"Who is it?" Li Luoshui asked suspiciously.
"Come with me." Luo Zheng pulled his mother's hand. His gaze fell on Luo Xiao. After thinking for a moment, he said, "Father, come with us."
He didn't know if Chi You would be happy to see this son-in-law.
The two of them suspiciously entered the spatial tunnel Luo Zheng had created and arrived in front of Chi You.
"Zheng 'er, who is it that wants to see me? Why are you being so mysterious …" Li Luoshui asked in confusion. When she saw Chi You's statue, she was slightly stunned. When she felt the aura coming from Chi You's statue, her body suddenly trembled. Her beautiful eyes were filled with tears, and she called out in a trembling voice, "Father?"
Chi You nodded slightly and said indifferently, "What happened in the divine territory? Why is your body gone?"

Chapter 2606
Translator: Atlas Studios Editor: Atlas Studios
A secret agreement.
Li Luoshui had always been very strong. Even in front of her husband and son, she was rarely sad.
However, in front of her father, all the grievances she had suffered all these years were revealed.
As she sobbed, she told Chi You about what happened in the divine territory, from the beginning of the journey to Luo Zheng inheriting the nascent will.
After listening to this, Chi You's indifferent and weathered voice revealed some emotions. "Back then, I thought I set the divine territory free and achieved it without anyone knowing. I didn't expect you to go through such hardships …"
"Father, this is not your fault." Li Luoshui shook her head and said, "Why don't you use a split soul to take charge of the divine territory? If so, our Li Tribe should be better off. "
This question had always troubled Li Luoshui. Even Luo Xiao was very puzzled when he mentioned this.
Splitting a soul was not difficult for Chi You. Moreover, Chi You could even cultivate a split soul to guard the divine territory. Normal Nirvana Realm experts would not dare to offend him.
Chi You smiled faintly and immediately answered, "Because they have the means to track all split souls. If I placed a split soul in the divine territory, it would naturally expose the location of the divine territory. I'm afraid the divine territory would not be able to hide for so long …"

"This is our last chance to see each other?" Li Luoshui was even more sad.
For a powerful existence like Chi You, as long as a remnant soul existed, there would be a chance for him to make a comeback. She did not expect her father to not even be a remnant soul.
"Maybe, maybe not." Chi You shook his head.
Hearing Chi You's ambiguous words, Li Luoshui's face was full of determination. "Then what should we do? As long as there is a glimmer of hope, Luoshui will not give up! "
Chi You was silent again …
That glimmer of hope was too slim. Even if Chi You revived himself, it would be difficult to reverse the fate of Jiuli.

Li Luoshui frowned and stared at Chi You. "But …"
