Obviously, he did not want Li Luoshui to lead her people back to the Mother World.
Li Luoshui naturally had such thoughts in her heart. She wanted the Divine Realm to return to the era of the Sacred Clan's prosperity and let the Jiuli Clan spread throughout the Divine Realm. She also hoped that the Divine Realm would give birth to more Nirvana Realm experts.
However, Chi You stood at the peak of the clouds and saw things more clearly. He knew very well that it was impossible to save Jiu Li with just a God Region.
Who knew that Li Luoshui would resolutely shake her head, "Father, I can promise you anything else, but to have the Li Clan cower in the divine realm forever, I can't do it!"
"Hehe …"
For the first time, Chi You let out a laugh.
Then, he said, "The night before the Divine Realm released that glimmer of hope, I handed it to you. The one to go to the Mother World cannot be you, nor can it be any other Li Clan member."
"Father, are you talking about Luo Zheng and Luo Yan?" Li Luoshui's eyes flashed as she asked.
Although Chi You had not seen Luo Yan, he naturally knew that Luo Yan was definitely the other six-stripe Blood Fixing Mysterious Seed.
He nodded and said, "Yes, I want them to go to the Mother World. One of them will go to Li Mountain, and the other will return …"
"I want to go with them!" Li Luoshui interrupted before Chi You could finish speaking.
Chi You shook his head, "You should stay in the Mother World."
"Why?" Li Luoshui asked again.
"There are some things that you can't participate in. Even I can't decide, so you must listen to me!" As he said this, he glanced at Luo Xiao indifferently.
It was Luo Xiao's first time seeing his father-in-law. As a saint, he was nervous under Chi You's gaze.
However, Chi You only said to Luo Xiao, "Please treat my daughter well. You all retreat to the small bridge. I still have some things to tell Luo Zheng."
"I will take good care of Luoshui," Luo Xiao bowed to Chi You.
Although Li Luoshui was reluctant, she was still taken away by Luo Xiao.
At this time, Chi You raised his right hand again. The golden cage that had imprisoned 9527 disappeared. He stared at 9527 and said, "If Ji really chooses Luo Zheng as the Man of the End, in some aspects, our goals are the same."
"That's what I thought from the beginning," 9527 replied unhurriedly.
"How much help can Ji give Luo Zheng?" Chi You asked.
9527 thought and then answered, "I have fallen into a deep sleep. Right now, I can only give Luo Zheng a portion of my bloodline and the ability to use it."
"Not enough," Chi You said.
"It's useless to bargain with me. I don't have the authority of Ji. Plus, Luo Zheng is only a true god now. Before he steps into the Nirvana Realm, everything is just hope," 9527 said.
Luo Zheng stood at the side and listened in a daze. He did not expect Chi You and 9527 to bargain in front of him. Chi You was obviously using him to ask for benefits from Ji.
At the end of the conversation, Chi You waved his hand lightly and started to communicate with 9527 alone. He did not let Luo Zheng hear him.
After about half an incense stick's worth of time, Chi You took a deep look at Luo Zheng and suddenly let out a sigh.
This sigh reverberated through the entire space. Then, the entire Chiyou statue, including the weapons on his six arms, instantly weathered and turned into a pile of gravel. With a loud rumble, it collapsed, leaving behind only the Soaring Serpent Sword.
As for Chi You's soul, it had completely disappeared.
Chi You's soul was not even a remnant soul. After it was activated, it could only stay for a short period of time. This was Chi You's last arrangement.
After the statue weathered, the Sword of Tengshe became an object without an owner again. The Green Wood Puppet Soldiers under the bridge were activated again.
Luo Zheng reacted quickly. He quickly picked up the Sword of Tengshe in his hand to prevent the Green Wood Puppet Soldiers from rioting.
"Buzz …"
9527 drew a green trajectory in the air and went straight into Luo Zheng's chest.
"What did my grandpa say to you?"
Luo Zheng asked. He was curious about the agreement Chi You and 9527 reached in the end.
Who knew that 9527 laughed and refused to reveal it to Luo Zheng. "Anyway, your fate is in my hands. Just listen to me in the future!"
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes and then left the room.

Roaring Fuxi.
After returning to the floating island, the Chiyou Guards had some changes in their state of mind.
Although Luo Zheng had heard about the Chiyou Tribe's defeat and Chiyou's death in the Sea of Truth, the Chiyou Guards still had high hopes for the omnipotent Great Chieftain.
Now that this news was confirmed by Chi You himself, it could be considered to be true.
Elder Jin, Fu Er, and the rest of the Chiyou Guards were eager to return to the mother world to find the remaining You Xiong Tribe.
Therefore, during this period of time, they had been thinking about the Lingxiao Flying Ship. But what was the point of going to the mother world now?
Although Li Luoshui saw her father for the last time and did not have any regrets, she could not help but feel depressed when she thought about Jiuli's fate.
Luo Xiao was not from Jiuli after all. No matter how he comforted her, it did not have much effect. In the end, he could only let Li Luoshui talk to Lingshu.
After Emperor Hanqing died, Lingshu was also released and stayed on the floating island during this period of time.
Although Lingshu and Luoshui were not weak, they had lost the support of their inner worlds and could not improve their cultivation.
The way to change this situation was simple. They had to retain their memories and reincarnate. The new body would naturally be able to open up their inner worlds. Only then would they have the chance to cross the Sea of Truth and step onto the Nirvana Realm again.
During this period of time, Lingshu had been preparing for reincarnation. She did not expect her sister to come and mention the news of her father's death. The two sisters could only comfort each other while feeling sad.
Among the group of Nirvana Realm experts, the only one who was in a good mood was Ming Wei.
Although she was sad about Chiyou's death, Chiyou's request to send Luo Yan to Mount Li was very much to Ming Wei's liking.
When they were in the outer realm, Ming Wei was very interested in Luo Yan. However, because Luo Yan could not step into the Chaos, she had not been able to meet him.
After entering the Divine Territory, Ming Wei immediately found Luo Yan and tested Luo Yan's bloodline.
A bloodline sealed with the six-vein Blood Fixing Mysterious Seed was definitely not ordinary.

At the bottom of the chaos.
When the prison was opened, all the Nirvana Realm powerhouses left.
Only a portion of the Aragami who were unwilling to leave remained in the area, continuing to maintain the territories of the major races.
At the top of one of the bone towers, a black-robed man looked up at the sky.
The grayish-brown chaotic clouds were floating like a solidified mist. From his perspective, the chaotic clouds were floating at an extremely slow speed. However, he knew that it was because the chaotic clouds were too huge that it created such a visual illusion. In fact, the chaotic clouds were not slow at all.
Similarly, there was also the light red gemstone floating in the sky. That was the Divine Realm that was speeding up and leaving. It seemed like the Divine Realm was moving slowly, but in fact, it was moving at a very fast speed. The Divine Realm was constantly moving away from the bottom of the chaos.
"They've finally left. Are they planning to return to the mother world?" The black-robed man mumbled.
He took off the hood of his black robe, revealing his long black hair. Under the messy hair was a masculine face.
After confirming that the Divine Realm had left, the black-robed man walked to the top of the bone tower and ran down the surface of the bone tower.
It would take a few months to fall from the top of the bone tower to the bottom. However, the black-robed man accelerated along the surface of the bone tower. He turned into a black wind and rushed to the ground at a speed that could not be captured by the naked eye.
In less than ten breaths, he had already rushed to the bottom of the bone tower.
Smashing down from above at such a high speed, even a speck of dust would contain a terrifying force, let alone a person. If he did not take any precautions, he would be able to create a huge crater at the bottom of the bone tower!
However, the moment he landed, his speed suddenly stopped. The tip of his foot lightly tapped the air. The space where his foot stepped on emitted a "kacha" sound as he gently landed on the ground.
Then, the gray-robed man flew across the Eternal Wasteland.
Along the way, he encountered many "sneaky people". However, when these sneaky people saw the gray-robed man, they were intimidated by his aura and ran away with faces full of fear.
Not long after, the gray-robed man stopped in a wasteland.
He gently lifted his foot and suddenly stomped down. The entire wasteland immediately trembled, emitting a rumbling sound.
"I'm leaving this place! Do you hear me! "The gray-robed man roared.
The Eternal Wasteland was silent. It was a flat land without even a mountain. Naturally, there was no response.
The gray-robed man was somewhat indignant as he stomped down once again.
Countless desolate bones scattered, forming a crater that was a few thousand feet wide. The gray-robed man continued shouting, "Do you hear me! Ji! Answer me! "
The entire world remained silent towards him. He felt like he was talking to himself like a madman.
"Your choice might not be correct. Before he has the potential, I might help him, but once he has the potential, I will find a chance to replace him!" The gray-robed man roared again.
This time, his voice was even louder.
The roar stirred up a blast, forming a hurricane that sent thousands of desolate bones flying in all directions.
The gray-robed man probably understood that "Ji" would not respond to him. In his anger, he stretched out his fist. His starry eyes were filled with hatred, "You shall lie here for the rest of your life!"
As his voice faded, an Eight Trigrams mark condensed on his fist. Without much hesitation, he smashed his fist downwards.
"Boom!"
This time, the ground of the entire "prison" began to tremble.
The Eternal Wasteland beneath the gray-robed man's feet began to expand. The wasteland that was tens of thousands of miles wide suddenly grew to a height of tens of thousands of feet. After the frenzied expansion, the entire Eternal Wasteland exploded with a "Poof". Everything in the wasteland, including the nearly indestructible desolate bones, were reduced to dust!
This punch had not only destroyed the Eternal Wasteland, it had also smashed open a crater that was tens of thousands of feet deep!
The gigantic head that had been buried at the bottom of the Eternal Wasteland finally revealed its true appearance!
The gigantic beast's skull had lost all its skin and flesh, but it still emitted a vast aura. Its empty eye sockets seemed to be silently telling something.
The gray-robed man stared at the gigantic beast, but he did not dare to step on the skull. Instead, he chose to leave.

Chapter 2608
Preparations Before Departure
In a palace on the floating island, a mellow fragrance of meat wafted through the air.
True Gods did not need food to fill their stomachs, but all kinds of delicacies had always been very popular in the divine realm.
The ingredients on the floating island were collected from all over the divine realm and were extremely expensive. Even the disciples of the floating island found it difficult to afford them, let alone the citizens of the gods.

Luo Yan had been separated from Luo Zheng since she was young. She had spent very little time with Luo Xiao and had not had the chance to meet her mother. Now that her family was reunited, she naturally cherished the time before her.

Xun, Su Lingyun, Mu Ning and the others also had the same idea.
Han Liusu, who was sitting a little further away, smiled gently. "Besides, we also want to see what the mother world looks like!"
Xi Youqin and the others, who were born in the universe, had no interest in the mother world, but Mu Ning and Han Liusu had stood at the top of the divine realm since birth. Their horizons were much wider than theirs. They were in awe of the Nirvana Realm and were also curious about the mother world.

Only when Luo Zheng stepped into the Nirvana Realm, Evil Deities had the qualifications to reach the Perfected True God. This was the limitation of the cultivation method of the Great World. Even Luo Zheng could not violate it.

Black Round Hole

Luo Zheng could not change the flow of time in his entire body, but it was not difficult to synchronize the flow of time on this floating island with the outside world.

Ming Wei shook her head and said, "The Void Hole itself isn't dangerous. The Sky-splitting Hou is a Ferocious Beast that is favored by the Law of Space. It doesn't rely on techniques like greater teleportation to travel through the Chaos."
"It doesn't rely on greater teleportation?" Luo Zheng was surprised.
Generally speaking, greater teleportation was still entering a spatial tunnel. However, the deeper one's understanding of space, the more stable the spatial tunnel would be.
For example, Ming Wei could create a spatial tunnel that was tens of millions of miles long. She only needed to take one step to complete the tunnel. Saints could also create spatial tunnels that were tens of millions of miles long. However, they needed to travel through the spatial tunnel for a period of time before reaching the end of the tunnel.
"In essence, greater teleportation is an ability to fold space. A Ferocious Beast like the Sky-splitting Hou can directly open a door and connect to any place in the Chaos." Ming Wei stared at the black hole and said, "The spatial door opened by the Sky-splitting Hou is called the Void Hole."
"Then if we pass through this Void, we will go to another place in the Chaos?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Yes, so the Void Hole itself is not dangerous. However, I don't know what we will encounter after we pass through the Void Hole …" Ming Wei said.
Hearing Ming Wei's explanation, Luo Zheng wasn't as nervous as before. He smiled and said, "Since the Void Cave is just a door to travel through the Chaos, there shouldn't be any danger. Maybe this door can help us enter the Mother World in advance?"
Although Luo Zheng said so, Ming Wei, Chi Yi, and the other Nuwa Clan people didn't look relaxed at all.
Chi Yi added, "Although the chaos is vast and empty, it is also filled with all sorts of unknowns and danger zones. If the other end of this void hole is connected to the Ultimate graveyard or the Land of Extinction … we will all die."
The chaos appeared to be empty because the chaos itself was too huge. Under the huge scale, it looked empty.
In fact, there were many unknown existences in the chaos, such as the Ultimate graveyard and the Land of Extinction that Chi Yi mentioned. These two danger zones were famous in the Mother World, but so far, no one knew what the inside of these two danger zones were like. This was because none of the creatures that were sucked into them had ever come out alive.
Seeing how serious Chi Yi was, Luo Yan couldn't help but grab Luo Zheng's arm. She looked a little nervous.
"Yan 'er, don't be afraid. We can't be so unlucky as to be brought to these damned places," Luo Zheng comforted her with a smile.
Seeing how nervous Luo Yan was, Ming Wei also comforted her, "Don't listen to Chi Yi's nonsense. Our luck isn't that bad …"
"Yes!" Luo Yan's eyes trembled slightly. She smiled and said, "With big brother beside me, Yan 'er naturally isn't afraid!"
Just as everyone was talking, the Lingxiao Airship trembled more and more violently.
Chi Yi simply stopped controlling the astrolabe and allowed the Lingxiao Airship to be pulled by the power of the "Void". Under the traction, the Lingxiao Airship flew along the messy blue lines, swaying, and then rushed into the dark void.
The moment the Lingxiao Airship entered the Void, everyone was immersed in darkness.
The sides of the Lingxiao Airship were originally inlaid with a circle of "Fiery Fluorite" used for illumination, but now the light of the Fiery Fluorite had strangely disappeared.
In the depths of this darkness, there was a whistling sound.
This whistling sound wasn't the sound of the wind, but more like the sound of some kind of behemoth passing through space.
"Hoo … Hoo … Hoo … Hoo!"
The sound sounded extremely far away, but from time to time, it suddenly rang in their ears.
Everyone held their breath and didn't dare to say a word.
Although Ming Wei explained the origin of the Void, she only understood the knowledge from ancient books. She hadn't personally entered the Void. Who knew if these records were true?
That strange sound always made people think of some bad things.
On the surface, Luo Yan said she wasn't afraid, but her hands still tightly clutched Luo Zheng. She curled up at Luo Zheng's side, her body slightly trembling.
In the past, when she was in Buzhou Spirit Mountain, she had always been under Yu Taibai's protection to gain experience. The only time she took a risk was when she went to the bottom of Ruo Mudi. However, at that time, Luo Yan's strength held an enormous advantage. Coupled with Luo Zheng's protection, she was fearless.
After reuniting with her parents and brother, Luo Yan had always been immersed in joy. She hoped that this kind of life could continue.
But the heavens didn't fulfill her wishes. Now that she was forced to embark on this journey, an unknown fear pressed down on her heart.
Sensing Luo Yan's emotions, Luo Zheng felt a trace of apology.
Although Mother gave Luo Yan the right to choose, Luo Yan was so sensible. How could she refuse her parents?
Perhaps it was a mistake to send her to Mount Li …
"Don't be afraid. We should be able to pass through in a while," Luo Zheng whispered.
Luo Zheng felt pity for her and could only gently stroke her silky hair. He thought of placing Luo Yan in Ming Wei's inner world after leaving the Void Cave. If she was in Ming Wei's inner world, she wouldn't have to be so frightened.
After traversing through the darkness for half an incense's time, Luo Zheng became more and more confused.
This Void Cave could fold the space on both sides. In essence, it should be a method of using the Law of Space. However, in this dark space, he couldn't even sense a trace of the Power of Space. How did the Sky-Splitting Roar do it?
While he was thinking, Luo Zheng felt a trace of light at the corner of his eyes. He focused his gaze towards the source of the light. His pupils suddenly shrank, and he reached out to cover Luo Yan's eyes.
Chi Yi, Ming Wei, and the Nirvana Realm experts of the Nuwa Clan all sucked in a breath of cold air. They were all dumbstruck.
An incomparably huge head of a ferocious beast was facing the Lingxiao Flying Ship, and the light source was the eyes of this ferocious beast!

Chapter 2614
Objective
Due to the vastness of the chaos, there were many types of unknown beasts hidden within.
Some even asserted that there were large groups of races in the chaos that had yet to be discovered.
The ten great beasts of the chaos were the most famous in the motherworld. Not only did they possess absolute strength, but they had also descended upon the motherworld several times and caused tremendous destruction.
The Skysplitter Hou was tens of millions of kilometers long. It had no scales or claws and was shaped like a snake. It had a pair of thin wings on both sides of its head, allowing it to travel through space without a trace.
Several years ago, the Skysplitter Hou had descended upon the motherworld and wreaked havoc in Coldlight Province, almost razing the entire province to the ground. Later on, it fought with Zhurong. It was said that although Zhurong had the absolute upper hand, he was still helpless against the Skysplitter Hou. In the end, the Skysplitter Hou easily passed through space and hid in the chaos.
When Ming Wei was still a child, she had seen this record in an ancient text.
When she was young, she had read all sorts of descriptions of the Skysplitter Hou, and her heart had been filled with fear.
Back then, Ming Wei had never imagined that she would directly face the Skysplitter Hou several years later!
Chi Yi had previously joked that he was afraid of entering the Void Hole and falling into the Sinkhole graveyard. At that time, Ming Wei had had an ominous premonition.
The Sinkhole left behind by the Skysplitter Hou hadn't existed for long. If they saw the Sinkhole, it meant that the Skysplitter Hou had just left. If they encountered the Skysplitter Hou in the Sinkhole, that would be the worst case scenario. It would be even worse than falling into one of those danger zones!
However, Ming Wei didn't speak of her worries. Speaking of them would only increase her worries and wouldn't solve the problem. She could only pray that such a thing wouldn't happen.
But sometimes, the more you worried about something, the more it would happen. The moment she saw the Skysplitter Hou, her heart stopped beating.
"Huu … Wuu …"
When the Skysplitter Hou's tens of millions of kilometers long body gently twisted, it emitted a whistling sound.
That pair of eyes were like two huge stars. Under its gaze, the space suddenly seemed to have completely stopped. The aura contained within it was extremely terrifying.
In its eyes, the Cloudsoar Airship was as insignificant as a speck of dust.
Luo Zheng's body stiffened, and her throat went dry.
Such a colossus could even swallow the motherworld in one gulp, let alone a tiny Cloudsoar Airship.
Even though Luo Yan's eyes were closed, she could still feel the aura of the Sky-Ripping Roar.
An existence like the Sky-Rending Hou could destroy a True God's soul just by relying on its aura.
Luo Zheng's Sun Soul had experienced the baptism of the Sea of True Intent and was still able to withstand it. Although Luo Yan also possessed a Sun Soul, she ultimately had yet to step into the Sea of True Intent. The pressure that her Sun Soul bore was naturally much greater than Luo Zheng's!
After sensing Luo Yan's pain, Luo Zheng reached out his hand and flipped it over, taking out a pitch-black pill.
For this trip to the mother world, Luo Zheng had made ample preparations. This pill was given to him by Elder Jin and could replenish his soul power.
Actually, Luo Zheng had an even better elixir on him, which was the Soul Elixir obtained from the Sea of True Intent. However, according to the rules, once the Soul Elixir was brought into the Sea of True Intent, it could not be taken out.
He opened Luo Yan's lips and stuffed this elixir into Luo Yan's mouth.
Luo Yan's soul power was quickly replenished, and her Sun Soul, which was originally trembling, finally calmed down. However, she still bit her lips as if she was maintaining her strength in front of her brother.
Fortunately, the Sky-Ripping Roar only stared at the High Firmament Flying Ship with its star-sized eyes and had no intention of pouncing over.
As for Ming Wei, Chi Yi, and the other Nirvana Realm experts, they did not dare to act rashly and confronted the Sky-Ripping Roar.
After a while, Chi Yi finally could not hold it in any longer. He lowered his voice and said, "Ming Wei, what should we do?"
Ming Wei shook her head lightly. "Such a ferocious beast is definitely not something that we can fight against …"
"But it keeps staring at us like this. Could it be that it intends to kill us with its gaze?" Chi Yi said with a bitter expression.
This experience was not easy to endure, just like the moment before a criminal's execution was the most torturous. Chi Yi wished that the Sky-Ripping Roar could quickly swallow them in one gulp!
After a stalemate of about 30 minutes, the Sky-Ripping Roar finally began to move.
That huge "snake head" slowly moved towards the High Firmament Flying Ship. As the distance continued to shrink, everyone could clearly see the wrinkles on the Sky-Ripping Roar's skin. Just one wrinkle was like a bottomless canyon, which showed how huge its body was.
Even if Ming Wei, Luo Zheng, and the others transformed into huge Rage Gods, they were still insignificant existences in front of it.
As the Sky-Ripping Roar continued to move closer, everyone's hearts became more and more anxious.
When the Sky-Ripping Roar and the High Firmament Flying Ship were still a few hundred miles away, they heard a whistling wind.
"Huff … Huff …"
The Sky-Ripping Roar actually began to inhale. It seemed like it was sniffing for something?
Under the strong wind, the High Firmament Flying Ship began to shake again. Seeing this, Chi Yi quickly controlled the jade plate to stabilize the High Firmament Flying Ship.
From the looks of it, the Sky-Ripping Roar did not intend to swallow the High Firmament Flying Ship. It would be a waste if they were sucked in by it.
"Huff …"
After the Sky-Ripping Roar sniffed back and forth a few times, the membrane on both sides of its head suddenly spread out. At the same time, it let out a shrill cry.
"Shhh —"
When the long cry was heard, Luo Zheng and the others could not help but cover their ears.
Under the baptism of its cry, although it was painful, everyone was even more confused.
"What does the Sky-Ripping Roar mean?" Chi Yi said helplessly.
"Ferocious beasts like the Sky-Ripping Roar have extraordinary spiritual intelligence. If it wants to know something, it should be able to communicate with us directly!" A woman from the Nuwa Clan said with a frown.
Ming Wei looked helpless. "Maybe it disdains to communicate with us …"
Feeling helpless, Ming Wei was also quite confused.
Looking at the situation, the Sky-Ripping Roar did not intend to swallow the High Firmament Flying Ship. It seemed to have sensed something on the flying ship.
But what was on the High Firmament Flying Ship?
It was nothing but Luo Zheng, Luo Yan, and them, who were in the Nirvana Realm.
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt a slight warmth in his chest. 9527's sigh sounded in his mind. "Sigh … Luo Zheng, leave the High Firmament Flying Ship. Let me talk to this guy."
The Sky-Ripping Roar's senses were extremely sharp. Even if 9527's soul power was so weak, it was still detected by it.
9527 did not want to reveal its true God in front of Ming Wei and the others. It could only let Luo Zheng leave the flying ship and meet the Sky-Ripping Roar.
"This guy is coming for you?" Luo Zheng's expression turned serious as he asked 9527.
"Yes. When you entered the Void Hole, I expected this situation. However, the High Firmament Flying Ship could not escape the Void Hole's gravitational force. I could only let it be." 9527's tone was somewhat helpless.

Chapter 2615
Translator: Nyoi-Bo Studio Editor: Nyoi-Bo Studio
Shortcut
Ming Wei and the others were still trembling with fear.
Who knew what the Sky-Rending Centipede was planning? It was possible for such an existence to swallow the flying ship in one gulp.
Just when everyone was at a loss, Luo Zheng suddenly said, "I'll go talk to it."
When these Nirvana Realm experts heard Luo Zheng's words, they were all shocked.
"Luo Zheng, are you crazy?" Ming Wei suppressed the fear in her heart and said softly.
"Brother …" Luo Yan also called out worriedly.
Luo Zheng said calmly, "It's coming for me."
"Coming for you? How is that possible? "Chi Yi didn't believe Luo Zheng at all.
The Sky-Rending Centipede came and went without a trace. How could it have any interaction with Luo Zheng in the God Domain?
Luo Zheng couldn't explain anything to Chi Yi. He patted Luo Yan's head lightly and said, "Stay here. Trust me, it'll be fine."
After saying that, Luo Zheng jumped lightly. The tip of his foot tapped on the side of the Lingxiao Flying Ship, and his whole body leaped up in this pitch-black space.
Ming Wei and the others didn't stop him because it was meaningless. Maybe what Luo Zheng said was true?
Visually, the Sky-Rending Centipede and the Lingxiao Flying Ship were very close. That was because the Sky-Rending Centipede was too big, giving people the feeling that it was right in front of them. In fact, the Sky-Rending Centipede and the Lingxiao Flying Ship were three to four hundred miles apart.
However, this distance was only a few breaths' time for Luo Zheng. Soon, he closed the distance between him and the Sky-Rending Centipede to a few hundred feet.
At such a close distance, the pressure that the Sky-Rending Centipede gave Luo Zheng disappeared.
Luo Zheng stood at the top of the God Domain and couldn't see the entire God Domain. The current situation was the same. He could only see the two eyes of the Sky-Rending Centipede hanging above his head like the sun. The wrinkles on the surface of the Sky-Rending Centipede's skin were like mountains. He couldn't see the Sky-Rending Centipede's full appearance at all.
Just as Luo Zheng stood still, the Sky-Rending Centipede opened its big mouth.
No one knew how wide this mouth was, and even with Luo Zheng's great eyesight, he could barely see the edge of this mouth. On both sides of this mouth were rows of fine barbs, each of which was fifty to sixty thousand miles long.
"Shh — shh —"
The Sky-Rending Crocodile let out that strange sound again. The syllables of the sound changed and were filled with rhythm. This should be the unique language of the Sky-Rending Crocodile.
This was because Luo Zheng was right next to the Sky-Rending Centipede's mouth. When it spoke, Luo Zheng smelled a unique fragrance.
Generally speaking, this kind of ferocious beast was highly poisonous. When it opened its mouth, it would definitely emit a foul stench. This smell made Luo Zheng very surprised.
In the distance, Ming Wei, Chi Yi, and Luo Yan on the Soaring Firmament Airship also had nervous expressions.
As long as the Sky-Rending Hou opened its mouth, no one would be able to save Luo Zheng!
"Is it talking to you? How do you communicate? "Luo Zheng asked.
9527 thought for a moment before saying, "It seems that I need to borrow your body. I'm too small right now."
Strictly speaking, 9527 was not a sub-soul of "Ji". Although its soul power came from "Ji", it was incomparably weak. It was indeed difficult to use its consciousness to communicate with such a colossus.
After Luo Zheng agreed, 9527 escaped into Luo Zheng's mind.
As Luo Zheng closed his eyes slightly, when he opened them again, a green light flashed between his eyes. Luo Zheng also opened his mouth. At the same time, his inner world began to brew the Spiritual Wind Divine Dao.
"Shh —"
Luo Zheng opened his mouth and let out the same sound as the Sky-Rending Hou.
With the help of the Spiritual Wind Divine Dao, Luo Zheng's voice was more than a hundred times louder.
Although it couldn't be compared to the Sky-Rending Hou's voice, it could still travel extremely far in this silent darkness.
Chi Yi's eyes widened and his mouth opened wide. "This guy can really communicate with the Sky-Rending Hou? He's not talking nonsense, is he? "
Ming Wei was also surprised as she stared at Luo Zheng's back hundreds of miles away. "That shouldn't be the case. Luo Zheng wouldn't joke around with his life. If his nonsense angered the Sky-Rending Centipede, he knows the consequences."
"But how can he master the language of the ferocious beasts?" Chi Yi curled his lips.
Every race in the chaos had their own language. The most similar language was the human race's language. Those intelligent ferocious beasts had basically mastered it. However, it was very difficult for humans to master the languages of other races. They could only do so after special training.
Luo Zheng could speak now, so Chi Yi and Ming Wei naturally couldn't understand.
"Shh —" The Sky-Rending Hou responded again.
Luo Zheng also made a "shh" sound. The two's sounds almost overlapped, but it didn't seem to affect their communication.
Luo Zheng quietly listened to the communication between 9527 and the Sky-Rending Hou. Naturally, he couldn't understand what they were talking about. However, this language was very special. There was a strong emotion attached to the hissing sounds. Luo Zheng could clearly feel this emotion.
9527's hissing sounds were very calm. It seemed to be explaining something to the Sky-Rending Hou. However, the Sky-Rending Hou's hissing sounds were more and more anxious. The rhythm of the hissing sounds was full of confusion and grievance.
The whole process lasted for an hour.
Luo Zheng only felt that his ears were numb. The Sky-Rending Hou suddenly let out a sharp cry. Its huge body gently twisted and finally turned its huge head around.
In this dark space, this huge creature twisted a few times. Finally, it turned its head back and looked deeply at Luo Zheng. Then, it disappeared into the distance.
Seeing the Sky-Rending Hou leave, Ming Wei, Chi Yi, and the others finally breathed a sigh of relief. However, they were also immersed in confusion and shock.
"Luo Zheng actually convinced the Sky-Rending Hou to leave!" Chi Yi said with a grimace.
Ming Wei pursed her lips. Although from the first time she saw this little guy, she felt that Luo Zheng was an extraordinary person. However, to be able to "convince" the Sky-Rending Hou to leave after seeing it for the first time was still beyond Ming Wei's imagination.
After all, in Ming Wei's eyes, encountering a ferocious beast like the Sky-Rending Hou was simply an unexpected disaster. If it were any other ferocious beast, Ming Wei might have fought back. However, against an existence like the Sky-Rending Hou, she didn't even have the courage to fight back.
After 9527 returned to the bone piece in Luo Zheng's chest, Luo Zheng asked, "What did you say to it?"
"Tell it to go away and open a Void Hole to the Mother World," 9527 said lightly.
"…"
Ming Wei had said that the Sky-Rending Hou's ability could connect any space in the chaos. This ability was unique and was the Sky-Rending Hou's most powerful talent.
However, Luo Zheng didn't expect 9527 to be able to command the Sky-Rending Hou at will.
Originally, it would take several years to reach the Mother World by relying on the Lingxiao Flying Boat. If they passed through this Void Hole, wouldn't they be able to reach the Mother World very quickly?
"After talking for so long, it can't have only communicated with one sentence, right?" Luo Zheng asked again.

Chapter 2616

Chaotic Ring
Luo Zheng was curious about the relationship between "Ji" and the Sky-Splitting Roar, but he couldn't do anything if 9527 didn't want to talk about it.
He returned to the Lingxiao Airship. Chi Yi, Ming Wei, and the ladies of the Nuwa Clan all "stared" at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at a monster.
After the Sky-Splitting Roar left, this space was in absolute darkness. Everyone used their divine sense to scan their surroundings.
Luo Yan was smiling as if it was natural for her brother to do all this …
"Let's go. The Sky-Splitting Centipede promised to open a Void Hole to the Mother World. After passing through the Void Hole, we should be very close to the Mother World." Luo Zheng had just finished speaking when he added, "Don't ask me. I don't understand what the Sky-Splitting Centipede said."
He had communicated with the Sky-Splitting Roar for a long time, but now he said he didn't understand what the Sky-Splitting Roar said. Even a child wouldn't be fooled.
But seeing that Luo Zheng didn't seem to be lying, no one questioned Luo Zheng.
Ming Wei just smiled and said, "I didn't expect to take such a shortcut this time. With the help of the Sky-Splitting Roar, we can pass through the chaos …"
Now, not only did they turn the misfortune into a blessing, but they also saved a lot of time. Everyone could only sigh at the heaven-defying luck that Luo Zheng brought them.
Chi Yi started the Lingxiao Airship again and moved forward silently in this darkness.
Not long after, everyone saw a small white dot.
That white dot was like a hole in the black curtain. Although it was small, it was very eye-catching in this darkness. Chi Yi noticed it the moment it appeared.
"It's the other side of the void hole. The two void holes correspond to each other. The entrance is pure black, and the exit is pure white. Let's go!" Ming Wei also became excited after seeing the white dot.
Chi Yi nodded and controlled the Lingxiao Airship to go straight to the white dot.
As the Lingxiao Airship kept moving forward, the area of the white dot became bigger and bigger. When they were tens of thousands of miles away, the white dot had become a huge white hole.

The Soaring Firmament Airship trembled again. The white hole also emitted an irresistible gravitational force, dragging the Soaring Firmament Airship over.

In the white light, everyone could clearly feel that the Ling Xiao Airship was accelerating. Under the violent vibration, the entire Ling Xiao Airship was directly ejected by the white hole.
After dozens of breaths, Luo Zheng finally opened his eyes...
The giant white hole had already disappeared, and the grayish-brown chaotic cloud appeared in front of him once again.
"This is not the Mother World, right?" Luo Zheng stood at the front of the boat and looked around. He only saw tens of huge Chaos clouds stacked into a ring, but outside the clouds, there was still nothing.
Ming Wei stared at the huge chaotic cloud and smiled. "No, this is the Mother World!"
Chi Yi also nodded. "If a Vicious Beast like the Sky-Rending Hou casually approached the Mother World, it would definitely cause a disaster. It did send us to our destination, but there is still some distance from here."
Seeing that Luo Zheng and Luo Yan were still puzzled, Ming Wei pointed at the chaotic cloud and said at the same time, "The Mother World is above those chaotic clouds! It's just that the chaotic clouds are covering the Mother World. You can see it if you go through. "

A Mother World actually needed tens of chaotic clouds to cover it. This Mother World's body was even bigger than Luo Zheng had imagined.
"It should take another three to four months to get close," Chi Yi estimated.
"We're already moving at an extremely fast speed. We only need less than half a year to go from the edge of the chaos to the Mother World … Those guys, I'm afraid they've only just started. They'll need at least twenty to thirty thousand years to get here." Ming Wei pursed her lips and smiled. She was indeed very happy to be able to take this shortcut to return to the Mother World.
The "them" she was talking about naturally referred to the Nirvana Realm experts in the bone pagoda at the bottom of the chaos. Without the help of Divine Crystals or the Lingxiao Flying Ship, they could only rely on their physical bodies to fly through the chaos. Not only would it consume a lot of time, but they might also encounter all kinds of accidents along the way.
"Three to four months." Luo Zheng nodded. After thinking for a while, he suddenly asked Ming Wei, "I want to seal a Soul Lodging Land in my Inner World. I wonder if Ming Wei has a method to seal it?"
"Soul Lodging Land?" Ming Wei was stunned. "Why do you want to seal this?"
In Ming Wei's eyes, sealing a Soul Lodging Land was an arduous and unrewarding task.
This was because after the Soul Lodging Land was given out, it was equivalent to giving the control of a part of the world to someone else.
"There is an extremely powerful genius in my Inner World. I want to seal a Soul Lodging Land for him." After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng decided to tell the truth.
Other than the matter of 9527 that he could not reveal, Luo Zheng did not need to hide anything else.
"Genius? How much of a genius is he? Could he be more outstanding than you, Luo Zheng? "Chi Yi asked with a smile.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly, and a bitter smile appeared on his face. "Perhaps in some aspects, this person is really more outstanding than me. If I release him, I'm afraid that it will be difficult to control him in the future. This Soul Lodging Land can be considered as a preparation for a rainy day."
As soon as he said this, Ming Wei and Chi Yi's faces immediately showed great interest.
A person like Luo Zheng had an extremely close bloodline relationship with Chi You. After all, he was a genius born from the six-stripe Blood Fixing Mysterious Species. Now that Luo Zheng said that there was someone more outstanding than him in his Inner World, it sounded somewhat unbelievable.
"Comprehending 3,000 Divine Daos in ten years is not outstanding?" Luo Zheng said lightly.
Chi Yi blinked his eyes, and Ming Wei's eyebrows also raised high.
3,000 Divine Daos was the definition of the various energy existences in the Chaos by the masters in the Mother World. According to the specific division, the number of Divine Daos was actually different. Even the vast majority of the True Gods in the Mother World only needed to master one or two Divine Daos to be able to attack the Sea of Truth.
Only a few abnormal geniuses were qualified to study multiple Divine Daos.
Of course, some top masters in the Mother World also tried to master 3,000 Divine Daos, and Chi You was one of them.
"Ten years, Luo Zheng, are you joking?" Ming Wei asked.
Existences like 9527 were indifferent to studying Divine Daos. Even if the Evil Deities were extremely terrifying, it could still calmly wait. The only thing that moved 9527 was that the Evil Deities had integrated 2,994 Divine Daos.

Chapter 2617
Conferring Saints
Luo Zheng shook his head and replied, "When I first discovered it, I also found it hard to believe, but it is indeed the truth …"
Ming Wei also felt that Luo Zheng could not be talking nonsense, but this kind of thing was indeed hard to believe at once.
"The method of conferring Spiritual Lands is not difficult." She turned to look at the other Nuwa woman on the Ling Xiao Flying Ship and said, "Ge Ling, copy the method of conferring Spiritual Lands to Luo Zheng in a jade slip."
The woman called Ge Ling nodded, and with a light flip of her lily-white hand, a long and narrow jade slip appeared in her hand.
She constantly poured her divine sense into the jade slip, and after about two incense sticks' worth of time, Ge Ling finished copying. She smiled at Luo Zheng and pushed the jade slip over.
After Luo Zheng opened the jade slip, he browsed through the method of conferring Spiritual Lands.
There was a limit to the number of Spiritual Lands that could be conferred out from the Inner World, and generally there would not be more than three.
The Divine Realm was an exception. Now there were about fifty Saints in the Divine Realm, but although there were many Spiritual Lands in the Divine Realm, the cultivation of the Saints was much lower than Chi You's.
After Luo Zheng memorized some of the key points in the jade slip, he once again condensed an Inner Avatar.
To the north of the Inner World was a barren land, and few creatures set foot on this land.
In this world, legend said that the Barren Land was inhabited by the God of Thunder and the Goddess of Lightning. Whenever they were angry, thunder would descend, making earth-shaking noises.
Evil Deities was located in the center of this barren land, sitting on a bare rock.
In Evil Deities' life, he had never encountered a bottleneck in any of his cultivation. Any cultivation method, any method, he could succeed in one go. This was the case when he cultivated martial arts, and it was the same when he cultivated the Divine Dao.
But now Evil Deities had encountered a huge bottleneck, and that was fusing the Divine Dao.
From Luo Zheng's eyes, Evil Deities understood that he had taken the lead, but the puppet beside Luo Zheng did not seem to think so.
Evil Deities was good at accepting challenges. Since he had decided to fuse the 3,000 Divine Daos, he had to succeed …
Because fusing the 3,000 Divine Daos was a huge challenge, he was obsessed with it, and even the idea of leaving this world had been diluted.
"Crack!"
As the sound of an explosion came from the Evil God's Inner World, his face sank slightly.

Chapter 2621
Entering the Pass

…

"Whoosh —"
While falling at high speed, He Yin was constantly adjusting his position to ensure that he could accurately approach the Soaring Firmament Airboat.
50 kilometers, 50 kilometers …
"Buzz!"
The moonlight slaughter was once again used by Chi Yi.
Ming Wei raised her head to look at the approaching figure in white. The silver-white moonlight in her hand was also constantly gathering.
Just as the other party was about 10 kilometers away, Ming Wei gently waved her finger. A colorful halo had already spread into the sky, and this colorful halo was also mixed with the power of the Moonlight Slaughter Formation!
She knew that the opponent's strength was extraordinary, so she used a killing move!
"Heh …"

Among these people, there were Nirvana Realm experts, but most of them were True Gods. When they saw the power of this blade light, fear appeared on their faces.
However, such a battle was not rare in the mother world. As long as it did not affect them, they would just treat it as a show.
"Boom!"

CHAPTER 2623 - CHAPTER 263
A Strange Sandstorm
In a short moment, the yellow sandstorm had completely enveloped Luo Zheng.
This sandstorm was enough to sweep ordinary mortals and livestock into the sky, but it posed no threat to Luo Zheng.
The fine gravel was like dense raindrops falling towards Luo Zheng, but it all stopped three inches in front of Luo Zheng. An invisible force formed a film that isolated all the gravel outside.
"Heaven's Wall City to the north of the desert …"
Looking at the gravel flying all over the sky, Luo Zheng revealed a bitter smile.
When he first stepped into the Realm of the Gods, he was alone. Without anyone to guide him, he could only slowly explore the Realm of the Gods.
This time, with Ming Wei leading the team, he originally wanted to save a lot of trouble, but he didn't expect that they would get separated at the first step into the Mother World!
He had used Greater Teleportation hundreds of times to wander around the Scarlet Sun Desert. It could be imagined that this desert was very large. Even if it was a clear sky, Luo Zheng would find it difficult to distinguish between north, south, east, and west, let alone in this sand-filled weather.
"It's best to find someone and ask around!"
Luo Zheng was only at a loss for a moment, but he had already collected his thoughts. After taking a light step, he disappeared from the top of the hill.
Although he couldn't distinguish the direction, Luo Zheng tried his best to use Greater Teleportation in one direction. This way, he could ensure that he left this desert with the shortest route.
"Buzz …"
With a single Greater Teleportation, Luo Zheng had already crossed a distance of fifty thousand kilometers.
The moment he came out of the spatial passage, he was still met with yellow sand flying towards him.
Looking at the sandstorm in front of him, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of surprise.
Sandstorms weren't rare weather. In the desert, when there was a strong wind, there would be sandstorms. However, he had already crossed a distance of fifty thousand kilometers and still couldn't get out of this sandstorm. The range of this sandstorm was beyond his imagination.
"The strength of the wind in this desert isn't strong enough to create such a large-scale sandstorm. How did this sandstorm come about?"
Luo Zheng frowned. He couldn't figure out the mystery, so he could only use Greater Teleportation again.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
He used Greater Teleportation seven times in a row and traveled a total of four to five hundred thousand miles. However, everywhere he went, there was still a raging sandstorm.
Not being able to determine where he was made him panic.
Luo Zheng no longer moved forward. Instead, he changed his direction and used Greater Teleportation towards the sky!
Ming Wei had said that the mother world was like a flattened egg. However, this egg was too big. Even if Luo Zheng kept using Greater Teleportation, he would not be able to touch the top of the mother world.
However, as he continued to move upwards, he realized that things were getting weirder and weirder.
He shifted upwards three times and arrived at a height of 150,000 miles. As he stood in the air, he was suddenly surrounded by rolling yellow sand.
"The height of this sandstorm is over a hundred and fifty thousand kilometers?"
Luo Zheng hadn't taken the sandstorm seriously before, but now he realized the strangeness of it.
Helplessly, he could only lower his altitude and fly close to the desert …
Moving forward like this naturally wasn't as fast as Greater Teleportation, but it could help him better distinguish his surroundings.
After flying like this for several hours, Luo Zheng suddenly saw a few bulges under the sand dune to his left. These bulges were covered by sand, so he couldn't see what they were.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment and spread out his divine sense.
As he investigated with his divine sense, a trace of joy appeared on his face. Those bulges were actually tents, and there were people inside the tents!
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate in the slightest and flew towards the tents.
The sandstorm had been blowing for so long that a thick layer of yellow sand had already covered the tents.
Luo Zheng gently waved his hand and swept away the yellow sand outside the tents.
The tent's entrance was a curtain sewn from a thick layer of animal skin. Luo Zheng reached out and gently lifted the curtain. The moment he lifted it, a sharp, snow-white knife pierced towards him!
"Whoosh!"
The knife's strength wasn't small, and it was aimed directly at Luo Zheng's chest.
Luo Zheng's expression was indifferent. His right hand shot out like lightning and pinched the sharp knife's edge with two fingers. The knife couldn't move in the slightest.
"Don't …"
Just as Luo Zheng passed by, several spears stabbed towards him from both sides of the curtain.
These spears were aimed at Luo Zheng's eyes, face, chest, and throat. They obviously wanted to kill him.
Although Luo Zheng wasn't afraid of ordinary swords and sabers, he was still a little angry at being treated like this. Luo Zheng's hands danced like flowers, and the spears that were stabbing towards him were all snatched away by Luo Zheng.
Several lean young men stood in the tent. These young men didn't expect Luo Zheng to have such strength. They continuously retreated, and at the same time pulled out the scimitars at their waists.
But the space in the tent was very small, and they couldn't use their full strength. For a moment, it was chaos.
"I'm just passing by, why are you pulling out your scimitars?" Luo Zheng threw those spears on the ground and asked coldly.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, those young men were slightly stunned.
The leader had a scar on his face. He looked at Luo Zheng with a trace of suspicion, and immediately asked: "You, you're not an assassin sent by the King?"
Luo Zheng sighed in relief. The other party spoke the same language, so at least they could communicate smoothly.
The cultural inheritance in the Divine Domain wasn't spontaneous, but came from the Mother World. The language used here was of the same origin as the Divine Domain.
"Of course not, I'm just passing by," Luo Zheng replied indifferently.
Those young men were skeptical, but they still held their scimitars tightly, and didn't have any intention of backing down.
At this moment, a soft female voice suddenly came from the inner part of the tent, "Let him in."
"But Senior Magic Flute …" The scar-faced youth had a worried expression.
That soft body came again, "No need to say anymore. If he was really sent by the King to kill me, all of us would already be dead."
This girl's eyesight was very accurate. With Luo Zheng's strength, he only needed one palm to turn these tents and the people inside into ashes.
When those young men heard this, they looked at Luo Zheng with a trace of fear in their eyes. Based on that move just now, they were definitely not Luo Zheng's opponent.
As the young men moved aside, Luo Zheng discovered that these tents had a passage between them.
He followed the passage where the voice came from, and a faint fragrance immediately assailed his nostrils. In this tent, there was a veiled girl.
Under this thin veil, there was a slightly round little face. This girl's eye sockets were deeper, and although her appearance couldn't be said to be devastatingly beautiful, it had an exotic charm.

Chapter 2624
Magic Flute's Condition
The Third Layer Pass was one of the exits to the outside of the Mother World.
Although it wasn't the main exit, the Triple Layer Pass didn't belong to any faction, so it was favored by the neutral merchant alliance.
The large clans in the Mother World were intertwined and intertwined. They often had to go through a lot of trouble to arrange some goods along the way.
Some of the more powerful merchant alliances came up with the idea that it would be better to transport living creatures out of the mother world and then return to the mother world through various checkpoints. This could also be considered a shortcut.
The Chiyang Desert was adjacent to the Three Stacks Pass. Those merchants who were familiar with the trade routes would naturally not get lost in this desert. However, there were always some exceptions, and Luo Zheng was one of them.
"Yes," Luo Zheng admitted with a nod.
However, this woman would never have thought that Luo Zheng was not from the Origin World.
"After this sandstorm rolled up, it actually shrouded this desert. I've been looking for it for a long time, but I still couldn't get past it. I wonder if there's a trick in the Chiyang Desert?" Luo Zheng took the opportunity to ask his doubts.
Unexpectedly, the woman didn't directly answer Luo Zheng's question. She smiled faintly and said slowly, "Sir, if you want to leave the Red Sun Desert, where do you plan to go?"
Luo Zheng expected that she wouldn't have any connection with the Xuan Yuan Wei, so he immediately said, "Heaven's Wall City."
"Heaven's Wall City is a big city north of the Scarlet Sun Desert. I've been there before."
Luo Zheng saw that she was trying to evade his question, so he was a little impatient. He asked directly, "If that's the case, can you tell me how to leave this desert?"
"Of course there is a way to leave this desert, but you need to wait until the sandstorm in the Red Sun Desert subsides. You can walk out of the Red Sun Desert by heading north," the woman replied with a smile.
Luo Zheng also knew that the reason he couldn't get out of the desert was most likely because of the sandstorm. He then asked, "How long will this sandstorm last?"
"Once the sandstorm starts in the Red Sun Desert, it will last for about one and a half years," the woman said.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He still wanted to rush to Heaven's Wall City to meet up with Ming Wei and the others, but this was taking too long.
"Is there no other way?" Luo Zheng asked.
The woman calmly said, "This sandstorm is an illusion created by the breath of the Fog Summoning God Spirit. If a normal person enters, they will not be able to leave. But we, the Sand Nation, have a way."
"Mist-summoning spirit?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
The lady smiled gently and said, "In your eyes, it should be a powerful ferocious beast. However, it is our god and has been protecting our Sand Country for countless years..."
Luo Zheng made some inquiries and roughly understood the situation.
In the eyes of those Paramita Realm experts, the Crimsonsun Desert was naturally a wasteland.
In fact, this desert wasn't that desolate. There were oases and a large number of Sand Nation people. There was even a country and dozens of large and small tribes.
The Fog Summoning Godly Spirit should be a ferocious beast that occupied the Scarlet Sun Desert. This ferocious beast was good at creating illusions.
To be able to trap Luo Zheng in this sandstorm, it was enough to show that the "Fog Summoning Godly Spirit" mentioned by the lady should be an extremely powerful existence. Even Paramita Realm experts would find it difficult to kill it.
Therefore, most of the caravans and flying boats that wanted to pass through the Three-Layered Pass would take a detour after entering the pass and avoid the Scarlet Sun Desert.
In his panic, Luo Zheng naturally fell into this desert and was unable to find an exit.
Also, because of the existence of the "Fog Summoning Godly Spirit", it protected the Sand Nation people in the desert. Naturally, the Sand Nation people worshipped this ferocious beast as a god.
"Since you have a way to leave, please point the way. If there's anything I can help with, please advise me," Luo Zheng said.
He also understood the meaning of the lady's words.
This lady was obviously being chased by someone, which was why she hid in the depths of the desert.
She took a detour for a long time, as if she wanted to ask for his help.
Since Luo Zheng had a favor to ask of her, he straightforwardly said it.
When the lady heard Luo Zheng's words, her eyes lit up slightly.
She had been to the Heaven's Wall City and was after all a Sand Nation person who had seen the world. She was unable to judge the strength of this person, but since Luo Zheng was an outsider, his strength was definitely not weak. If she could ask for his help, it could be said to be saving her life.
However, she was afraid that Luo Zheng would reject her, so she did not ask directly. Now that Luo Zheng took the initiative to ask, she was relieved.
It was not that she was scheming, but she was afraid that she would miss the opportunity that fell from the sky.
"To tell you the truth, Young Master Luo, Magic Flute was originally the princess of the Sand Nation. A few months ago, there was a rebellion in the Sand Nation. Magic Flute's brothers were all killed by the new King. Magic Flute borrowed the protection of the Fog Summoning Godly Spirit to escape into the desert and keep her life. If Young Master can help Magic Flute take back the throne, Magic Flute will naturally be extremely grateful!" Her voice suddenly became low.
Magic Flute was very clear that the debt of her bringing Luo Zheng out of the desert was not enough for someone like Luo Zheng to take the risk to help. This time, she revealed a pitiful expression.
The battle for the throne was originally very cruel. Her only choice was to leave her home, but Luo Zheng's appearance gave her a glimmer of hope.
Luo Zheng considered in his heart. This matter was not difficult for Luo Zheng.
From the looks of the guards beside this lady, the strong martial artists of the Sand Nation were most likely a group of Divine Sea and Divine Transformation Realm martial artists. It would not take too much time to help her get rid of these troubles. He nodded and agreed.
"Since it is so, Young Master Luo, follow Magic Flute and set off!"
After that, she called for the guards to remove the tents.
After a series of preparations, Magic Flute took out a curved green flute. She placed the flute on her lips and gently blew on it. A melodious flute sound was transmitted out.
"Wu wu wu …"
Just as the flute sound was heard, a strange scene appeared.
The yellow sand that was originally rolling in all directions seemed to have heard the flute's command and moved to the sides.
"Eh?"
Just as the yellow sand moved away, Luo Zheng felt a slight fluctuation of spatial laws.
It seemed that the illusion created by the sandstorm did indeed use spatial laws, and even the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment. He did not know what kind of ferocious beast was hiding in the desert to be able to create such a strange illusion.
"This flute was bestowed by the Fog Summoning God. Magic Flute used this flute to escape from the Sand Nation," Magic Flute said softly to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng looked at the flute and asked, "In the entire Sand Nation, only you have this flute?"
If he used this flute to open a path, he could hide in the desert and escape the pursuit of others.
Magic Flute shook his head and said, "The Sand Nation has a total of three Flutes of Protection. That is why we are worried that the King will send people to kill us."
Luo Zheng nodded. Since he had already found a way to leave the desert, he was not so anxious.

Chapter 2625
Semblance
True Gods rarely cared about the conflicts of the secular world. In the Divine Domain, those true gods who had attained the Divine Dao were all racking their brains to enter the floating islands, hoping to obtain better resources.
It was the same in the Mother World. After the True Gods had reached the Great Perfection Realm, they would wholeheartedly set foot in the Sea of True Meaning, hoping to reach the Nirvana Realm as soon as possible.
However, there was one difference between the Mother World and God's Domain.
The Divine Realm's Divine Citizens could only live outside the Divine City, unable to form a large scale.
The number of Gods in the Origin World was countless times more than in the Divine Domain. These Gods had formed their own countries. Between the countries, there were battles, battles, and the succession of the throne.
The Sand Country was merely one of the countless countries in the massive Mother World.
After all, the divine folk were not mortals. Even if they had not reached the Divine Sea Realm, their physical strength and endurance were not much weaker than those in the Divine Sea Realm.
In half a day's time, Magic Flute had already brought Luo Zheng over a hundred miles.
Other than using the "Flute of Protection" to open up the sandstorm, Magic Flute was constantly changing its route.
Seeing Magic Flute's actions, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of suspicion.
The scenery in the desert was dry and desolate. There were almost no landmarks in the surroundings. Moreover, under the rolling sandstorm, these sand dunes would change their positions.
Although she could rely on the flute to break through the spatial illusion, what did she rely on to identify the path?
Luo Zheng's heart was filled with suspicions, so he was a little more careful.
He quietly spread out his Divine Sense, and then projected the surrounding sand dunes into his mind. With Luo Zheng's current memory, he could clearly remember all the details of the surroundings.
Two hours later, Luo Zheng looked at the sand dune not far away. A strange light flashed in the depths of his pupils.
"Seven sand dunes. Although the position has changed slightly, I should have been here just now!" Luo Zheng thought.
Suspicions were suspicions, but Luo Zheng remained calm and collected. He continued to follow Magic Flute, and at the same time, quietly sized up the guards behind Magic Flute.
These guards had grim expressions on their faces. That scarred man was at the back of the group, looking around as if he was on guard against something …
Another two hours later, Luo Zheng saw the seven sand dunes again. However, the seven sand dunes had moved a little bit further.
At this moment, Luo Zheng could roughly confirm that this lady called Magic Flute was indeed leading him around in circles!
He wondered what she was up to …
Luo Zheng still didn't say anything despite his suspicions.
"Before nightfall, we should be able to find the Sand Country. At that time, I'll have to trouble Young Master Luo," Magic Flute said softly as he put down his flute. He smiled sweetly at Luo Zheng, and his face under the veil revealed a charming smile.
Anyone who saw this smile would feel a rush of excitement, and Luo Zheng was no exception.
But after realizing that Magic Flute was leading him around in circles, Luo Zheng was extremely vigilant. Magic Flute's pitiful words could not be trusted.
After half an hour, Luo Zheng saw a greyish yellow city wall in the rolling sand. Not far ahead, there really was a big city!
And those seven sand dunes stood at the side of the Sand Country.
