At this moment, Magic Flute smiled at Luo Zheng, "Young Master Luo, please follow me into the Sand Country and help me kill the King. If Magic Flute can regain the throne, my Sand Country will reward you handsomely."
"Alright," Luo Zheng nodded.
He wanted to see what Magic Flute was up to.
As the group slowly walked towards the city gate, Luo Zheng asked as he walked, "Since the Sand Country's King took the throne, why didn't he send people to protect the city? There seems to be no one on my city wall? "
Magic Flute thought for a while, frowning slightly under the veil, and replied, "My royal family has already been massacred by the King. I think the King thinks that a little girl like me will not be able to cause any trouble, so naturally there is no need to be on guard."
"But I see that there are no other Sand Citizens in this Sand Country?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Today's sand is rolling, the Sand Citizens should all be at home. If Young Master Luo enters, you will naturally see them," Magic Flute replied.
In the midst of this questioning, Magic Flute had already led Luo Zheng into the city.
Indeed, it was as Magic Flute had said. In the wide streets of the Sand Country, there would occasionally be a few Sand Citizens passing through.
But those Sand Citizens' gait was very strange, as if they were dragging their feet on the ground.
Luo Zheng stared at those Sand Citizens, his eyes flashing with a green luster. With the help of the Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes, those Sand Citizens had already revealed their true appearance. They were actually statues made of sand!
"Young Master Luo, quickly follow me to the palace," Magic Flute urged, his voice also becoming somewhat urgent.
But Luo Zheng had an unhurried look, and asked indifferently, "Such a big Sand Country, with so many Sand Citizens, I wonder what will be used to feed them?"
Divine Citizens were not martial artists. If they had not stepped into the Spirit Sea Realm, they would not be able to abstain from eating. So the Divine Citizens of the Divine Domain needed to grow crops and raise livestock to feed themselves.
For such a big Sand Country to survive in the rolling sand, they could not possibly rely on sand for food, right?
"This …"
Luo Zheng threw out many questions along the way, and Magic Flute had to rack his brains to answer them.
But this question, Magic Flute could not give a reasonable answer.
Luo Zheng then laughed lightly, "Or is it that there is actually no one in this Sand Country?"
Magic Flute and the guards behind her had a huge change in expression, but Magic Flute still calmed down and replied, "Young Master Luo, are you joking? Such a big Sand City, how can there be no one, I … "
Luo Zheng snorted coldly, the tip of his foot lightly stepped on the ground, and a terrifying power flooded downwards.
The sand on the ground, under the surge of power, spread out like a ripple …
"Shua shua …"
With Luo Zheng as the center, the ripples spread out in an earth-shattering manner. The buildings in the sandstorm began to collapse one after another, and the Sand Nation people turned into piles of yellow sand.
Magic Flute and the guards quickly retreated, and heard her shout, "Fog Summoning Senior! I have brought the person to you! Please show yourself quickly! "
"Hu hu …"
At this moment, the wind blew, and the seven sand dunes that originally stood outside the city seemed to have grown legs, and quickly spread towards Luo Zheng.
As the sand dunes moved, the surface layer of sand was constantly shaken off, and from the sand dunes, thick green vines were revealed. And at the top of the vines, there was a big mouth full of sharp teeth, the shape was strange and exaggerated.
"Is this a plant … or a beast?"
Luo Zheng looked at this thing, and his face showed some disappointment.

Chapter 2626
Accompanying the Sand People
Magic Flute and her guards had retreated far away. Under the veil, her beautiful face showed some excitement.
It was a trap from the start.
Although the last step was seen through by Luo Zheng, her goal had been achieved.
As long as the Fog Summoning Venerable swallowed Luo Zheng, she and her subordinates would get a good reward!
"In this Scarlet Sun Desert, there should be many Sand People, right?"
Even though the "Fog Summoner Venerable" was close at hand, Luo Zheng still turned his head and asked indifferently.
Magic Flute looked at Luo Zheng strangely, not knowing why he would ask such a question.
But she still smiled sweetly, "Of course there are, and we Sand People make a living from this. Sorry, kind person!"
It was extremely difficult for ordinary Sand People to survive in this barren desert, but there was a "Fog Summoning Venerable" in this desert.
This "Misty Summoning Sage" had a very powerful talent. It could set up a spatial illusion array to trap a lost True God. However, its weakness was also obvious. The Misty Summoning Sage was a plant after all. Its range of movement was very small.
So a part of the Sand People chose to cooperate with the Fog Summoning Venerable to lure the lost True Gods to this place for the Fog Summoning Venerable to swallow them. After the Fog Summoning Venerable swallowed the True Gods, it could secrete a light green tree sap. After swallowing the tree sap, the Sand People could live longer and be immune to diseases.
So many Sand People made up a story to gain the trust of outsiders and bring them to the mouth of the Fog Summoning Venerable.
"As a human, you actually colluded with a monster, what a shame..." Luo Zheng stared at Magic Flute and said in a light tone.
"Keke... this time there is a Perfect True God, what good luck." The Fog Summoning Venerable's mouth was full of sharp teeth and made a strange sound. It could actually speak human language.
"Shua!"
Just as the Fog Summoning Venerable finished speaking, a vine full of sharp thorns swept out. After washing a gully in the desert, it rolled towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not dodge and allowed the vine to wrap around his body.
"Shame? It's just a way to survive. "Magic Flute's face revealed a crafty look.
The guards around her all sneered.
"This prey sure is full of nonsense …"
"You're about to die, and you're still talking about humiliation? This is the first time I've seen this, haha!"
"Magic Flute, this time I want more of the Fog Summoning Venerable's tree sap, you promised me last time!"
In their eyes, Luo Zheng was hung upside down by the vines. Then, with a swing of the vines, his entire body was thrown into a huge mouth.
The big mouth opened and closed, chewing continuously.
After all, the Fog-Summoning Venerable was a plant. It had to chew Luo Zheng into pieces before it could digest him little by little.
But after chewing a few times, that big mouth suddenly stopped. Even the other big mouths full of fangs shuddered and stopped in midair.
When Magic Flute and the rest saw the Fog Summoning Elder acting like that, they all had strange looks on their faces.
"What's wrong with the Fog-Summoning Venerable?"
"That Luo fellow doesn't taste good? Hahaha … "
The guards joked.
Only Magic Flute's eyes revealed a hint of wariness. Just now, Luo Zheng's fearless look made her feel very strange.
The "Fog Summoning Venerable" that the Sand Nation people spoke of was actually called "Arabian Demonic Flower". This Arabian Demonic Flower had grown to such a stage that other than Nirvana Realm experts, it could easily devour True Gods.
The Arabian Demonic Flower's spatial illusion formation could even confuse Nirvana Realm experts. But when it encountered a real Nirvana Realm expert, Magic Flute would obediently point out a way out and let those Nirvana Realm experts go. Let the Arabian Demonic Flower devour a Nirvana Realm expert? Not only would that harm the Arabian Demonic Flower, even they would not be able to escape!
But this Luo fellow was just a Perfect True God, there shouldn't be any problem, right?
Just as Magic Flute was faintly worried, a majestic force shot out from the Arabian Demonic Flower's mouth.
Then Magic Flute saw that the big mouth suddenly swelled up, as if something was trying to struggle out!
"Peng!"
A second later, the mouth was completely obliterated.
Luo Zheng's body instantly doubled in size, and in the eyes of Magic Flute and the others, he had transformed into a giant more than ten thousand zhang tall!
In his Desolate God form, Luo Zheng lightly extended his hand, pulling the vines and the seven mouths of the Arabian Demonic Flower together. With a fierce tug, he pulled it out of the ground!
As he squeezed, the Arabian Demonic Flower's mouths were reduced to pieces.
"Only a part?"
Luo Zheng looked at the broken roots of the vines, then turned his head and stared into the distance. Once again, he stretched out his hand towards the bottom of the desert. With a sudden dig, he had already dug out a large pit in the empty desert!
Lying in this pit was a strangely shaped plant. This plant had hands and feet, and seemed to be in the shape of a human. Upon closer inspection, one could faintly make out a face. This face, like a human's, was tinged with fear.
"You are the true body of the Fog Summoner Venerable, correct?" Luo Zheng sneered, his massive fist smashing down into the pit.
"I, I'm not … don't … I can give it to you …" the Arabian Demonic Flower begged for mercy.
But Luo Zheng showed no hesitation. His massive fist had already smashed it into a pool of goo.
This goo exuded a faint fragrance. Luo Zheng had smelled this fragrance before, as it had assaulted his nose when he first saw Magic Flute.
After dealing with the Arabian Demonic Flower, Luo Zheng dispersed the Desolate God energy in his body.
Magic Flute and the others stared in shock at Luo Zheng, their eyes filled with fear.
Let alone these Sand Nation people, even many Paramita realm experts had never heard of the Desolate God, a product of ancient times.
Seeing Luo Zheng smash the Arabian Demonic Flower into pieces like a giant god, they were all scared silly.
"Colluding with a vicious creature is not only a disgrace, but also a waste of my trust," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Magic Flute's guards finally regained their senses and turned to run. Only Magic Flute stood there, motionless.
Luo Zheng stretched out a hand towards those guards. Lightning surged from his fingertips, and a bolt of lightning bloomed.
This bolt of lightning suddenly leaped through the air, jumping several times on the bodies of those guards, instantly turning their bodies into charcoal.
"Why didn't you run?" Luo Zheng stared at Magic Flute and asked.
Magic Flute laughed bitterly and muttered, "Someone who can easily kill the Fog Summoner Venerable, running is meaningless …"
In the end, they were only God's people. Borrowing the power of the Fog Summoner Venerable, they could kill a True God. But they themselves had no strength to speak of, and were at the bottom of the Mother World.
"You are right."
Luo Zheng said expressionlessly. Another bolt of lightning struck Magic Flute's body from his fingertips.
Her originally curvaceous body was also turned into charcoal, and the flute in her hand was bounced out, landing in the desert.

Chapter 2627
Release
After killing the Arab-Eating Demonic Flower, the sandstorm within a radius of a hundred miles disappeared.
Luo Zheng looked into the distance. A hundred miles away, there was still rolling sand …
"It seems that there is more than one Fog-Summoning Venerable in the Chiyang Desert. They are everywhere. This Fog-Summoning Venerable only has a radius of a hundred miles. If I continue to move forward, I am afraid that I will fall into the spatial illusions of other Fog-Summoning Venerables …"
As Luo Zheng spoke, his eyes fell on the flute on the ground. He reached out and grabbed the flute. The flute spun on the ground and was caught in his hand. "This so-called Flute of Protection should be made up by her. But this flute can indeed break the spatial illusions of the Fog-Summoning Venerable. It must be a tool used by the Sand Nation to deceive the True Gods. But if I want to get out of this desert, I am afraid that I have to rely on this flute."
Luo Zheng had just finished speaking when 9527's voice came. "Your analysis is completely correct. Hehe, it seems that this puzzle is not difficult for you."
Hearing 9527's words, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. "You knew from the beginning?"
"Of course. This Fog-Summoning Venerable's original name is the Arab-Eating Demonic Flower. It is just a demonic plant. But its spatial illusions are indeed unique," 9527 said.
"Then why didn't you tell me earlier?" Luo Zheng asked.
9527 continued to chuckle and said, "I'm just letting you experience the malice of the Mother World. This place is not like God's Domain. The environment of the Mother World is much crueler and worse. Dangers like this can be found everywhere."
Luo Zheng held back for a long time. Finally, he rolled his eyes and spat out two words. "Idiot!"
But 9527 was right about one thing. After all, the divine realm was a world built by Chiyou for Jiuli. So the most dangerous places in the divine realm were concentrated in the forbidden areas. The threats outside the forbidden areas were not great. There were almost no ferocious creatures.
The Mother World was much crueler than the Divine Realm. There were no forbidden areas here, but everywhere was forbidden.
Although Luo Zheng had been led around in circles by the Sand Nation people, he still had a rough understanding of the Scarlet Sun Wasteland. Magic Flute's words were half true and half false, but the direction he identified should be correct.
After confirming the direction, Luo Zheng flew towards the north with the flute.
After a while, Luo Zheng once again entered the sandstorm.
When he was caught up in the sandstorm, he was sure that he had fallen into another spatial illusion, presumably created by another Arabian demonic flower.
Luo Zheng placed the flute near his mouth and gently blew it. As the melodious sound of the flute spread out, the yellow sand that was sweeping towards them immediately moved to the sides.
This flute truly could easily break through spatial illusions. In other words, the Arabian demonic flower wanted the Sand Nation people to help it attract prey, so it would naturally yield to the sound of the flute. This was a tacit understanding between the Sand Nation people and the Arabian demonic flower.
Luo Zheng could also directly find the Arabian demonic flower in this spatial illusion and crush it, but there were countless Arabian demonic flowers in the Scarlet Sun Wasteland, and he didn't have the time to get rid of them one by one.
As Luo Zheng flew over with the flute, he saw a black-clothed woman lying on a distant sand dune.
As Luo Zheng got closer, he saw that the woman's leg was injured.
The black-clothed woman had probably sensed Luo Zheng's existence. Beneath her long eyelashes, her lovely eyes looked up. Just when she was about to call for help, she noticed the flute in Luo Zheng's hand and paled.
Luo Zheng stared at the black-clothed woman and sneered. The Profound Lightning Dao in his hand began to gather, and a bolt of lightning began to gather.
When the woman in black saw this, her face turned pale. She suddenly got up from the sand dune and ran away in a scrambling manner …
This black-clothed woman was clearly another Sand Nation person. She had been lying here to deceive the lost True God, but when she saw the flute Luo Zheng had seized, she instantly understood that this fellow could not be her prey.
Luo Zheng did not pursue the woman. He continued to play the flute as he flew through the air.
As he traveled north, Luo Zheng encountered ten-some groups of Sand Nation people. These Sand Nation people used similar methods to deceive others. They would either appear to be refugees in groups or beautiful women traveling alone.
After flying through the sandstorm for seven to eight hours, Luo Zheng finally saw a hint of green in the dusky desert. A few short green shrubs dotted the desert like stars.
As they continued to head north, there were more and more green shrubs. After crossing a clear river, the Scarlet Sun Wasteland ended here. On the other side of the river was a brown land, and in the distance, there was a huge city standing tall.
"That should be the Heaven's Wall City that Ming Wei mentioned." Luo Zheng looked at the city from the sky.
Luo Zheng did not enter the Heaven's Wall City immediately. He found a secluded place and set up an illusion array. Then, he sat cross-legged and transformed into an Inner Body Incarnation to enter the Inner World.
He had originally promised the Evil Deities that he would release the Evil Deities immediately after entering the Mother World.
However, after entering the Mother World, Luo Zheng immediately fell into the Red Sun Desert. Naturally, he could not leave the Evil Deities in that place.
The Evil Deities had been restless these few days. He was afraid that Luo Zheng would deliberately "forget" him again.
After becoming a Perfected True God, the Evil Deities had to redo the fusion of Dao energy. This was because the Dao energy that he had previously fused was not perfected.
Although he had to redo the fusion, the sequence of the fusion of Divine Dao was unchanged. The speed at which the Evil Deities redid the fusion was not slow. However, perhaps because he was restless and hoped that he could leave this world as soon as possible, or perhaps he was doing it on purpose, accidents often occurred during the fusion process.
When the perfected fusion of Dao energy exploded, the power was not ordinary. Although there was no one on the Barren Continent, a perfectly good continent was almost blown to pieces by him.
The moment Luo Zheng appeared in front of him, the eyes of the Evil Deities suddenly lit up. He suddenly stood up and stared at Luo Zheng. "I, I can go out now?"
Luo Zheng looked at the anxious look of the Evil Deities and smiled faintly. "If I don't let you out now, I'm afraid you will destroy this world!"
The Evil Deities smiled embarrassedly.
"Aren't you going to say goodbye to your family?" Luo Zheng asked.
"No need." The Evil Deities shook his head with certainty.
"Alright then, relax." Luo Zheng said as he stretched out his hand and pushed the Evil Deities.
The Evil Deities felt an invisible force choking him. Under the guidance of that force, the Evil Deities felt that he was constantly ascending, and ascending …
He flew past the blue sky and white clouds. He then flew past the nine stars and flew toward the deepest part of this world.

Chapter 2628
Entering the City
Evil God stood there and didn't move at all. He looked at the world like he was sensing something.
In fact, Luo Zheng found it hard to understand Evil Deities' feelings. This new world was a new starting point for Evil Deities.
"There's a desert over there, and there's a huge city. Where is this?" Evil Deities asked Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng blinked, then shook his head. "To be honest, I'm not sure."
"You're not sure?" Evil Deities didn't believe him.
Luo Zheng found it hard to explain the connection between the Mother World, the Divine Realm, the universe, and the Chaos. He said, "Yes, because like you, this is the first time I've set foot in this world. You can call it the Mother World, and it's also the largest world!"
"Is there another world outside this one?" Evil Deities asked again.
"It's the Chaos," Luo Zheng said.
Evil Deities thought for a while, then asked, "Is there another world outside the Chaos?"
Luo Zheng shrugged, shook his head, and said, "I don't know."
This question seemed simple, but it was probably related to philosophy. At least, Luo Zheng's understanding of the world was still limited to the Chaos.
Evil Deities didn't get an answer from Luo Zheng, but he didn't look disappointed. After all, his biggest wish was finally fulfilled. He had left Luo Zheng's Inner World.
"In that case, leave this question to me! Let's part ways here! " Evil Deities smiled, then bowed respectfully to Luo Zheng. He couldn't wait to get away from Luo Zheng.
Wisps of Spiritual Wind and Dark Thunder Taoist Connotation spread out from his body. These two Taoist Connotations softened each other and formed a unique Extra-Dao Magic, which was the Wind and Thunder Escape Method created by the Evil God himself.
Whoosh!
With the support of this escape technique, the Evil Deities flew along the river and disappeared in front of Luo Zheng in the blink of an eye.
On the surface, he maintained a respectful attitude towards Luo Zheng, but in his heart, he was extremely disgusted. With his personality, if not for the fact that the world within his body had turned into a "Spirit Hosting Ground" and was still restricted by Luo Zheng, he might have already attacked Luo Zheng just now!
"You avoided the Chiyang Desert and didn't want to go to Heaven's Wall City. Instead, you went with the flow. That's smart of you." Luo Zheng looked at the disappearing silhouette of the Evil Deities and said faintly, "I wonder if we will meet again …"
Luo Zheng was very clear about Evil Deities. Such a person would never know his place, and with his talent, he would eventually make his voice heard in the mother world.
After standing in place for a while, Luo Zheng turned around and headed for the Heaven's Wall City.
The Heaven's Wall City was different from the Heaven's Wall City in the mother world. Because those Heaven's Wall City had the protection of the stone slab, it had become a precious resource. If one wanted to enter a Heaven's Wall City, they had to pay Shenwu coins.
When Luo Zheng arrived at the city gate, he saw an endless stream of God citizens at the gate.
Among these God citizens, there were some Divine Sea Realm, Extreme Divine Realm, and Divine Transformation Realm martial artists. In addition, there were a few True Gods mixed in.
Luo Zheng didn't hide his strength. In an unfamiliar environment, hiding one's cultivation was often more dangerous. If some experts saw through it, it would arouse suspicion and jealousy.
After observing for a while, Luo Zheng followed the other God citizens into the Heaven's Wall City.
In the instant that Luo Zheng entered the city, he felt several divine senses sweep past him. Judging from the strength of these divine senses, the souls of the people who had released the divine senses should have already stepped into the Yang Soul Tier. In other words, their cultivation level was at least that of a Consummate True God.
Luo Zheng couldn't figure out the origin of these divine senses, so he pretended that he didn't know. He strolled along the streets of the Heaven's Wall City, looking around.
If Ming Wei and the others had shaken off the white-robed man's pursuit, they should have reached the Heaven's Wall City by now. He wondered if Ming Wei, Chi Yi, and the others had left behind some special mark or method of communication.
But Luo Zheng quickly rejected this idea. There were too many God citizens in the Heaven's Wall City. The buildings in the entire city were like scales of scales. Even the Central God City in the Divine Domain couldn't compare.
It looked like he had to stay in the Heaven's Wall City for a while. At the very least, he needed to find a channel to gather some information.
But if there was no news of Ming Wei and the others, he couldn't possibly stay in the Heaven's Wall City forever.
Just as Luo Zheng was vexed, a faint voice floated into Luo Zheng's ears. "Friend, why have you come to the Heaven's Wall City?"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. He glanced around and saw a long-haired youth sitting on the second floor of a building a few hundred feet away. This long-haired youth actually had the cultivation of Great Perfection.
This was the first Great Perfection that Luo Zheng had discovered in his mother world.
In Luo Zheng's opinion, there were many experts in his mother world. Even an existence like Ming Wei couldn't be considered the top.
There were probably as many Great Perfection True Gods as there were dogs. The situation in the Heaven's Wall City seemed to be poles apart from what he had imagined.
"I'm just passing by," Luo Zheng's lips moved slightly and he transmitted his voice using Qi.
The long-haired youth revealed a smile of disbelief and said to Luo Zheng, "Whether you're passing by or not, please come up and have a seat!"
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate. With a light sweep of his body, he went up the building and sat opposite the long-haired youth.
"My name is Lai Huabei. How should I address you?" The long-haired youth cupped his hands politely at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng immediately returned the greeting. "Luo Zheng."
Lai Huabei was slightly stunned. "The surname Luo? That's rare … "
Although the culture of the God Realm was inherited from the mother world, there were still differences. Many families in the God Realm had surnames that were naturally born. Therefore, the surname Luo was very common in the God Realm, but it was extremely rare in the mother world. At least, this was the first time Lai Huabei had heard of it.
"May I know why you're looking for me?" Luo Zheng asked.
Lai Huabei smiled faintly. "There are very few Great Perfection True Gods in the Heaven's Wall City. Since Brother Luo Zheng has entered the city, there will naturally be people who will come in contact with you. I'm just the first one."
Luo Zheng's expression was slightly surprised. According to Lai Huabei's words, a Great Perfection True God like him was in high demand in the Heaven's Wall City?
Thinking of the divine senses that had swept over him when he entered the city, he believed it a little.
Luo Zheng said, "I'm just passing by the Heaven's Wall City. I have no intention of staying here for long and have no intention of joining the forces here."
Before Lai Huabei could open his mouth to invite him, Luo Zheng had rejected him directly. There was a flash of disappointment on his face.
During this period of time, the Heaven's Wall City wasn't peaceful. If he could rope in the help of a Great Perfection True God, it would be very important to Lai Huabei.
Just then, Luo Zheng continued to speak, "However, I have something that I would like to inquire about."
Hearing that Luo Zheng wanted to inquire about something, Lai Huabei was instantly interested. "I wonder what Brother Luo Zheng wants to inquire about?"

Chapter 2629
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Rat Alliance
Heaven's Wall City was the first major city after entering the Triad Pass.
Some caravans would rest in Heaven's Wall City after bypassing it, so Nirvana Realm experts weren't rare.
Lai Huabei didn't know why Luo Zheng asked such a question, so he replied, "Large caravans arrive at the East Port of Heaven's Wall City every day. Those caravans have Nirvana Realm experts overseeing them, so Heaven's Wall City naturally has Nirvana Realm experts entering. Brother Luo Zheng, are you looking for someone?"
Luo Zheng said, "I'm looking for a Nirvana Realm woman."
Then, he gave a rough description of Ming Wei's attire and appearance.
Lai Huabei shook his head. "I haven't seen her before."
Luo Zheng wasn't surprised. Ming Wei was the strongest person in the group, so she would definitely receive the attention of the white-clothed man.
Even if Ming Wei was lucky enough to escape, she wouldn't expose her identity so easily. Now, looking for Ming Wei like this was like looking for a needle in a haystack.
Luo Zheng nodded and immediately got up to leave.
"I don't know, but the Rat Alliance might. If Brother Luo Zheng wants to buy information from the Rat Alliance, I can bring Brother Luo Zheng there," Lai Huabei added.
"Rat Alliance? What's that? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Lai Huabei looked surprised. "Brother Luo Zheng, you haven't even heard of the Rat Alliance?"
Luo Zheng shook his head. He really didn't know what the Rat Alliance was.
"Then it's definitely worth a trip, Brother Luo Zheng. There's probably no information in this world that the Rat Alliance doesn't know. However, information about the Rat Alliance requires divine crystals. I wonder if Brother Luo Zheng has enough on hand?" Lai Huabei asked again.
Hearing Lai Huabei mention divine crystals, Luo Zheng immediately asked, "How many divine crystals does it cost to find someone from the Rat Alliance?"
"At least one divine crystal," Lai Huabei replied, but there was some hesitation in his eyes.
When he saw that Luo Zheng didn't even know about the "Rat Alliance" and that Heaven's Wall City was very close to the Tri-Layer Pass, he suspected that Luo Zheng came from a remote area outside of the motherworld. People who came from remote areas were usually very poor and couldn't possibly take out so many divine crystals.
Lai Huabei wanted to rope in Luo Zheng. He was wondering if he should help Luo Zheng pay this sum of divine crystals. However, one divine crystal was a huge sum to Lai Huabei. Naturally, he was hesitant.
"One divine crystal?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
He had no concept of the value of divine crystals, but he had obtained Chiyou's legacy in the God's Domain. On this trip to the mother world, he had taken away almost half of the divine crystals. One divine crystal was too cheap for Luo Zheng.
Lai Huabei misunderstood and thought that Luo Zheng was frightened.
He thought that one divine crystal would be worth it if he could rope in Luo Zheng. After all, he was really short on manpower this time. He clenched his teeth and said, "Information from the Rat Alliance isn't cheap. If Brother Luo Zheng can't afford this price, I can help!"
Seeing Lai Huabei's pained expression, Luo Zheng was slightly amused. However, Lai Huabei had good intentions.
One divine crystal was considered expensive to a Perfected True God. This person was willing to help him when they had just met. Of course, he wanted to rope him in, but it was rare for him to do something like this.
"I won't trouble you. Although one divine crystal for information is a little expensive, I can still afford it. However, please lead the way," Luo Zheng said.
Lai Huabei looked at Luo Zheng suspiciously. He wondered if Luo Zheng could take out one divine crystal. However, he still led Luo Zheng out of the building.
"Who created the Rat Alliance? Can any information be bought?" Luo Zheng asked.
Just the Red Sun Desert and the surrounding area were very vast. The information Luo Zheng needed was almost nothing special. Luo Zheng was still skeptical that he could obtain the information he needed from the Rat Alliance.
However, Lai Huabei was very confident. "Since Brother Luo Zheng has never heard of the Rat Alliance, you naturally don't know how amazing it is. They really know everything. Brother Luo Zheng, you will understand in a while."
The two of them traveled through Heaven's Wall City. In half an hour, they had traveled through more than half of the city and arrived at the east side of Heaven's Wall City.
The streets on the east side of Heaven's Wall City became even more spacious. However, there were fewer people on the streets. Soon, the two of them stopped in front of an empty house. Two guards stood at the entrance of the house. The cultivation levels of the guards were also Perfected True Gods.
However, their attire was somewhat strange. Their entire bodies were wrapped in dark-colored robes. Moreover, the robes seemed to have the effect of blocking divine senses, preventing others from using divine senses to spy on them.
"This is the Rat Alliance. Brother Luo Zheng, you can enter by yourself. The Rat Alliance has many rules. I can only wait outside," said Lai Huabei.
Luo Zheng walked towards the house. One of the guards took the initiative to welcome him and asked, "Are you looking for someone or something?"
When he heard the guard's voice, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. The voice was very shrill. It didn't sound like a human's voice.
"I'm looking for someone," replied Luo Zheng.
"Do you know the rules?" asked the guard.
"I don't," said Luo Zheng.
"Once the information of the Rat Alliance is sold, it is not refundable. Furthermore, you cannot tell anyone that you obtained the information from us. If you violate the rules, you will be on the Rat Alliance's wanted list," continued the guard in his shrill voice.
These rules …
After pondering for a moment, Luo Zheng quickly understood.
No matter where it was, there was a great deal of taboo information. The Rat Alliance's information was likely to be quite risky. If the information was leaked and angered some experts, they might take their anger out on the Rat Alliance. That was why the Rat Alliance had set up such rules.
"Are you willing to abide by the rules?" asked the guard.
Luo Zheng raised his head and nodded. "Of course I'm willing …"
Halfway through his sentence, he suddenly saw the guard's true appearance through the gap in his robes. His heart was suddenly shocked.
This guard had a sharp mouth, and his eyes were the size of soybeans. His face was covered in a layer of dust!
How was he human? It was just like a rat!
The guard also noticed Luo Zheng's shock. He tightened his gray robes and said in a shrill voice, "Go in! Turn left and walk to the end. You'll be able to obtain information! "
These two guards were clearly rat-shaped Vicious Beasts!
If this were in the divine realm, Luo Zheng would have already killed them. However, this was his homeworld. There were many things that Luo Zheng couldn't understand. In the end, he restrained his killing intent.
No wonder they were called the Rat Alliance. This Rat Alliance was probably a faction formed by rats.
As he thought about it, Luo Zheng entered the house.
"Squeak, squeak, squeak …"
After entering the house, Luo Zheng heard rats roaming around the house. These rats were of different shapes and sizes. They were all running around the house.
Luo Zheng followed the guard's instructions and walked around. When he reached the end, he saw a white curtain.
As he approached, he heard a shrill voice from behind the curtain. "Come in!"
Was there another rat-shaped Vicious Beast inside?

Chapter 2630
Inside the Eared Mouse
Just as Luo Zheng had thought, what was behind the curtain was not a person, but a mouse.
This mouse was taller than a person and was seated cross-legged on the floor. A massive formation had been drawn on the floor of the room, and it was sitting in the very center of the formation.
"You seem to be hostile toward me?" This mouse seemed to be able to see through Luo Zheng's thoughts.
Luo Zheng was surprised, but he soon replied, "It's just that I'm not used to facing Savage Beasts …"
There were also people in the Divine Realm who raised Savage Beasts, and there were even quite a few contracted beasts in the universe. However, Savage Beasts were still Savage Beasts. They usually only interacted with their masters and could not integrate into human society.
But the situation in the mother world was somewhat different. These Savage Beasts had completely independent identities and lived peacefully with humans?
In the Scarlet Sun Desolate Wood, Luo Zheng had seen the Sand Nation colluding with the Arabian demonic flower and had reprimanded them, taking it as a disgrace. Now, it seemed that this situation was normal in the mother world.
The mouse did not seem to care about Luo Zheng's attitude. It asked, "I heard that you're looking for someone?"
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
"What's that person's cultivation level? What appearance? When and where did she appear? The more detailed you are, the easier it will be for me to find her, "it said.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment, then replied, "Three days ago, she entered the Third Layer Pass from the Primal Chaos. As for whether or not she passed through the Scarlet Sun Desert, I'm not sure. She's a Paramita Realm expert …"
Luo Zheng originally did not want to reveal too much information about Ming Wei. If this Rat Alliance had some sort of relationship with the Xuanyuan Guard, it would be troublesome.
But on second thought, the Xuanyuan Guard was even stronger than Ming Wei, so he probably did not have any sort of relationship with the remote Heaven's Wall City. He could only tell it everything he knew.
After listening to Luo Zheng's explanation, the rat nodded.
Then, it lightly waved its two claws, and the two large ears on its head perked up.
These ears were several times larger than its head, making the mouse look extremely strange. How was this a mouse? It was clearly a rabbit …
Buzz!
At the same time, the array on the floor of the room was activated, and Luo Zheng felt a powerful Divine Sense pouring into the array. After the mouse entered a meditative state, its soybean-sized eyes closed, and it actually entered a meditative state.
"I didn't expect the Ear Mouse clan to develop so quickly. Even in such a remote city, there are traces of them. Hehe …" 9527 suddenly transmitted a consciousness.

"Can they coexist peacefully with humans?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Although Luo Zheng had told Ming Wei and the others some information about the Mother World on the way to the Mother World, the Mother World was so huge that it was difficult to answer all the questions that were involved. Luo Zheng could only rely on himself to experience it.

Even the Chaos Ancient Gods "Ji" and "Ruin" were not humans. Even Nuwa herself could not be considered a human.
The concept of humans was established by Nuwa after she created the Cultivation Method of the Big World.
"The Ear Rat Clan's eyes and ears are all over the Mother World. They can communicate with their own kind. It is difficult to hide any information in this world from their eyes and ears. You were right to ask them for information about Ming Wei," 9527 said.
Although 9527 said so, Luo Zheng was still not used to it. After all, he had always respected humans since he was young. It was difficult for him to put those ferocious beasts on the same level as humans.
"Buzz …"
The light emitted by the formation on the floor of the room became brighter and brighter. Luo Zheng could feel countless tiny consciousnesses from the formation.
The owner of each consciousness was a rat. These rats were distributed in the desert, the wilderness, the forest, and the town. They were almost everywhere. They continuously passed on what they saw, heard, and knew to the Ear Rat.
Not long after, the light of the formation gradually faded. The Ear Rat immediately opened its eyes. It glanced at Luo Zheng and said, "I have found the person you are looking for."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of joy, "Where is she? Is she still alive? "
The Ear Rat calmly pushed a porcelain plate over and said, "This information is not cheap. It costs twenty divine gems. If you are willing to buy it, put the divine gems in the plate and I will tell you."
"Twenty divine gems?" Luo Zheng frowned.
Divine gems were not a problem for Luo Zheng, but Lai Huabei told him that he only needed one divine gem. Now that the price had suddenly increased by twenty times, it was naturally difficult for him to accept.
"There are different levels of information. Some important information can be bought for twenty divine crystals, or even twenty thousand divine crystals. We of the Ear Mouse Clan use our ears to listen to the world. Naturally, we can determine the value of information. If you don't want to buy it, then please leave." The Ear Mouse was used to this sort of thing.
Luo Zheng was somewhat helpless. He could only curl his lips. He raised his right hand and pointed it at the porcelain plate. He gently pulled on his Sumeru Ring. Sparkling and translucent divine gems fell onto the porcelain plate.
The Ear Rat casually fiddled with the porcelain plate to the side. He stretched out his sharp claws and lightly swiped at the formation on the floor. An illusion enveloped Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng saw Ming Wei and Chi Yi standing side by side. They were flying close to the ground.
Looking at the environment, it did not seem to be the Scarlet Sun Desert, but a plain.
"Where is this?" Luo Zheng stared at Ming Wei in the illusion and asked.
"Green Brocade Plains, two million kilometers away from Heaven's Wall City," the Ear Rat said in a shrill voice. "These two are not from the Xuan Yuan Guard, but they actually possess the Great Moonlight Slaughtering Formation. This is quite interesting …"
The illusion only lasted for a breath's time before it disappeared. Luo Zheng asked somewhat anxiously, "Is that all?"
He saw that Ming Wei and the others had activated the Great Moonlight Slaughtering Formation as they fled. They probably had not shaken off the white-clothed man's pursuit.
"Of course not. What you saw was just a scene projected by a rat." As the Ear Rat spoke, he once again stretched out his sharp claws and lightly swiped at the formation.
Soon, another scene appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng saw a dazzling blue light in the image. The white-clothed man had already caught up and was heading straight for Ming Wei and Chi Yi.

Chapter 2632
Dao Sword Palace
After entering the Three Stacks Pass, from the Scarlet Sun Desert to the Heaven's Wall City and then to the Green Brocade Plains, they all belonged to the Tiandu Region.
The Tiandu Region was extremely vast, and the Heaven's Wall City was only a springboard to other cities. Those large merchant groups were only using it as a springboard. All the giant flying ships would simply resupply in the Heaven's Wall City before quickly leaving for their next destination.
After staying in the Heaven's Wall City for a few days, Luo Zheng gradually gained a general understanding of the place.
"From what Brother Huabei said, it seems to take a lot of effort to travel from one region to another?" Luo Zheng asked.
Lai Huabei nodded, "Of course. The Mother World claims to be a complete world, but in reality, it's a region and a world. Most people, even those True Gods, find it difficult to cross a large region!"
Luo Zheng originally thought that the Mother World was the same as the Divine Territory and was a complete world. However, after listening to Lai Huabei, he was wrong.
In comparison, the Mother World's layout was somewhat similar to the Upper-Class World.
A region was equivalent to an independent world, and the regions were actually separated from each other.
"I wonder which region the famous Li Mountain is in? Is it far from here? "Luo Zheng asked again.
"Li Mountain …" Lai Huabei's eyes revealed a strange expression, "Li Mountain is in the Daluo Dao Region. It's naturally very far from our Tiandu Region. Could it be that Brother Luo Zheng still wants to go to Li Mountain?"
After interacting with Luo Zheng for a few days, Lai Huabei had already determined that Luo Zheng came from outside the Mother World. After all, Luo Zheng seemed to lack a lot of basic knowledge.
Luo Zheng shook his head. He would eventually go to Li Mountain, but not now.
Lai Huabei continued, "Ordinary Perfect True Gods like us don't have the qualifications to travel between regions unless we can join a region level sect. Only then will we have the qualifications."
"Region level sect? Is Li Mountain considered a region level sect? "Luo Zheng asked.
Lai Huabei smiled and shook his head, "Li Mountain definitely isn't. To us True Gods, an existence like Li Mountain is a legendary great power! The region level sect I'm talking about is a great power that controls a region. For example, the Dao Sword Palace! "
At this point, Lai Huabei's eyes revealed a trace of yearning.
"You said that a province level sect controls a province. Could it be that the Tiandu Province is controlled by the Dao Sword Palace?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Because of the Tri-Layered Pass, the Tiandu Province is the exit to the Mother World. It is not allowed to be controlled by any sect. This Dao Sword Palace is in charge of the Seven Stars Province next to the Tiandu Province," Lai Huabei continued.
"Then, how do I join the Dao Sword Palace?" Luo Zheng asked.
Seeing Luo Zheng's interest in the Dao Sword Palace, Lai Huabei's eyes glinted slightly.
Lai Huabei was a Consummate True God. Although his abilities were slightly weaker, as someone from the Mother World, his vision was still very sharp. Luo Zheng was also a Consummate True God like him, but the True Meaning of Dao faintly transmitted to him showed that he had already entered the Sea of True Meaning.
He intended to rope in Luo Zheng. Naturally, it was also because he was interested in Luo Zheng's abilities.
"To be honest, my lifelong wish is to join the Dao Sword Palace. However, it is still too difficult for the people from the Tiandu Province to join the Dao Sword Palace. Every year, the Dao Sword Palace will give out a quota in the Tiandu Province. However, it is basically impossible for the quota to be allocated to the Heaven's Wall City. However, a month ago, I received a piece of news," Lai Huabei said with a serious face, "In the west of the Green Brocade Plain, there was a Tian Luo Guard from the Dao Sword Palace who died in one of the caves. If one were to obtain the Tian Luo Guard's tomb, he would be qualified to join the Dao Sword Palace."
"As you already know about this piece of news, you should have taken action?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Lai Huabei revealed a bitter smile, "Of course I did. Half a month ago, I invited a few other Consummate True Gods from the Heaven's Wall City to head over. I didn't expect to encounter quite a bit of trouble in the cave. It turned out that the Tian Luo Guard had turned into the evil spirit of the tomb and a Consummate True God had died in the hands of the evil spirit …"
"Since Brother Huabei mentioned this matter now, do you want me to make a trip there?" Luo Zheng asked in reply.
Luo Zheng naturally knew that Lai Huabei had a motive for looking for him, but he couldn't guess what it was about.
Lai Huabei nodded, "Although Brother Luo Zheng didn't display your abilities, I can see that you have already stepped into the Sea of Truth. Your Sun Soul should be far more valiant than a Consummate True God like me. You should be able to subdue the evil spirit of the tomb …"
As a Consummate True God, although Lai Huabei also possessed a Sun Soul, he hadn't stepped into the Sea of Truth. His Sun Soul hadn't been tested by the Sea of Truth. There was still a huge gap between his Sun Soul and Luo Zheng's.
After Luo Zheng pondered for a while, he nodded slightly, "If so, I can go with you."
Although this wasn't the Origin World, as long as he didn't encounter any Nirvana Realm experts, with Luo Zheng's current abilities, he should be able to protect himself.
Hearing this, Lai Huabei instantly revealed a joyful expression, "If so, I will gather a few more brothers and set off tomorrow!"
Early the next morning, Lai Huabei led Luo Zheng to the entrance of Heaven's Wall City.
Just as Lai Huabei had said, there were already three Consummate True Gods gathered at the entrance of the city. The three of them looked very young, but Luo Zheng could also see that their bone ages were basically two to five thousand years old.
These Consummate True Gods were the same as Lai Huabei, they hadn't stepped into the Sea of Truth.
After Lai Huabei made the introductions, he asked one of them, "Why hasn't Nomad arrived yet?"
One of them sighed and said, "The last time you brought Nomad there, Nomad has been thinking about it. I don't know what benefits he promised, but he revealed this information to some people at the port. I heard that a few people set off with Nomad yesterday!"
Hearing this, Lai Huabei's expression changed greatly, "He … How could Nomad do that!"
"The people he found were the guards of the caravans who were resting in Heaven's Wall City. Naturally, they weren't weak. Even if they really got the tomb of the Tian Luo Guard, I'm afraid it wouldn't fall into Nomad's hands," that person sighed.
"Then let's set off now!" Lai Huabei was also anxious.
Joining Dao Sword Palace was these people's dream. Otherwise, they would be limited to Consummate True God for their whole lives and wouldn't be able to make a name for themselves. They didn't even dare to think about stepping into the Nirvana Realm.
Luo Zheng didn't say much. Nomad should be one of Lai Huabei's good friends. It was very common for him to betray his friends for benefits.
However, he also understood that if there were other people involved, it wouldn't be so simple to explore the cave of the Tian Luo Guard.
Under Lai Huabei's lead, a group of five Consummate True Gods, including Luo Zheng, flew toward the west of Heaven's Wall City.
After flying for half a day in the boundless desert, a green grassland appeared in front of Luo Zheng.

The Obstructer
After entering the Green Brocade Plain, Lai Huabei had a gray compass in his hand.

Consciousness was another pair of eyes for them.

Even if one was at the True God Realm, as long as one was bitten by the Poison Chelating Army Ant, the poison would spread all over the body in one breath. If one was bitten by the Poison Chelating Army Ant, one's physical body would rot in the time it takes to burn a stick of incense, and one would die very miserably.
Therefore, someone once said that if one was bitten by the Poison Chelating Army Ant, one must cut off one's wrist the moment one was bitten. One breath later, one would die without a doubt. This showed how ferocious this poisonous ant was.

If you are sensible and leave obediently, I will pretend this never happened. If you take another step forward … the scenery in this karst cave is not bad, and it can be your burial place."
The threat in the young man's words was very strong, and his eyes were full of contempt.
"Nomad!"
Lai Huabei's eyes were full of resentment.
The few of them had similar cultivations in the Heaven's Wall City, and their relationship was also very good.
Lai Huabei had never thought that Nomad would lead outsiders here, and now he was going to block them outside. How could he not hate him?
Looking at Lai Huabei's expression, Luo Zheng sighed softly in his heart. The feeling of being betrayed by a friend was indeed unpleasant.
The young man waved his hand impatiently and said, "Leave, good-for-nothings. You are not qualified to touch the burial mound of the Tianluo Guards."
The other three Perfect True Gods already had the intention to leave. Although the burial mound of the Tianluo Guards was attractive, it was not worth it to lose their lives for it.
"Lai Huabei, why don't we just go back?" one of them said.
Lai Huabei's face also revealed a look of hesitation. The helpers that Nomad had invited were far more powerful than he had expected.
Although he had also invited Luo Zheng, a Perfect True God who had stepped into the Sea of True Meaning, he was only interested in Luo Zheng's Sun Soul to be able to subdue the evil spirit in the burial mound. As for Luo Zheng's true abilities, he did not know, and at the same time, did not expect much.
Lai Huabei felt helpless. In the end, he could only nod his head in grief and indignation and accept his friend's suggestion.

Chapter 2634
Crushing the Ants
Lai Huabei didn't know why Luo Zheng would ask this question, but he was well aware of the Poison Chelating Ant's ferocious reputation.
"The Poison Chelating Ant's strength lies not only in its ability to travel alone, but also in its incredible speed and resistance to fire and water. It's very difficult to kill it with ordinary means." Lai Huabei sighed, "Brother Luo Zheng, we'll have to trouble you to accompany us on this trip. It's not worthwhile for us to lose our lives here."
Luo Zheng continued to ask, "If these Poison Chelating Ant besiege us, I wonder if Brother Huabei and the others will be able to fend them off?"
Lai Huabei glanced at Luo Zheng in astonishment, replying, "Although it's difficult for us to avoid these Poison Chelating Ant, we can still keep them outside … Brother Luo Zheng, why do you ask this question? Could it be … "
Luo Zheng's eyes had already turned to the young man not far away.
The young man continued to lean against the cave wall as he waited for the group to leave. Naturally, he heard Luo Zheng's words and a cold smile appeared on his face. "If you want to die, just ask me. I can send you off …"
As soon as the young man finished speaking, Luo Zheng's figure leapt up and flew towards him.
Seeing that Luo Zheng really dared to rush over, the young man's sneer deepened. He whistled softly and the Poison Chelating Ant hiding on the cave wall fell down like raindrops, drilling into Luo Zheng's body.
"Brother Luo Zheng!"
Seeing this, Lai Huabei was shocked and inwardly cursed Luo Zheng for being too impulsive and stupid.
Even if he really wanted to fight, he should've rushed over and restrained the young man controlling the Poison Chelating Ant. If he flew over so slowly, he would've been bitten by the Poison Chelating Ant in an instant. Wasn't this courting death?
Lai Huabei naturally didn't understand Luo Zheng's intentions.
Luo Zheng was afraid that they wouldn't be able to resist the Poison Chelating Ant. If he swaggered over, he would naturally attract most of the Poison Chelating Ant to himself.
As for the Poison Chelating Ant's strange poison? This was not something Luo Zheng was worried about.
"Da da da …"
The Poison Chelating Army Ants landed on Luo Zheng's body and pierced their jaws into Luo Zheng's skin, injecting their toxins into him.
The moment the Poison Chelating Army Ant's poison was injected into Luo Zheng's body, a pale green aura appeared on the surface of Luo Zheng's body. In the blink of an eye, this aura covered his entire body!
"These Poisonous Chelating Army Ants can pierce through my skin. The poison is indeed transmitted very quickly …" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered.
However, no matter how poisonous the Chelating Poison Ants were, they could not cause any harm to Luo Zheng.
While flying, Luo Zheng's body suddenly shook. If it had been an ordinary ant, this shake would have been enough to shatter it into dust, but this Poison Chelating Army Ant's small body was actually very sturdy. Luo Zheng only managed to shake off the ant that was clinging to his body, not shattering it.
The young man was still leaning against the cave wall, looking at Luo Zheng as if he was looking at a dead man. "I've seen stupid people, but I've never seen such a stupid person. How could he court death like this …"
This young man was good at controlling all kinds of strange insects. In addition, he cultivated the True Meaning of Nature, which made it difficult for most True Gods to deal with him. Most of the time, he could kill people without leaving a trace.
This time, he was only ordered to scare off these few good-for-nothings. He never expected that there would be such an idiot among these good-for-nothings, who insisted on rushing to his death.
In his eyes, Luo Zheng's body would be paralyzed and fall to the ground within one or two breaths. Then, his whole body would fester and he would die. He had seen this kind of death too many times.
"Phew …"
Even though Luo Zheng deliberately suppressed his speed, he was able to cross the distance of about three hundred meters in an instant.

Divine Gems were used to feed the Nirvana Realm Token. Luo Zheng knew this when he was in the Divine Realm. For True Gods who didn't have Nirvana Realm Tokens, Divine Gems could only be used as a kind of currency for transactions.
