Apart from the Qingfeng Longsword, there was also the set of green clothes, boots, and hat among Jiang Bolan's belongings. Then, there was the octagonal copper piece.
He had not asked Luo Nian to identify the copper piece over the past few days. It looked like he had to hurry.
Although he did not know what the map in the copper piece meant, it was definitely not an ordinary item left behind by the Palace Master of a superpower.
The pale-faced woman put away the Qingfeng Longsword. She stared at Lai Huabei and said weakly, "The Path Sword Palace … does have a rule … If you can find the cenotaph … you can enter the Path Sword Palace … Five-star Heavenly Net Guard will give you five spots … The owner of this sword … will give you ten spots."
Although she spoke intermittently, Lai Huabei heard her clearly.
However, Lai Huabei was slightly disappointed. He only needed one spot. Even with Luo Zheng and his three good friends, there were only five spots. What was the use of the extra five spots?
"In addition … 800 years ago … the reward for the Palace Master … will be given to you …" she continued.
As soon as she said this, Lai Huabei felt as if his heart had been pounded by a heavy hammer. His heart thumped non-stop. He stammered, "May, may I ask … what is the reward?"
The woman's expression was calm as she replied, "About … 50 times the wealth of the Heaven's Wall City …"
The Qi Family guards in the air, who did not know whether to leave or stay, all sucked in a breath of cold air. The Sky Wall City Lord was also dumbfounded.
The Heaven's Wall City was indeed a small city in the Sky Capital State. However, because it was the closest city to the Tri-Layer Pass and the existence of the East Port, the flow of people and trade here was not small. The wealth accumulated by such a large population was definitely not a small amount.
50 times the wealth of the Heaven's Wall City. Just thinking about it made him feel dizzy. Lai Huabei was afraid that he would faint.
The pale-faced woman suddenly focused her gaze on Luo Zheng. A strange look flashed across her sad eyes as she asked, "You … don't seem … to have any psychological fluctuations?"
Luo Zheng naturally did not have any psychological fluctuations. His expression was very calm.
After all, the Dao Sword Palace was a superpower. The reward for the Palace Master was definitely not low. However, the divine crystals in Luo Zheng's hands were the inheritance of the entire Jiuli Tribe. Although he had only taken half of it, it was still an astronomical figure. Therefore, he was not too shocked when he heard her words.
Luo Zheng was considering another problem. Lai Huabei's strength was not enough, and his talent was far from enough to be valued by the Dao Sword Palace. Obtaining such a large amount of wealth was definitely not a good thing for him. Instead, it was a disaster.

Chapter 2644
A Qualified Person
Luo Zheng hadn't expected this woman to pay attention to him.
After all, it was Lai Huabei who had presented her with the Azure Edge Sword, not Luo Zheng.
He really shouldn't have revealed such an expression …
After some thought, Luo Zheng immediately revealed a worried expression and said, "Such a generous reward is indeed astonishing. I … I'm just worried that Brother Huabei might not be able to bear it. As the saying goes, an ordinary man's wealth is his own ruin."
The pale-faced woman looked at Luo Zheng and said thoughtfully, "So you're worried about this problem … It's not hard to solve … just give him a portion of it … but you're right. If the entire reward is given to Lai Huabei … I'm afraid many people in the Dao Sword Palace will become restless."
Lai Huabei's expression became nervous. He looked at the woman and asked, "Then what should I do?"
The pale-faced woman calmly glanced at Lai Huabei and then looked away. At the same time, she said, "What you do … has nothing to do with me … if you can find ten people, I'll take you on a journey … I'll only wait for one day."
After saying this, her figure gently floated away.
The three azure-clothed men from the Dao Sword Palace followed her. The Lord of Heaven's Wall City was afraid of neglecting them and also followed closely behind.
Only Luo Zheng, Lai Huabei, and the Qi Clan guards remained.
The Qi Clan guards didn't dare to have any thoughts of revenge. After confirming that the woman wouldn't cause trouble for them, they all fled as fast as they could.
Lai Huabei's expression was still nervous. The more he thought about Luo Zheng's words, the more reasonable they made sense. He asked, "Brother Luo Zheng, what … what do you think I should do?"
Luo Zheng smiled and comforted him, "Brother Huabei, there's no need to be too anxious. Although the Dao Sword Palace's reward is enormous, you can just refuse it."
"Refuse?" Lai Huabei was slightly taken aback.
This sum of wealth was enough to make the Lai Clan a great power in the entire Tiandu Province. Not everyone could casually refuse such a reward.
Lai Huabei's face revealed hesitation and even a trace of pain.
"After all, if you obtain this cenotaph, won't you achieve your goal of joining the Dao Sword Palace? If you are obsessed with this large amount of wealth, you will only bring more trouble to yourself, and even bring about the disaster of death, "Luo Zheng continued.
Of course, he was doing this for Lai Huabei's sake, but that was all he could say. As for what choice Lai Huabei would make, Luo Zheng would not interfere too much.
Humans would die in pursuit of wealth, and birds would die in pursuit of food. Even true gods were no exception. It was just that the wealth here had become "God Crystals" instead of the usual gold and silver. Not everyone could resist the temptation of such a huge amount of wealth.
Apart from this wealth, Lai Huabei also had 10 spots to enter the Dao Sword Palace.
All the large and small clans in the Heaven's Wall City had also heard the news. On the same day, dozens of Patriarchs had already paid a visit to Lai Huabei, hoping to buy a slot from him at a high price.
Even the city lord of the Heaven's Wall city returned after making arrangements for the four people from the Dao Sword Palace. He hoped to obtain one or two spots from Lai Huabei.
Lai Huabei did not forget about his good friends. When the three of them who had gone into the cave with Luo Zheng that day heard this news, they were all wild with joy. Although they had not done much, they had still risked their lives. This was the best reward for them.
Luo Zheng did not need this spot, but Lai Huabei did his best to give it to him. Just as Luo Zheng was trying to refuse, the city lord of the Heaven's Wall city said, "Friend Luo Zheng, the Autumn College has asked me to pass a message to you."
"What?" Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback. He did not know why the woman would ask the city lord to pass a message to him.
"The Autumn College has said that if you go, she will give you a special spot. Also … if you stay in the Tiandu province, the Qi family will definitely seek revenge. If the Qi family sends people to kill you, it will probably bring about a lot of trouble," the city lord said.
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. Although these words were vague, there was clearly a hint of coercion in them.
But why did the woman want him to join the Dao Sword Palace?
If Luo Zheng really wanted to join a superpower, he had better choices. Even though Mount Li was very far away from here, if Luo Zheng set his mind on going, he would be able to reach Mount Li one day after spending a lot of time. There, he would not only be able to receive Ming Wei's protection, but he would also be able to be with Luo Yan.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to refuse, 9527 suddenly said, "Luo Zheng, agree."
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked.
Chi You and 9527 had told him something. Luo Zheng had repeatedly asked 9527, but 9527 had refused to tell him. As a result, Luo Zheng did not know what to do now. Now, he suddenly wanted him to join the Dao Sword Palace.
"Listen to me," 9527 continued.
"You still haven't told me the reason!" Luo Zheng was a little dissatisfied.
"But that's what Chi You told me. So I'll help you plan your future path." 9527 laughed.
"What do you think I am?" Luo Zheng's face darkened.
"A pawn," 9527 joked.
"Why don't you go and die …"
Although Luo Zheng did not know 9527's real plan, he still reluctantly agreed.
Convincing Luo Zheng was originally Qiu Xuegong's task. Seeing that Luo Zheng had changed his mind and agreed, the Heaven's Wall City Lord finally breathed a sigh of relief.
The Heaven's Wall City Lord also knew what Qiu Xuegong had his eyes on. It was most likely the Dao Fusion Theurgy that Luo Zheng had previously displayed. For someone who could fuse so many Divine Daos, even if he had not reached the Nirvana Realm, his talent was terrifying.
Of course, a true god's lifespan was unlimited. If Luo Zheng's bone age was tens of millions of years, or even hundreds of millions of years, it would not be a big deal if he cultivated hundreds or even thousands of Divine Daos. However, this kid's bone age was not even 200 years old …
Since Luo Zheng had been specially approved by that woman, Lai Huabei only had six spots left.
Of these six spots, two were allocated to the Lai Family's disciples, two were given to the Heaven's Wall City Lord, and the remaining two were allocated to the two Distinguished Families of Heaven's Wall City. Everyone was happy. The Lai Family was not considered a big family in Heaven's Wall City. In the future, they would probably rise because of Lai Huabei.
The next day, Heaven's Wall City, East Port.
The Heaven's Wall City Lord led many people to send them off.
The pale-faced woman held the Green Edge Longsword in her hand. There was a lingering sadness in her eyes that could not be wiped away.
Actually, she did not reveal all the information. The owner of this Green Edge Longsword was her father.
A few hundred years had passed, and the pain in her heart had already calmed down. However, her father's death involved many aspects. Returning to the Dao Sword Palace this time would probably cause quite a lot of trouble …
As she thought, her gaze swept down the spacious flying boat and finally landed on Luo Zheng.

Chapter 2645
Kingdom of Arabian Demonic Flowers
When Lai Huabei and his group stepped onto the spacious flying boat, they were all very excited.
To them, this was a brand new life.
The Dao Sword Palace didn't give them much time to prepare. After they received the news, they hurriedly bade farewell to their families.
The azure flying ship of the Dao Sword Palace was exquisitely decorated. The wood used to build the ship had natural patterns on it. It should be an extremely rare type of wood. The natural patterns had the effect of calming one's mind. Everything on the flying ship was exquisite and comfortable.
After the ten Consummate True Gods boarded the flying boat, most of them were somewhat restrained. Lai Huabei and his group even tiptoed.
Only Luo Zheng was very calm.
After all, the divine territory itself was a flying boat. He had personally piloted such a huge flying boat before. Other flying boats seemed small in comparison. Moreover, the flying boat of the Dao Sword Palace couldn't be compared to the Lingxiao Flying Boat.
After everyone boarded the green flying boat, one of the men in green robes shouted, "Let's go!"
The green flying boat trembled slightly and silently left the east harbor.
The huge Heaven's Wall City quickly left. Soon, everyone ascended into the sky and flew forward at high speed.
Luo Zheng stood on the side of the boat and looked down. Even though he was high up in the sky, he could still see a patch of yellow.
"After the Heaven's Wall City, it's still a desert?" Luo Zheng's eyes focused slightly.
There were sandstorms floating above the desert. Luo Zheng also knew that these sandstorms were caused by the Arabian Demonic Flowers.
If the Heaven's Wall City was still surrounded by a vast desert, it would be bad for the Evil Deities.
A few days ago, after he released the Evil Deities, the Evil Deities didn't choose to enter the Heaven's Wall City. Instead, they left along the river. However, from a bird's eye view, the river stretched for hundreds of miles before finally merging into the desert …
However, from the looks of it, the Evil Deities shouldn't be in any danger. After all, once the Evil Deities died, they would be reborn in the "Spirit Lodging Land" in Luo Zheng's body.
"You … seem to be … very interested in the Tiandu Region?"
At this time, a weak voice came from behind Luo Zheng.
The pale-faced woman slowly walked beside him, as if she had no strength. She leaned her upper body against the side of the ship and looked down.
"Where does this desert end?" Luo Zheng asked.
The pale-faced woman blinked her eyes. She stretched out her jade-like hand and gently waved it. Then she said, "The entire Tiandu Province … is a desert … This is the Drought Desert … Over there is the Setting Sun Desert … Over there …"
"The Tiandu Province's environment is so bad?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
The woman revealed a faint smile. "Otherwise … Otherwise, who would give up on this state … Just because this place … only has sand …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng fell into deep thought.
Lai Huabei explained to Luo Zheng that the Tiandu Province was not under the control of any major powers because of the Three Stacks Pass, and other major sects were not allowed to occupy this province. Luo Zheng also had some doubts in his heart, but now it seemed that this woman's explanation was more credible.
Because the Tiandu State was too barren, the other major powers did not even put it in their eyes.
"You aren't … someone from the Tiandu State, right?" The pale woman asked again.
This question had finally come …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. Of course, he had expected that the Dao Sword Palace would interrogate him about his identity. He had also prepared a set of excuses.
"I am indeed not." Luo Zheng nodded.
He waited for the woman to continue her interrogation. Unexpectedly, this topic stopped here, and Luo Zheng's excuses could not be used.
On the airship, Lai Huabei and the others sat obediently to one side, while the three blue-clothed men stood at the bow of the airship, controlling it. Other than the sound of the wind produced by the high-speed flight, the airship was extremely quiet for a while.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed as he looked down. "That's …"
In the desert below, an unprecedented storm had arisen.
The area covered by this enormous sandstorm far exceeded Luo Zheng's imagination, and in the center of this sandstorm, there was actually a vague outline of a vast city.
"The city of the Sand Nation," she said indifferently as she looked down.
Luo Zheng thought of the Sand Nation in the Red Sun Desert and said, "These Sand Nation people actually have their own country?"
"Country?" The woman lightly shook her head. "That's not the country of the Sand Nation … that's the country of the Arabian demonic flower … Look."
Luo Zheng looked in the direction she was pointing. Even from high up in the sky, Luo Zheng could see the enormous Arabian demonic flowers standing in the middle of the city. These Arabian demonic flowers were at least a thousand times larger than the ones Luo Zheng had seen before!
Each Arabian demonic flower was like a giant monster crawling on the ground, its seven heads looking in every direction.
This time, Luo Zheng was truly stunned. His mouth slightly opened as he asked, "How can these Arabian demonic flowers be so large …"
"Large … look again …" The pale-faced woman had him look to the other side.
Luo Zheng's pupils instantly constricted.
Tens of thousands of li in front of the airship was an even larger Arabian demonic flower.
This Arabian demonic flower was probably more than ten thousand li tall, and its enormous body was curled up on the ground. If it were to spread out, it would probably be more than a hundred thousand li long!
"That's the king of the Arabian demonic flower … it's reached the fifteenth Heaven of the Paramita Realm … the entire Sand Nation … is ruled by it … or perhaps it's the ruler of the entire Tiandu Province …" she indifferently said.
"A plant can reach the Paramita Realm and grow to such a level!" Luo Zheng was somewhat dazed.
The woman faintly smiled. "In this world … there are many more bizarre things …"
"But with so many Arabian demonic flowers, what do they feed on?" Luo Zheng asked another question.
Since the Arabian demonic flowers had established a massive country, their numbers were naturally enormous. Relying on the Sand Nation to lure in outsiders was simply impossible to fill the bellies of these Arabian demonic flowers.
"Their food … is the Sand Nation people themselves …" she indifferently said.
"They all feed on the Sand Nation people? Those Sand Nation people won't flee? " Luo Zheng lightly clenched his fists. He was no savior of the world, and he had even killed several Sand Nation people who had deceived him, but the Sand Nation people were still humans like him.
The woman indifferently looked at Luo Zheng and then said, "You really aren't … a person of the mother world!"
In Luo Zheng's subconscious mind, the human race should occupy the dominant position. The other beasts and monsters could be subordinate to the human race, but they could not be above the human race. Whether it was in the universe or in the Divine Realm, this was common knowledge.

Chapter 2646
Edited by: Michyrr
The Unknown Flame
Even Luo Zheng had heard of the Rat Alliance and the Arabian Demonic Flower after entering the Triple-Layer Pass.
But the concept of putting people first was deeply rooted in Luo Zheng's mind, and he found it very difficult to adapt.
Although the Dao Sword Palace had no interest in occupying the Tiandu Province, they had sent people to exterminate the Arabian Demonic Flowers in the Tiandu Province.
Although the Arabian Demonic Flowers could not move, they had incredible reproductive abilities. Each Arabian Demonic Flower could scatter millions of seeds, allowing them to rapidly grow even in the cruel environment of the desert.
Each time the Dao Sword Palace exterminated a Arabian Demonic Flower, it would only take a few years for it to grow again.
Thirty thousand years ago, when the Dao Sword Palace was too preoccupied with its own problems to pay attention to the Tiandu Province, an Arabian demonic flower in the Tiandu Province had silently stepped into the Paramita Realm and continued to grow. In the end, it fused with a Paramita token from the Fifteenth Heaven and became the king of the Arabian demonic flowers. From then on, it became even more difficult to exterminate them.
Moreover, the Arabian Demonic Flowers were very intelligent, and they used a unique system to raise the Sand People.
These Sand Nation people worshipped the Arabian Demonic Flower as a god. A portion of the Sand Nation people were able to obtain benefits from the Arabian Demonic Flower, but the majority of the Sand Nation people, in their ignorance, sacrificed themselves to the Arabian Demonic Flower.
To this portion of the Sand People, sacrificing themselves to the Arabian Demonic Flowers was a supreme honor.
When the Dao Sword Palace tried to stop the Arabian Demonic Flowers, it even provoked the hatred and attacks of these Sand People. In the end, the Dao Sword Palace could only allow the Arabian Demonic Flowers to grow in the desert.
"It's probably … taking my good intentions as ill intent …" She said softly, and a little bit of color returned to her pale face. This little bit of color actually dispelled her lifeless aura, making her look radiant.
The woman did not force Luo Zheng to speak of her background. Instead, she focused on explaining the situation in the Tiandu Province to Luo Zheng.
After hearing her intermittent explanation, Luo Zheng sighed. It seemed that the mother world was much more complicated than he had imagined …
Luo Zheng looked ahead, a hint of concern in his eyes. "But won't that Arabian demonic flower king attack us?"
The thick roots of the Arabian demonic flower stretched for one hundred thousand li, and if they were raised high enough, they could tear the airship to shreds.
"It's strong enough to dominate this place, but it doesn't dare to offend the flying ships that pass by … If it really offends our Dao Sword Palace and those merchant groups with powerful backgrounds, it will only bring disaster upon itself … Don't think that it's stupid," she said with a smile.
Perhaps it was due to the excitement of talking, her originally intermittent voice became much more coherent.
The three azure-robed men at the front of the flying boat quietly watched this side, muttering in their hearts.
From their point of view, Luo Zheng wasn't that special. It was just that he had cultivated more Shinto. But what did this mean? In this world, only crossing the Nirvana Realm was the real starting point.
They didn't know that the normally taciturn Qiu Xuegong would be chatting so much with this brat …
Not long after, the flying boat finally passed by the massive Arabian demonic flower king. Spatial ripples began to spread out from the boat, enveloping it before it vanished into the void.
Even though the Tiandu Province was a small province, it was still astonishingly large. If one wanted to save time, they would need to use greater teleportation.
… …
… …
The river in front of Heaven's Wall City was called the Clear River. The water of the Clear River was sweet and clear, nourishing many of the divine people around Heaven's Wall City.
At the end of the Clear River was the Hanhe Desert.
Evil Deities lightly circled around the edge of a sand dune. His eyes narrowed as he allowed the scalding sand to slap against his face.
"Friend … Friend … Can you take me to Heaven's Wall City?" A man's voice rang out in Evil Deities' ears.
This man appeared extremely weak, panting after saying a few words. His lips were extremely dry and cracked. It was clear that he was exhausted to the extreme.
Evil Deities stood on top of the sand dune and examined the man, an indescribable emotion in his eyes.
"Friend …" The man struggled to crawl across the sand dune until he finally reached the foot of Evil Deities. "Save me … I don't have any strength left …"
Evil Deities examined the man at his feet.
A ruthless smile appeared on his handsome face. "Interesting. So the humans of the outside world are no different. They can also deceive people."
"Wha, what do you mean …" the man weakly asked.
"Your methods are too clumsy!"
Evil Deities couldn't be bothered to explain how he had seen through the man's flaws. His lips slightly cracked as a ruthless aura erupted from his body.
He slowly squatted down and then grabbed the man by the neck and lifted him up.
When the man saw Evil Deities' ruthless face, he finally panicked. He was extremely weak just a moment ago, but now he desperately screamed, "Save me, this guy wants to kill me …"
But halfway through his scream, he felt an extremely hot aura surging from Evil Deities' hands. In the blink of an eye, he lost consciousness.
That was Evil Deities's supernatural ability — Unknown Fire. This flame only had temperature but no form. Even if it burned in the dark, it wouldn't release any light. Its power might be average, but it was impossible to guard against!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Just as the man cried for help, dozens of bulges appeared below the sand dune. It seemed like something was quickly drilling underneath.
When Evil Deities saw this, the corners of his mouth curled up again, revealing a devilish smile. Then, he raised the man in his hands and slammed him onto the ground.
"Pop!"
A strange scene appeared. The man in his hands quickly shriveled up!
The Unknown Fire released by Evil Deities just now had burned the man's blood, flesh, bones, and brain clean. His skin was filled with the Unknown Fire.
When he slammed the ground, the Unknown Fire in the man's body rushed into the ground and surged in all directions at the bottom of the desert.
"Ah ah!"
"What kind of fire is this …"
"It's burning!"
Below those bulges, there were Sand Nation people!
This group of Sand Nation people's cultivation wasn't low. Most of them were Upper True Gods. Because they had the advantage in numbers, they not only knew how to deceive, but also directly attack.
However, they were unlucky to have encountered Evil Deities this time.
These Sand Nation people crawled out from the ground and rolled crazily on the ground. Their skin and flesh were burned by the Unknown Fire. Their bodies were charred, but there was no trace of the fire.
In the blink of an eye, these Sand Nation people's lives were taken by the Unknown Fire.
Among the charred corpses, there was one person who was still alive. Evil Deities slowly walked towards this person.
In order to understand this world as quickly as possible, the fastest way was to directly search for souls.
In fact, beside Clear River, Evil Deities had a conflict with a God's citizen because of a small conflict. They directly slaughtered the God's citizen's village and obtained many memories of this world.

Chapter 2647
Edited by: Michyrr
Sword Congealing Palace
After the Evil Deities searched through this person's memories, he was taken aback.
"What the hell is this person's head filled with!?" A hint of indignation appeared on his face.
He didn't have any sort of good or evil, and his conduct was unreasonable and ruthless.
But there was one thing that made him the same as everyone else, and that was that he was a human. He had probably still been influenced by Luo Zheng.
In Luo Zheng's inner world, other races had risen to prominence, such as the Celestial Spirit Race. But with Luo Zheng's deliberate suppression and the rise of the Human race, the Celestial Spirit Race had slowly been marginalized. And the Evil Deities had fought on behalf of the Human race, but had never killed a Celestial Spirit expert.
In any case, the Evil Deities was extremely repulsed by other races …
From his memories, he had discovered the kingdom of the Sand People, and he also knew that these Sand People had submitted to the Arabian Demonic Flower. Every year, they would produce large numbers of Sand People for the Arabian Demonic Flower to eat!
"How could a perfectly good Human race submit to a few plants? Hmph! In the future, when I'm strong enough, I'll flatten these man-eating flowers one by one! "the Evil Deities muttered to himself.
As he continued to search through his memories, he was taken aback.
"True Dao Intent? Paramita? "
When the Evil Deities left Luo Zheng's inner world, he had sensed a special energy from Luo Zheng's body that the Evil Deities had never sensed before.
But the Evil Deities had been focused on leaving Luo Zheng, so he naturally hadn't tried to find out where that energy came from.
Now, he finally understood that Perfected True God was not the end, nor was fusing Dao. If he wanted to get stronger, he had to throw himself into the Paramita.
As an extremely ambitious person, this discovery was enough to make him tremble with joy.
He laughed dryly for a long time as if he had gone mad. Then, he threw down the Sand Nation person in his hand and walked slowly towards the yellow sand. The Sand Nation person's soul had been shattered into pieces by his soul-searching technique. He had become a living dead.
The Evil Deities had found a way to leave the sandstorm, but he had no plans to venture deep into the desert.
In the depths of the desert, the powerful Arabian Demonic Flowers of the Sand Kingdom lurked.
Although the Evil Deities was proud, he did not believe that he was a match for the Arabian Demonic Flower. Continuing to venture deeper into the desert would be suicidal.
But in his memories, the Evil Deities had found a different way. He only needed to follow the central line by surprise by surprise. There was another port there, by this port, the Demonic Flower.
… …
… …
Three days later …
The cyan flying boat had already flown to the edge of the Tiandu Region, and the huge boundary wall was like a layer of green and blue glass in front of the flying boat.
"The space laws in this realm wall are actually so deep and stable." Luo Zheng's mouth opened slightly as he looked at it, revealing a look of surprise.
There were boundary walls in the Great Thousand World, and there were boundary walls between the universes. However, there were no boundary walls between the major domains in the Divine Domain. However, there was an extremely sturdy spatial barrier in the outer layer of the Divine Domain. The spatial barrier was so thick that even Ming Wei and the others couldn't break it with all their might.
But this Demonic Deities Note was clearly far more stable than the Demonic Demonic Demonic Realm's outer layer. How would the Sword Palace pass through?
Luo Zheng quickly realized that his worries were unnecessary …
The azure-robed man on the Demonic Flower lightly jumped onto the Demonic Deities Note and thrust a palm at the Demonic Deities Note.
"Bo!"
A ripple appeared on the surface of the Demonic Deities Note, and then a complex golden Sanskrit character appeared.
When Luo Zheng saw this golden Sanskrit character, he raised an eyebrow. Although the golden Sanskrit characters were mostly the same, some were simpler while others were abnormally complex. The golden Sanskrit character before him was actually rather similar to the golden Sanskrit characters around Luo Zheng's inner world.
Alas, the golden Sanskrit character flashed by so quickly that Luo Zheng had no time to record it.
The azure-robed man returned to the Demonic Flower and discussed with the other two. A strange ripple appeared on the surface of the Demonic Flower, and a large formation unfurled in front of the Demonic Flower like an umbrella.
Under the flowing light of this large formation, a huge Sanskrit character was outlined. This Sanskrit character faintly matched the golden Sanskrit characters on the Demonic Deities Note.
When the front of the Demonic Flower touched the Demonic Deities Note, there was no obstruction at all. The entire Demonic Flower entered the thick Demonic Flower!
Even Lai Huabei and the others raised their heads and looked around curiously. Although they knew of the Demonic Flower's existence, this was the first time in their lives that they had crossed a large state.
"After the large formation in front of the Demonic Flower unfurled, the Demonic Flower was actually recognized by the Demonic Flower." Luo Zheng clicked his tongue in wonder.
The Demonic Flower's Demonic Flower was about three thousand feet thick. The Demonic Flower slowly traveled for five minutes before it was able to pass through. After coming out from the other side of the Demonic Flower, a gust of fresh air rushed towards them.
The world in front of them was green and full of vitality. This was the Seven Stars State where the Dao Sword Palace was located.
Phew …
As the Demonic Flower accelerated again, the surrounding scenery became blurry again.
Two hours later, the Demonic Flower stopped again. Lai Huabei and the other residents of Heaven's Wall City all stood up and leaned against the ship's railing to look down.
"This is the legendary Dao Sword Palace!"
"Towering Sword! That's a Towering Sword! "
"Facing the Towering Sword, comprehending the True Intent of Dao can be done with half the effort!"
When Lai Huabei and the others saw the landmark of the Dao Sword Palace, they were all very excited.
Below the Demonic Flower stood a huge mountain city. Densely packed houses and palaces were built along the mountain. In the center of the mountain city stood a giant sword that was seventy thousand feet long!
Even from such a great distance, Luo Zheng could feel the intense True Intent of Dao from the stone sword.
"I'm afraid that only Desolate Gods can wield such a giant sword. Didn't they say that the Desolate Gods in the Mother World have already gone extinct? How was this Towering Sword forged in the Dao Sword Palace? "Luo Zheng muttered in his heart.
As the Demonic Flower continued to descend, the Towering Sword also disappeared from their sight. Finally, the Demonic Flower stopped on a platform in the middle of the mountain city.
The pale-faced woman jumped up and stood on the ship's railing. She turned her head and looked at Lai Huabei and the others. Finally, her gaze fell on Luo Zheng. Her eyes flashed with a thoughtful light. She then said, "Yu Wenhua … Let's settle them in the Sword Congealing Palace …"
As her voice fell, her body trembled slightly. She turned into a streak of light and entered the mountain city.
"Come with me," the man called Yu Wenhua said calmly.
Under Yu Wenhua's lead, everyone walked along the mountain city's densely packed buildings and finally arrived at the Sword Congealing Palace.
When they arrived at the entrance of the Sword Congealing Palace, two men in green slowly walked over and bowed to Yu Wenhua.
"Because these eleven people unearthed the Cenotaph, the Autumn Academy made an exception and allowed them to enter the Sword Congealing Palace," Yu Wenhua explained.
The two men in green nodded and led Lai Huabei and the others into the Sword Congealing Palace.

Chapter 2648
Little Fiends
A man in azure robes sat in a corner of the Sword Condensation Palace.
The man was burly and dark-skinned. He had a frivolous smile on his face, and his face was covered in stubble. His clothes were covered in stains, making him look extremely sloppy.
Lai Huabei and the others had been thinking about their days in the Dao Sword Palace.
The burly man's words were like a bucket of cold water that had been poured over their heads. Luo Zheng was still fine, but Lai Huabei and the others immediately became extremely ugly.
"C- palace master Chu, how can you say that?" An azure-robed man felt humiliated as well.
The burly man smiled faintly. He crawled to his feet and slowly walked towards Lai Huabei and the others. As he walked, he said, "The Dao Sword Palace has thirteen academies under it. The Sword Burial Palace, the Demon Sword Palace, and the Broken Sword Palace are all first-rate academies. The Fury Sword Palace, the Upper Sword Palace … these are all second-rate academies. The Sword Condensation Palace, ranked last, was created to accept people like you who joined the Dao Sword Palace through strange methods. Staying in the Sword Condensation Palace will only be a waste of your lives. Are you sure you want to join?"
Lai Huabei was the first to lose his temper when he heard that.
The burly man's words were like an invisible hand, directly crushing his dreams.
"It's said that you can become a disciple of the Dao Sword Palace as long as you obtain the tombs. It looks like the Dao Sword Palace doesn't plan on fulfilling that promise?" Lai Huabei questioned.
The burly man smiled gently. "There's naturally no reason for the Dao Sword Palace to not fulfill its promise. If you're willing to stay in the Sword Condensation Palace, I'll naturally welcome you …"
"But what do you mean by what you just said?" Lai Huabei asked furiously.
The burly man shrugged. "I'm just telling the truth."
The Dao Sword Palace was a huge organization in the Seven Star province, so it was naturally intertwined with extremely complicated relationships.
Many large clans in the Seven Star province hoped to squeeze their own people into the Dao Sword Palace. Due to various considerations, the Dao Sword Palace was unable to refuse these clans.
Originally, the Dao Sword Palace had twelve Academia Palaces, but an exception was made to add the Sword Condensation Palace behind it, making it thirteen Academia Palaces. Those hedonistic disciples who could not refuse but were lacking in talent were naturally shoved into the Sword Condensation Palace.
Those who entered the Dao Sword Palace through the tombs would also join the Sword Condensation Palace.
It was just as the burly man had said. The Dao Sword Palace was a place for trash.
Lai Huabei and the others were furious when they found out the truth.
Luo Zheng's expression remained the same.
Back in Heaven's Wall City, Luo Zheng had been very suspicious of the rules of the Dao Sword Palace. It was just that he did not want to throw cold water on Huabei.
No matter which major force it was, they needed to unearth the most outstanding disciples. If they recruited ordinary disciples, it would only be a waste of resources. This was the case in the God's Domain, and it was the same in the Mother World.
"Now, do you still want to join us?" A treacherous smile appeared on the burly man's face.
Lai Huabei clenched his fists and furrowed his brows. He gritted his teeth and said, "I'm in!"
The other people in the Heaven's Wall City also knew that this opportunity didn't come easily. If they were to return to the Heaven's Wall City now, it would be a huge humiliation.
"I'll join too!"
"I'm in!"
"I'm willing to join …"
The others also followed suit and said.
The burly man looked at the sincere expressions of these people. His eyes revealed a trace of pity. "Alright, since that's the case, go to the backyard after you register. I hope that those little devils will stop for a while …"
Just as everyone was preparing to register with the two green robed men, the burly man said, "Remember, my name is Qiu Shengshui. I am the Palace Lord of the Sword Congealing Palace. If you encounter any trouble, it's best not to trouble me. Try to solve it yourself."
After speaking, the Palace Lord left without looking back.
Hearing these words, everyone was speechless. After registering with a nervous heart, they each received a nameplate. Perhaps it was because the Sword Congealing Palace wasn't valued, but the entire process was extremely simple.
The two green robed men brought everyone to the backyard and told them that this was the residence of the Sword Congealing Palace. They then left without looking back, as though they were afraid of something.
Although the Sword Congealing Palace wasn't valued, it was still built on the scale of a school. The backyard of the palace was extremely spacious.
Just as Lai Huabei and the others were still standing there in a daze, a soft whistle suddenly sounded from the backyard. From each corner of the backyard, twenty to thirty green robed youths slowly walked out.
Looking at the unfriendly faces of these youths, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
"Hehe, it's only been half a month and there are people who don't know what's good for them to join the Sword Congealing Palace. It seems like you guys aren't from Seven Stars City." The leader, a long haired youth, lightly laughed. His voice carried a trace of contempt.
"The people from Seven Stars City aren't that stupid. Our Sword Congealing Palace is notorious. Even if you beat them to death, they wouldn't dare to come." Another silver haired youth had a strange smile on his face.
Lai Huabei stood at the front and stared at the leader, trembling as he asked, "You … What do you mean?"
"Nothing. It's just that the Sword Congealing Palace has its own rules. The first step to joining the Sword Congealing Palace is to let you know the rules …"
Just as the long haired youth was still speaking, Luo Zheng had already turned around and walked out.
"Brother Luo Zheng, where are you going?" Lai Huabei anxiously asked.
Lai Huabei seemed to be the backbone of this group, but when he met with trouble, the first thing he would look to was Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not pay any attention to Lai Huabei. He jogged quickly and caught up with the two men in green.
When the two green robed youths saw Luo Zheng chasing after them, they asked with a strange expression, "Friend, do you need something?"
Luo Zheng asked with a smile, "I want to ask, what are the rules of the Sword Congealing Palace? If a disciple of the palace fights, what is the punishment? "
The two green robed youths looked at each other. They obviously knew what was going to happen in the backyard, which was why they left in such a hurry. Although the little demons' talents were ordinary, each one of them had a strong background. They were also the main culprits of the Sword Congealing Palace's notoriety.
"Our Sword Congealing Palace doesn't have many rules. But when the disciples spar, it's fine as long as no one dies." The green robed youths replied.
Almost all of the disciples who joined the Sword Congealing Palace had to go through the baptism of the little demons. Only a small number of people were able to persevere. The majority chose to leave the Sword Congealing Palace after a day.
This year, there were almost a hundred people who chose to leave.
"Thank you, I understand." Luo Zheng nodded and once again walked towards the backyard.
The two green robed youths shook their heads, "This person doesn't believe in heresy. If he leaves now, he might be able to avoid physical pain. Those guys are …"

Chapter 2649
Counterattack
Although the disciples of Sword Congealing Palace had average talent, they were closely related to the entire Seven Star City and the upper echelons of Dao Sword Palace.
They were either the direct descendants of some elders or the large clans of Seven Star City.
Before these people entered Sword Congealing Palace, they were like little tyrants in their own clans. After entering Sword Congealing Palace, they had become the malignant tumor of Dao Sword Palace. If you rashly provoked them, not only would you not gain any benefits, you would only bring trouble to yourself.
After Luo Zheng understood the general rules, he had a rough idea of what to do and returned to the backyard.
Before he even got close to the backyard, he heard a series of explosions coming from the backyard. A wave of heat came through the wall, followed by several screams.
Lai Huabei and the others were lying on the ground, their bodies were scorched and they were all heavily injured.
The person beside Lai Huabei was clearly enraged and said, "We entered Sword Congealing Palace, we are all fellow disciples. How can you treat us like this?"
This person was recommended by the Heaven's Wall City Lord and had a high status in Heaven's Wall City. He was also a proud person, how could he endure being teased by Sword Congealing Palace?
"Who is fellow disciple with you? Are you qualified?" The youngest silver-haired youth took two steps forward and stomped the person to the ground. He lowered his head and chuckled, "Entering our Condensing Sword Palace, the Grand Palaces and Qiu Shengshui's words don't count. Only our words count!"
This person gritted his teeth and asked, "Then how does it count as joining Sword Congealing Palace?"
The silver-haired youth shrugged and laughed, "Five stages. Just now was the first stage, the barbecued meat stage …"
"Then what's the second stage?" This person asked again.
"The second stage is the statue stage." The silver-haired youth's fingertips emitted traces of Dao Accumulation and a chill spread out, "We will freeze you into statues and place you in our yard for ten days to half a month. If you are willing, I will do it now."
Lai Huabei was extremely disappointed in Dao Sword Palace. Now that he was being humiliated by these people, his eyes were burning with anger.
Although he was a kind person, under his anger, he also exploded with strong killing intent, "I am willing to fight you to the death!"
"Xiu!"
The Dao Connotation in Lai Huabei's body burst out, and his whole body rushed towards the silver-haired youth like a sharp knife.
When a Complete Real God unleashed his full power, his power could not be underestimated, especially at such a close distance.
However, just as Lai Huabei took a step forward, a disciple of the Sword Condensation Palace took out a silver bead. The silver bead flashed with a silver light and Lai Huabei discovered that his Dao Connotation had completely disappeared …
Before he could reach the silver-haired youth, the silver-haired youth flipped over and kicked Lai Huabei in a very beautiful manner.
Lai Huabei's chest immediately caved in, and his entire body flew back several feet. He then fiercely hit a rockery in the backyard, and fainted for a moment.
"You want to risk your life against me with a lowly life like yours?" The silver-haired youth said coldly.
These hedonistic sons might not be very strong, but they were ultimately from large family clans. Almost all of them had great treasures in their hands, and all of them had Hermit divine powers. Ordinary Perfect True Gods would not be a match for them.
Seeing Lai Huabei's miserable state, the faces of the people from the Heaven's Wall City were extremely ugly.
Previously, they already knew that the Sword Condensation Palace was not a proper place for cultivation. However, it was still a learning palace under the Dao Sword Palace. No matter what, they had to join first.
They never expected that the Sword Condensation Palace would have such a group of ruthless people. Some of them recalled what Qiu Shengshui said, and understood that the "little devil" he was talking about was this group of people in front of them!
"Those who are willing to pass the five stages, stand up and queue up. The rockery in the backyard is too monotonous. Although you all are a little ugly, it's not bad to place you there. If you are not willing … you can leave the Sword Condensation Palace now," the silver-haired youth said with a laugh.
The hedonistic sons behind him also laughed. Every time there was a new person joining, it was a happy moment for them. It had almost become a habit over the years.
"Let's leave the Sword Condensation Palace …"
"These guys are obviously making things difficult for us!"
"Even if we agree, they will not let us off in the future!"
The people from the Heaven's Wall City also understood that these guys would not let the matter rest. Their goal was to chase away the new people who joined.
"You all are right," the silver-haired youth said indifferently. "The resources of the Sword Condensation Palace are limited. You guys who don't know where you came from, wanting a share of our resources is simply wishful thinking. If you know what's good for you, leave early …"
Just as the silver-haired youth finished speaking, Luo Zheng had already walked slowly into the backyard from the corridor.
His gaze swept over and saw Lai Huabei embedded in the rockery.
Under everyone's gaze, Luo Zheng walked up to the rockery and helped Lai Huabei down from the rockery. Following that, a green light appeared in his hand. That was the Dao of the Life Divine Dao.
Under the nourishment of the Dao, Lai Huabei's injuries quickly recovered. Not long after, he slowly woke up.
"Brother Luo Zheng … where did you go just now?" Lai Huabei asked as soon as he woke up.
"To understand the rules," Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
"Understand what rules?" Lai Huabei asked with a puzzled expression.
Luo Zheng released Lai Huabei and slowly got up. He said indifferently, "I heard that if you fight in the Sword Condensation Palace, it's fine as long as no one dies. I didn't expect the rules of the Sword Condensation Palace to be so loose."
When the silver-haired youth heard this, a mocking smile appeared on his face. "You went to understand the rules for no reason. Could it be that you dare to attack us?"
In Seven Stars City, they were the only ones who dared to challenge others. No one dared to provoke them.
Luo Zheng gently clasped his hands together. He looked at the silver-haired youth and smiled faintly. "That's exactly what I was planning to do!"
Just as he finished speaking, before anyone could react, Luo Zheng heavily slammed his heel on the ground. The black brick on the ground turned into powder and Luo Zheng charged towards the silver-haired youth like an arrow released from a bow.
The silver-haired youth never thought that Luo Zheng would dare to attack. His eyes flashed with a fierce light. Just as he was about to berate him, Luo Zheng's fist had already struck the silver-haired youth's face.
"Bang!"
Accompanied by a teeth-numbing muffled sound, the silver-haired youth's entire body tumbled in the air and smashed into the wall on the side of the backyard.
Luo Zheng had held back ninety percent of his strength in this punch. If he had used his full strength, he could have directly smashed the silver-haired youth's head into powder.
Even so, the silver-haired youth's injuries were extremely serious. His originally handsome facial features were blurred and he lay motionless on the ground like a dead dog.

A Lesson in Blood
The air in the backyard froze for a few breaths. These hedonistic sons of rich parents never thought that Luo Zheng would dare to make a move like this.

A crisp slap rang out. That hedonistic son of rich parents was sent flying by Luo Zheng. He spun seven or eight times in the air before falling to the ground.
These hedonistic sons of rich parents finally understood that this guy wasn't that simple.
"Mind Fixation Pearl!"
One of the hedonistic sons of rich parents took out that silver pearl again. This pearl flashed slightly and completely sealed Luo Zheng's Taoist Connotation.
However, Luo Zheng didn't use his Taoist Connotation at all. He only relied on the explosion of his strength to achieve extremely high speed!
The most important thing was that although these hedonistic sons of rich parents had cultivation, their actual combat ability was close to zero. They might be good at pranking others and using their magic treasures to bully ordinary Perfect True Gods, but they were powerless in front of Luo Zheng, who had experienced hundreds of battles.
Luo Zheng's Dantian was sealed by the Mind Fixation Pearl. He immediately felt that his Dantian wasn't operating smoothly. In the blink of an eye, he had already rushed to the front of that hedonistic son of rich parents. His hands stretched out like lightning, grasped the Mind Fixation Pearl, and suddenly twisted it.
"Crackle, crackle …"
That hedonistic son of rich parents' arm was broken into several pieces!

These people really only had cultivation and magic treasures. They had almost no ability to adapt. They were too weak …

Boom!
"Ka ka ka …"

"When was it not the same? Every time I see that group of rookies, I can't help but feel sorry for them. There are many things that can only be regretted after trying them out. "
While the two of them were talking, the ground was still trembling. The commotion in the backyard was getting louder and louder.
A moment later, a violent explosion suddenly came from the backyard.
Boom!
A terrifying aura spread out, causing the entire Sword Congealing Palace to tremble.
The two green robed disciples guarding the palace entrance were slightly stunned. They looked at each other and revealed a surprised expression. If they let this go on, these people would probably tear down the entire Sword Congealing Palace.
"Let's go! Let's go take a look! "
In the past, no matter how much trouble these hedonistic disciples caused, they had never caused such a huge commotion. This time, it seemed to be different.
The two green robed men went straight to the backyard …
In a short while, one of the green robed men went straight to the back mountain of the Sword Congealing Palace.
Qiu Shengshui was lying in a pavilion in the back mountain, drinking wine.
Ever since he was appointed as the Palace Master of the Sword Congealing Palace, Qiu Shengshui had been depressed most of the time. The Sword Congealing Palace's current state had something to do with him.
Whoosh!
The green robed man flew into the pavilion in the back mountain and stammered, "Palace Master Qiu, s-something bad has happened …"
Qiu Shengshui looked at him indifferently and said, "Those rookies can't take it anymore and want to leave?"
He had already expected this to happen, so he wasn't surprised.
"No!" The green robed man shook his head and said, "The disciples of the Yue, Bai, and Qiu families were all … all knocked unconscious. I don't believe it. Qiu Siyuan even triggered a Hermit Divine Ability. More than half of the Sword Congealing Palace was destroyed!"
"What?"
Hearing this, Qiu Shengshui's expression changed. He threw the wine jug aside and disappeared.
In the blink of an eye, Qiu Shengshui arrived at the Sword Congealing Palace.
Just as the green robed man said, the entire backyard of the Sword Congealing Palace had been turned into ruins.
The houses, pavilions, and courtyards had all been turned into ruins!
In the backyard, the rookies were all standing in their original positions. The hedonistic disciples of the Sword Congealing Palace were all in a terrible state.
The lightest injuries were broken arms and legs. There were even a few who had half their bodies smashed and were lying on the ground on the verge of death. It was unknown whether they were dead or alive. Among these hedonistic sons, the only one who was still standing was the long-haired young man.
Around the long-haired youth's body was a golden eggshell.
This golden eggshell was a Hermit Divine Ability given to him by the Qiu family's head. When he was injured, it would activate. It was because of this golden eggshell that he wasn't knocked unconscious by Luo Zheng.
"You're finished!"
"Not only did you offend the Qiu family! You also offended the Yue and Bai family! "
"It's enough for you to die ten thousand times!"
The long-haired youth hid behind the golden eggshell and threatened while gnashing his teeth.
Luo Zheng had heard too many threats. The long-haired youth's words were not threatening at all.
"Is that so?" Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He opened his arms and flew up like a roc.
"Boom!"
This time, Luo Zheng used eighty percent of his strength!
Under the tremendous force, the golden eggshell shook violently, but it still managed to block Luo Zheng's attack.
"Continue! You think you can break the Hermit Divine Ability set up by my grandfather? Wishful thinking! "The long-haired youth roared.
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng didn't care about the long-haired youth's ridicule. He just poured his violent strength on the golden eggshell.
After he smashed it again and again, a crack finally appeared on the top layer of the eggshell.
This time, the long-haired youth panicked. His eyes were full of fear. He knew very well that he was not Luo Zheng's opponent. Once the golden eggshell was broken, he would probably end up like the others.
"Hu …"
Luo Zheng took a deep breath and gathered his strength in his fist. Just as he was about to smash the golden eggshell, a thick palm suddenly blocked him from the side.
"Pu!"
Luo Zheng's fist smashed into the palm. He felt like an ox entering a sea of mud. All his strength disappeared without a trace.
"Enough." Qiu Shengshui stood in front of Luo Zheng.
He looked at the youth in front of him with a cautious gaze.
