Chapter 2652
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Two Birds with One Stone
Qiu Siyuan was the last to wake up.
When he woke up, he found himself soaked in a large vat. The vat was filled with a blood-red liquid.
This liquid was the juice of the Ghost Face Worm. This juice was rich in soul power and could effectively heal the damage to the soul.
The moment he woke up, anger surged in his heart!
Just as he was about to jump out of the vat, a lady behind him flew over like a ghost and pressed him back into the vat.
"Young Master's injuries are serious. Please continue to rest in the vat," the lady said with a sweet smile. She seemed to be very patient.
"I want to see my father! That Qiu Shengshui dared to hurt me. I want my father to chase him out of the Qiu Family! "Qiu Siyuan said unwillingly.
Hearing her young master's request, the lady shook her head slightly and said, "I've already asked your father …"
"What did my father say?" Qiu Siyuan's eyes flashed.
"He said you deserved it," the lady said helplessly. "Although Qiu Shengshui was demoted by the Elders Guild, he is still a hero of the Qiu Family. Back then, the Qiu Family let him down. Even the Elders didn't dare to expose this in front of Qiu Shengshui, but you did. You're lucky that he didn't kill you …"
Qiu Siyuan frowned slightly. He also realized that he had gone overboard. At least, he shouldn't have targeted Qiu Shengshui.
He still had lingering fears when he recalled Qiu Shengshui's roar.
"Hmph! Let's put this aside first! That kid from the Condensing Sword Palace … Qiao Qing, come with me to kill him! "Qiu Siyuan said again.
This lady called Qiao Qing was the adopted daughter of the Qiu Family. She was quite talented. Even if she didn't join the Academy, she had already stepped into the Nirvana Realm under the Qiu Family's nurturing. Killing a Perfect True God was not a problem for her.
Qiao Qing and Qiu Siyuan had always had a good relationship. Qiu Siyuan thought that she would agree, but Qiao Qing shook her head and said, "Young Master, Qiao Qing can't do this …"
"Why?" Qiu Siyuan's voice raised an octave. Qiao Qing used to listen to him.
"Master doesn't allow Qiao Qing to do it," she said helplessly.
"Then I'll find someone else!" There were many experts in the Qiu Clan, and as a Young Master, there had to be someone he could call.
"I'm afraid that won't work either …" Qiao Qing continued, "Qiu Xuecong has already made an appearance. She has also informed the Qiu family that the academy's matters should be resolved by yourselves. Your father has also agreed."
"How could this be?" Qiu Siyuan frowned. "Then I can't do anything to that kid?"
"There is a way." Qiao Qing smiled.
"What method?" Qiu Siyuan asked.
Qiao Qing blinked her eyes and said, "Young Master, you can take revenge with your own strength …"
Qiu Siyuan immediately fell silent.
That guy called Luo Zheng had already comprehended the True Meaning of Dao. His figure was like a ghost, and he had boundless strength. How could he rely on his own ability to take revenge? How was this possible?
Half a day later, Qiu Siyuan found Yue Baihao.
After these thirty plus hedonistic disciples were healed, they did not head to the Sword Congealing Palace. Instead, they gathered at a restaurant in Seven Stars City.
"What? Your Yue Baihao family is also like this? "Qiu Siyuan was slightly stunned.
Yue Baihao stroked the hair on his forehead and smacked his lips. "That's right. Those guards in the family can't even be ordered around. Every single one of them acted as if it had nothing to do with them. Just where did that Luo Zheng come from? Could it be that our Qiu family and Yue Baihao family are afraid of him? "
"Our family is afraid of an outsider? What kind of place is Heaven's Wall City? I've never even heard of it. In the entire Tiandu state, there are no forces on the stage! "Qiu Siyuan said emotionally.
"But they really don't dare to make a move against Luo Zheng." Yue Baihao's face was also filled with confusion.
Qiu Siyuan thought for a moment, then his eyes flashed. "Let's go find Qiu Ri! When the time comes, we'll join forces with Qiu Ri and see how that kid deals with it! "
"Qiu Ri …" Yue Baihao heard this suggestion and his expression changed slightly.
There were many hedonistic disciples in the Sword Congealing Palace, but there were still some geniuses among them. Qiu Ri was one of them.
However, most of the time, Qiu Ri was not in the Sword Congealing Palace. Furthermore, that fellow was a classic example of someone who only cared about money and not people. Ordering him around would probably cost a lot of divine crystals …
However, these hedonistic disciples valued their reputation more than anything else. When faced with such a setback, they would definitely seek revenge.
A group of more than thirty people rushed out of the restaurant. The other diners also knew where these people came from, so who would dare to provoke them?
On the top floor of the restaurant, the pale-faced Qiu Xuegong silently observed the situation below.
Beside Qiu Xuegong sat a white-haired old man. This old man was one of the three elders of the Dao Sword Palace, and also Qiu Siyuan's grandfather, Qiu Hanzhao.
"Is it really okay to do this?" Qiu Hanzhao asked with a frown.
Qiu Xuegong smiled faintly, then said, "These hedonistic disciples … need to be taught a lesson …"
"Then is Luo Zheng really a genius like you said, fusing over a thousand Divine Daos at such a young age?" Qiu Hanzhao asked again.
Qiu Xuegong nodded, "I said over a thousand Divine Daos … because the Dao essence he fused is too complicated … I can't even tell how many Divine Daos there are … maybe a thousand, maybe two thousand …"
Qiu Hanzhao's eyes revealed a strange look, "Such a person can't possibly come from a barren place like the Sky Capital Region."
"He's not from the Sky Capital Region … he must have come in from the Three Stacks Pass … but his true background … I'm not sure either …" Qiu Xuegong shook his head and said softly.
She was in charge of the Dao Sword Palace's Thirteen Learning Palaces. With Luo Zheng's talent, logically speaking, he should be able to enter the Buried Sword Palace or the Demon Sword Palace. However, she suddenly decided to throw Luo Zheng into the Sword Congealing Palace.
Firstly, she could test Luo Zheng. Secondly, she could use Luo Zheng to train the hedonistic disciples in Seven Star City. It could be said to be killing two birds with one stone.
However, she didn't expect Luo Zheng to be so straightforward. On the first day, he turned the Sword Congealing Palace upside down and even made Qiu Shengshui furious.
That day, she was also secretly observing Luo Zheng. If the situation went out of control, she would also intervene.
She didn't expect Luo Zheng to be so prudent and not kill a single person. She rather admired this.
Therefore, she notified the clans in Seven Star City in advance that no one was allowed to make a move against Luo Zheng. Now, it was up to Qiu Siyuan and the rest of the children to retaliate …
The Sword Congealing Palace remained peaceful these few days. The eleven of them lived in the huge backyard and it even seemed very spacious.
The academy continuously distributed some resources, such as the Dao Sword Palace's green robes, Azure Edge Swords, etc.
Of course, the Azure Edge Swords that Lai Huabei and the rest received were naturally different from those of the Heaven Luo Guards. However, it was enough to make them very surprised.

Chapter 2653
Dual Cultivation
The True Meaning of Dao was not particularly rare in the Origin World.
The Sanskrit that recorded the True Meaning of Dao was difficult to decipher, but once it was deciphered, it was extremely easy to duplicate.
There were many versions circulating in the market. Besides the human version, there were also versions recorded by other races.
The translations of the True Meaning of Dao were easy to obtain, but those who could truly comprehend them were few and far between.
"This translation is no different from what we usually see …" The person beside Lai Huabei flipped through the translation with a faint look of disappointment on his face.
After so many days in the Condensing Sword Palace, they had been looking forward to it. However, the things that were given out were all ordinary, so it was inevitable that they would be disappointed.
"The translations of the True Meaning of Dao are all complete. Not one word more or less. What difference do you expect there to be? The Dao Sword Palace's greatest resource is that towering sword. If we can observe it closely, it will be of great help to us, "another person advised.
"Liu Ci 'an is right. We didn't join the Dao Sword Palace for this translation," Lai Huabei nodded in agreement. Then, his gaze fell on Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng held the cyan book in his hand and had already flipped open a page. He was actually muttering to himself, as if he could not wait to cultivate the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate …
"Brother Luo Zheng? You plan to dual cultivate the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate? "Lai Huabei's eyes revealed a trace of surprise.
Luo Zheng's thoughts were seen through by Lai Huabei. He smiled slightly and said, "Can't I? I can only choose one True Meaning of Dao? "
"Of course not! However, you have already cultivated the True Meaning of Dao. If you were to re-cultivate the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate, I'm afraid it would be too time-consuming and exhausting. Although the Dao Sword Palace focuses on Eternal Sword Fate, other True Meanings of Dao have corresponding cultivation techniques, "Lai Huabei said.
Luo Zheng nodded and replied, "If there is no problem in cultivating another True Meaning of Dao, I naturally won't waste this opportunity …"
Speaking up to here, Luo Zheng sighed slightly in his heart.
These complete translations of the True Meaning of the Dao were extremely rare in the Divine Domain. Even Sages would find it hard to come by. However, in the Origin World, these were common goods. The difference was simply too great.
The main reason for this was the destruction of the Nine Li Race, which resulted in a gap in the inheritance of the motherworld in the God's Domain. It was just like how his mother still had a certain prejudice against the natives of the God's Domain. Naturally, she was unwilling to impart the True Meaning of Dao that she cultivated.
"Rumor has it that cultivating one more True Meaning of Dao would allow one to store one more Nirvana Token. Brother Luo Zheng's talent is outstanding. Naturally, it wouldn't be a problem for you to cultivate Dual Cultivation. Hehe …" The Perfect True God called Liu Ci 'an complimented.
These ten people had personally witnessed Luo Zheng tear apart more than thirty Condensing Sword Palace disciples.
In their hearts, Luo Zheng was already a celestial being. Subconsciously, they had already recognized him as their backbone.
"Brother Ci An, you flatter me. This Sword Fate Eternal Sword Intent is extremely complicated. I'm afraid it will take a lot of time …"
Before Luo Zheng could finish his words, an extremely sharp aura passed through the layers of walls of the Condensing Sword Palace and headed straight for the backyard.
This aura was like a physical object that pierced straight into the heart. Lai Huabei and the others trembled violently. Their eyes revealed a look of fear.
Luo Zheng felt this aura and raised his eyebrows slightly.
This aura was activated by the Sword Fate Eternal Sword Intent. It was strong, long, and sharp!
Following a flurry of footsteps, twenty to thirty people rushed out of the backyard. They were the group of playboys that Luo Zheng had taught a lesson …
"They're finally here …" The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth curled up slightly.
Luo Zheng was very clear about the nature of these playboys. If they didn't give in, they would think of all ways and means to mobilize all their resources to fight back.
When had these thirty or so people ever suffered such grievances?
After they rushed into the backyard, each and every one of them glared at Luo Zheng with unfriendly eyes.
If looks could kill, Luo Zheng would have died thousands of times.
"Brother Qiu Ri, it's him!" Qiu Siyuan said coldly.
"Let's attack together and cripple him," Yue Baihao said as he stroked the silver hair in front of his forehead.
At this moment, a strange voice came from the crowd. "This person just entered the Sea of True Intent not long ago, right? He's just a small fry that just crossed the line. Why do we need to attack together? Did you invite me here to look down on me, Qiu Ri? "
Then, a person walked out from the crowd. This person was eight feet tall and thin like a bamboo pole. However, his aura was very sharp. When fully activated, it was like an invisible sword.
This person was Qiu Ri, whom Qiu Siyuan had spent a lot of money to invite. He was also a genius of the Qiu Family.
There was no shelter in the backyard, so Qiu Ri's aura was even sharper. Lai Huabei and the others felt even more uncomfortable facing it, and their faces were as white as paper. However, Lai Huabei still mustered up his courage and stammered, "This, this is the Condensing Sword Academy. The academy's rules … are that only the academy's disciples can spar. You're from another academy, so I'm afraid it's against the rules!"
Because he was worried about the revenge of the playboys, Lai Huabei had spent the past few days understanding the academy's rules in detail.
"Hahaha …"
When the group of playboys heard this, they roared with laughter.
"These pieces of trash that came from god knows where. They've only been here for a few days, and they're already explaining the rules to us!" Qiu Siyuan said with a smile.
"I can't take it anymore … They don't know that Qiu Sheng Shui doesn't care about the Condensing Sword Academy. The academy's rules were set by us, haha …" Yue Bai Hao also laughed loudly.
The Dao Sword Academy had thirteen academies, and the Condensing Sword Academy's rules were the most relaxed. The other academies did not allow for casual spars.
These playboys had set the rules of the Condensing Sword Academy to make things easier for the disciples who had joined them after being chased away …
Lai Huabei and the others looked at each other, their eyes filled with worry.
Luo Zheng tilted his head slightly, stared at Qiu Siyuan, and asked, "Even if you guys set the rules, they're still rules. Bringing an outsider here is still against the rules, right?"
"Too bad … I'm a disciple of the Condensing Sword Academy," Qiu Ri said lightly.
Qiu Siyuan said sarcastically, "Only those from the Fly Battalion would study the rules. Too bad, isn't it?"
Luo Zheng shook his head expressionlessly, "I'm only asking this because I'm afraid he's from another academy. Since we're all disciples of the Condensing Sword Academy, then there's nothing to be sorry about …"
"What do you mean?" Qiu Ri's eyes narrowed slightly.
Then he heard a "peng" sound, and a large hole appeared in the ground that had just been repaired. Luo Zheng's body had already turned into a blur, drawing an extremely unreasonable trajectory as he headed straight for Qiu Ri.
Qiu Ri's strength was unquestionably the strongest in the Condensing Sword Academy, but he was the same as the other disciples. He had pitifully little experience in battle, so he never thought that Luo Zheng would suddenly attack.

Chapter 2654
Sword Observation
Qiu Ri was enraged by Luo Zheng's sudden attack.
The Dao Sword Palace was a sword cultivation sect after all, so they were very particular about the rules and despised such sudden attacks.
Luo Zheng had a ninety percent chance of defeating Qiu Ri, but he couldn't be bothered to tangle with him. He only wanted to end the battle quickly.
Just as Qiu Ri's face was filled with shock and confusion, Luo Zheng's palm landed on his chin. Then, Qiu Ri felt a loud boom in his head. A white light flashed, and he lost consciousness.
Luo Zheng glanced at the unconscious Qiu Ri, then glanced at Qiu Siyuan and Yue Baihao. He smiled faintly, "Do you still want to fight?"
Qiu Siyuan and Yue Baihao's faces were ashen. If it were anyone else, how would they dare to fight?
But they had come with such ferocity, so how could they retreat? They couldn't just lose face …
"Do it!" Qiu Siyuan yelled.
They were much more prepared today, so they might have a chance.
After the time it took for a stick of incense to burn, the thirty or so hedonistic disciples were lying on the ground again.
This time, Luo Zheng held back a lot. He only knocked them out one by one, and the Sword Condensation Academy wasn't damaged much …
In an elegant room in the academy, Qiu Sheng Shui saw this scene and smiled. He sighed inwardly at Qiu Sheng Gong's foresight.
Not only did he bring back the tomb of the previous Palace Master, but he also brought back a treasure. Even in the Burial Sword Palace, this kid would be top-notch, right? Perhaps this good-for-nothing Sword Condensation Academy would be able to turn things around?
He had always been dissatisfied with being demoted to the Sword Condensation Academy, but now he was looking forward to it.
After being taught a lesson twice, Qiu Siyuan and Yue Baihao finally recognized the problem in front of them.
The families they relied on were no longer helping them, and even Qiu Ri wasn't a match for Luo Zheng. It could be said that they didn't have the resources to fight back against Luo Zheng.
When they recognized the problem, they finally chose to accept their fate and live in harmony.
"Big Brother Luo Zheng, I'll leave the residence at the top of the backyard to you!"
"Everyone else just follow the rules …"
"…"
Qiu Siyuan's sudden change in attitude made Luo Zheng somewhat uncomfortable.
'That's good as well, at least these guys won't be thinking about revenge every day …'
Although this group of hedonistic sons were full of problems, once they accepted each other, they were actually very easy to get along with.
They were generous with their money and were loyal to their friends. For the past few days, they had also invited Lai Huabei and Luo Zheng to travel around Seven Stars City. Luo Zheng also used this opportunity to understand the history and origin of the Dao Sword Palace.
However, these hedonistic disciples didn't cherish the cultivation resources in front of them at all.
Disciples like Qiu Siyuan and Yue Baihao were not lacking in talent, but they had been born in large clans since they were young. Their status and starting point were much higher than others, and under the protection of their families, it was naturally difficult for them to have great ambitions. They did not have much ambition in the path of cultivation.
In comparison, Lai Huabei and the others were more cautious and conscientious. Most of the time, they read the translated version of the Sword Essence word by word.
Luo Zheng was now only a disciple of the Dao Sword Palace, and it was not worthwhile for him to educate these hedonistic sons and let them cultivate wholeheartedly.
However, it was strange that after Luo Zheng used his irresistible strength to crush them twice, these hedonistic sons had indeed changed.
At the very least, they hoped to become more powerful and had a little more interest in cultivation.
Qiu Siyuan and the others had also consulted Luo Zheng several times about some questions, including why Luo Zheng was far superior to them despite being in the same realm as them, and so on.
Luo Zheng would naturally give them pointers if he could answer the questions, but some questions involved Luo Zheng's own secrets, so it was naturally inconvenient for him to answer them.
Just one month later, the Dao Sword Palace organized the first "Sword Observation".
The target of the sword observation was the 13 great academies under the Dao Sword Palace.
Seven Stars City was built around a mountain, and it was truly a mountain city. Countless magnificent palaces were spread out around the huge Seven Stars Mountain. Looking down from the top of the mountain would give people a feeling of dizziness.
The 70,000 feet tall towering sword stabbed into the top of the mountain, emitting an indescribable majesty.
Even if a Nirvana Realm powerhouse saw this sword, they would feel a sense of solemnity and fear.
On the platform below the towering sword, a middle-aged man dressed in green with a green crown on his head shouted softly, "Burial Sword Palace to the rooftop!"
Although his voice was not loud, it traveled thousands of miles.
The platform that the middle-aged man was on extended outwards to rooftops of different heights, and each rooftop could accommodate up to 1,000 people.
When his voice was transmitted out, traces of spatial fluctuations appeared in the center of the rooftop.
Buzz buzz buzz …
In the blink of an eye, 300 disciples appeared on the rooftop. They maintained the same posture and sat cross-legged.
These disciples wore the same green robes and every single one of them emitted a sharp aura. From their eyes to their postures, they were all filled with arrogance. They were the disciples of the Sword Burying Academy and the most outstanding batch of disciples in the Dao Sword Palace.
"Demon Sword Palace to the rooftop!" The middle-aged man shouted again.
On the other rooftop, five hundred disciples sat cross-legged. The Demonic Sword Learning Palace's status was second only to the Sword Burial Palace. In recent years, the Demonic Sword Learning Palace seemed to have surpassed the Sword Burial Palace. The two academies had always been competing in the dark.
Therefore, when the disciples of the Demon Sword Palace appeared, they looked at the rooftop of the Burial Sword Palace with provocation in their eyes.
"Broken Sword Palace …"
"Furious Sword Palace …"
On each of the rooftops, the disciples of the academies gathered.
Finally, the middle-aged man looked at the smallest rooftop. Compared to the other rooftops, this rooftop was many times smaller. It could only accommodate about 100 people. This was the rooftop of the Condensing Sword Palace.
The Dao Sword Palace originally only had 12 academies, and this rooftop was added later for the Condensing Sword Palace. Compared to the other rooftops, it was naturally a little comical.
However, although the rooftop was small, it was never full. Sometimes, there was not even a single person. After all, the hedonistic disciples of the Condensing Sword Palace were not in the mood to cultivate. Even if the opportunity to 'observe the sword' was hard to come by, they would not cherish it.
But as usual, the middle-aged man would still call them over.
"Disciples of the Condensing Sword Palace to the rooftop!"
As the middle-aged man's voice spread out, the rooftop suddenly emitted waves of spatial fluctuations.
"Buzz buzz …"
More than 40 disciples of the Condensing Sword Palace appeared on the rooftop in unison.
Seeing so many people suddenly appear on the rooftop, the middle-aged man was slightly stunned. He had never seen such a scene before.
When the disciples of the other academies saw this scene, they also revealed a confused expression.
The Condensing Sword Palace's notoriety was known by the other academies. These hedonistic disciples were arrogant and domineering, but they all came from prominent families. They could not afford to offend them.
Fortunately, the 13 academies were all established independently. If one did not offend them, it would be difficult to have anything to do with them. Since they could not afford to offend them, they could naturally avoid them.
In the past, the Condensing Sword Palace did not have a single disciple to observe the sword. Occasionally, there would be seven or eight disciples. Instead of observing the sword properly, they would make a ruckus at the side and were expelled several times.

Chapter 2655
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Towering Sword
Lai Huabei, Liu Ci 'an, and the rest of the people from Heaven's Wall City saw the towering sword up close for the first time, so they were undoubtedly very excited.
The origins and legends of the towering sword were very widespread. Lai Huabei and the rest had often heard about it from their families since they were young.
A long time ago, the Seven Stars State was still a barren land with only a few humans living there. Moreover, these humans were still in an ignorant and uncivilized stage.
At that time, the powerhouse who ruled the Seven Stars State was the great demon Mirror Grasshopper. The Mirror Grasshopper was as big as a mountain and had immense strength. Its strength was also far superior to most of the human Nirvana Realm powerhouses.
Just a million years ago, the towering sword appeared out of nowhere and fell straight down, nailing the great demon Mirror Grasshopper to death on the Seven Stars Mountain …
The person who descended the towering sword was called Qiu Yinhe, and the Dao Sword Palace was created after he killed the Mirror Grasshopper.
In the past million years, the Dao Sword Palace had completely occupied the Seven Stars State and even spread to some of the surrounding states. Even the desolate Tiandu State was blessed by the Dao Sword Palace for the humans to have such power now.
Therefore, for people from the Tiandu State like Lai Huabei, joining the Dao Sword Palace was their greatest goal in life.
Luo Zheng sat cross-legged on the rooftop and glanced around. He saw that the people from the other academies were already seated neatly. Then, his gaze landed on the towering sword not far away.
Although observing the sword was to comprehend the Sword Fate Eternal True Intent, the aura that this towering sword emitted at this moment wasn't sharp. It was as if there was an invisible sword box that absorbed the sharp aura of the towering sword.
"This aura …"
As a sword cultivator, Luo Zheng's comprehension of the Sword Dao was extremely sharp.
Back then, when the "Qiu Xuegong" brought everyone into the Seven Stars City, the towering sword's aura was completely different from today.
Although the towering sword before him had retracted its aura, it was like a dragon hiding in the abyss. It could erupt at any time and was even more terrifying than before …
'Maybe there's someone in the Dao Sword Palace who can control this towering sword?' Luo Zheng thought to himself.
Qiu Siyuan, Yue Baihao, and the rest of the good-for-nothing disciples were also rather reserved now. After all, they were notorious, so it was natural for them to have a sudden change in the end.
At this moment, a voice suddenly came from another high platform. "Eh? What are these trash from the Condensing Sword Academy doing here? "
"As the parasites of Seven Stars City, they should have the awareness of being parasites … Did they discuss and come to cause trouble?"
"I want the Grand Swordsman to chase them out …"
The ones who spoke were the disciples of Bewitching Sword Palace and Sword Burial Palace.
Some of these disciples were local geniuses of Seven Stars City, and some were clansmen of the Qiu Clan and Yue Bai Clan. However, unlike Qiu Siyuan and the others, these people were all descendants of great clans and had great talents. Naturally, they weren't afraid of Qiu Siyuan and the others.
Who were Qiu Siyuan and the others? Although he had suffered a great loss in the hands of Luo Zheng during this period of time and had been completely subdued by Luo Zheng, how could he tolerate such words?
Qiu Siyuan was instantly enraged, "Qiu Zhixiu! What nonsense are you talking about?! "
Yue Bai Hao also scolded with a sullen face, "I am also a disciple of the Condensing Sword Academy. We can come and go as we please. What does it have to do with you?"
Qiu Zhixiu from the Sword Burial Palace coldly laughed, "Condensing Sword Academy is considered an academy? Hahaha, it's just a sheep pen for a bunch of trash like you …"
Luo Zheng, Lai Huabei, Liu Ci 'an and the others were still fine. After all, they had just arrived.
However, these hedonistic disciples couldn't endure it. No matter how bad the Condensing Sword Academy was, it was still their academy. How could they endure it?
These twenty to thirty hedonistic disciples suddenly stood up and glared at each other. It seemed like they had the intention to fight.
At this moment, the middle aged man on the platform lightly ordered, "Today, I summoned you all to observe the sword. What are you all making a ruckus for? If anyone continues to make a ruckus, they will immediately be chased down the mountain! "
The middle aged man was the Grand Swordsman who was in charge of observing the sword. He had an extremely high status in the Dao Sword Palace. His voice contained an irresistible majesty.
The disciples from the Sword Burial Palace and the Demon Sword Academy had playful smiles on their faces. Qiu Siyuan, Yue Baihao, and the others sat down angrily.
They didn't expect that they would be ridiculed when they finally wanted to seriously cultivate …
"If you all are wholeheartedly yearning for the Paramita Realm, you naturally won't be distracted. Let the other academies ridicule you. If there comes a day when you step into the Paramita Realm before them, that will be your greatest counterattack," Luo Zheng said softly.
When Qiu Siyuan and the others heard this, they revealed a bitter smile. "It's not that we haven't tried before. It's just that cultivating is too boring. We can't focus at all …"
Qiu Siyuan, Yue Baihao, and the others weren't people without talent. Otherwise, they wouldn't have been able to cultivate to the Perfection True God Realm at such a young age.
However, as they grew older, their thoughts became more and more complicated. Coupled with the care of the elders in their clans, they had long lost their drive.
It was also Luo Zheng's violent beating that caused them to change slightly.
However, they also understood in their hearts that this passion wouldn't last for more than a few days.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng stared at Qiu Siyuan and said, "Just because it's boring? I don't think so. "He shook his head and continued," You're more afraid of the predicament after failure, right? You're afraid of being treated as a person with mediocre talent and not working hard. That's true cowardice! "
When Qiu Siyuan, Yue Baihao, and the others heard this, they were momentarily stunned. Some thoughts from the depths of their hearts immediately surged forth.
Although they had given up on themselves in the Sword Condensation Academy, they still had their own pride in their hearts. For example, Yue Baihao stubbornly believed that his talent wasn't any worse than Qiu Zhixiu's, but he couldn't be bothered to prove himself.
However, as Luo Zheng said, hidden in the depths of their hearts wasn't disdain at all. In fact, it was cowardice. It was fear!
Although they were a bunch of good-for-nothing rich kids with nothing to do, they still wanted to prove their worth in their clans …
Just as Qiu Siyuan and the others were deep in thought, the Grand Sword Master said, "Academy disciples, get ready. I'm going to activate the great array now!"
As soon as the Grand Sword Master finished speaking. All of the academy disciples simultaneously took out the translation of the Eternal Sword Essence in their hands.
Then, the Grand Swordsman walked along the platform and sat down in the center. He stretched out his hands and moved them back and forth. Rings of light green light appeared around him.
At the same time, the aura of the towering sword changed …
The True Dao Intent that had originally been condensed into a ball slowly bloomed. The Eternal Sword Essence emitted from the huge sword slowly bloomed!
"Weng!"
From the surface of the towering sword, strands of black silk emerged, and these strands of black silk were like long worms, rapidly rushing towards the crowd!

Chapter 2656
Sword Intent Battle
Luo Zheng was very resistant to anything entering his mind.
After all, the Infinity Ruler was still in his mind. If someone else were to pry into it, it would cause unnecessary trouble.
The moment the strand of hair entered, Luo Zheng was slightly shocked. At the same time, he said in his mind, "9527, could this thing be …"
"Don't panic. This is just an illusion created by the sword using the Sword Intent of Eternity. This thing can't pry into your mind," 9527 explained without missing a beat. "Besides, the Infinity Ruler in your mind is just Chiyou's hallucination. It's not a big deal even if it's discovered …"
Even now, 9527 didn't have much hope of fusing with the Divine Dao.
Luo Zheng was slightly relieved. After the strand of hair entered his mind, it quickly connected with Luo Zheng's soul. Then, he realized that everything around him had disappeared.
The Grandmaster Swordsman, the rooftop, the scenery in the distance …
The only things that remained were the towering sword in front of him, the disciples of the Thirteen Academies, and the translation in their hands!
"What a magical illusion …"
"This Sword Intent of Eternity actually exists as if it's real!"
"No wonder it's said that one can get twice the results with half the effort when facing the towering sword. I feel like my mind has cleared up a lot!"
Lai Huabei and the others were very excited. They reached out and touched the strand of hair on their foreheads.
When the other disciples heard what Lai Huabei and the others said, their faces were full of disdain. Whispers could be heard faintly.
"Where did the Condensing Sword Academy get these bumpkins from?"
"Seems like their first time seeing the sword …"
"That academy is originally a garbage dump. Do you expect them to recruit any geniuses?"
"Ignore them. Don't waste time!"
In the Burial Sword Palace, the disciples of the Demon Sword Palace had already raised the translation in their hands. They began to chant and recite the translation in their hands.
Their voices were very loud and clear. One word after another jumped out of their mouths, and it was very smooth …
"The Sword of the Absolute Beginning. Since ancient times, it has been seeking. Diligent Sword Heart, it is the Dao of Eternity …"
One person was chanting like this, and two people were chanting like this. Everyone was chanting at their own speed. With so many voices gathered together, it formed a noise in the eyes.
Lai Huabei held the translation and recited, "Tai, Tai, Tai Chu …"
Chanting the true intent of the Dao was already extremely difficult, and Lai Huabei and the others had just started. Coupled with the noise from the surroundings, how could they concentrate? She couldn't continue after reading three or four words …
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng frowned slightly and turned to ask Qiu Siyuan, "One should choose a quiet place to recite the True Meaning of the Dao. Won't they affect the others by making so much noise?"
Qiu Siyuan shook his head lightly. "Big Brother Luo Zheng, you might not know this, but the Thirteen Academies are in a state of competition with each other. This way of cultivating the True Meaning of Dao was also instructed by their Palace Masters …"
"Why is there such a strange rule?" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
"What they said is that if one is really able to meticulously comprehend the Dao, then one's heart will naturally be free of distractions and not be affected by others. Actually, the influence is quite great. I don't know which idiot made this rule either." Qiu Siyuan curled his lips and said, "Moreover, look at the body of the towering greatsword. When they cultivate the True Meaning of the Dao, they will even attack each other! We call this the True Meaning Sword Battle! "
Luo Zheng looked in the direction Qiu Siyuan was pointing and saw streaks of green converging on the broad body of the sword. At the end of the green strands, there were sword-shaped patterns.
These sword-shaped patterns were of different sizes. The large ones were as tall as a person, while the small ones were only as long as a pinky finger.
"What do these sword-shaped patterns represent?" Luo Zheng asked.
"They represent the degree of comprehension of the True Meaning of Dao … Look at mine, it's only the size of a palm. In the past, I could recite two or three hundred words, so it's this big. Big Brother Luo Zheng, this is your first time cultivating the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate, right? That's why it's only the size of a thumb, "Qiu Siyuan explained.
This towering greatsword could only identify the degree of cultivation of the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate. Although Luo Zheng had already completed the True Meaning of Dao technique, two completely different True Meanings naturally couldn't be displayed.
Luo Zheng looked at the end of the strands that were connected to him. There was really only a thumb-sized sword-shaped pattern. He smiled and said, "This is interesting …"
Other than him, Lai Huabei and the others were also the size of a pinky finger. On the contrary, that Liu Ci 'an, who should have cultivated the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate, was actually slightly longer than Luo Zheng and the others. It was about the size of an index finger.
"Zeng!"
"Zeng!"
At this moment, the sound of metal clashing could be heard amidst the chanting.
Luo Zheng looked up towards the source of the sound and saw two sword-shaped patterns that were as tall as a person on the towering greatsword actually attacking each other. It was clearly just a pattern, but it actually produced the sound of swords clashing!
In the Burial Sword Palace and the Demon Sword Palace, a disciple each suddenly stood up.
The strands on their foreheads were connected to the two largest sword-shaped patterns. The two of them looked at each other, and their eyes revealed a blazing battle intent. The chanting from their mouths became louder and louder!
One of them said, "Crimson Fire Emperor, Five Elements Return to Vengeance, Ten Thousand Miles, Immortal, No …"
The other person said, "The Spirit walks the blazing heart, the righteous path, not that I have no life, not that I have no name …"
"Those two fellows are called Qiu Yi and Yue Bai Cheng. They can be considered the two most outstanding disciples in this batch. The two of them belong to the Burial Sword Palace and the Demon Sword Palace respectively. They also belong to two major families. They have always been at loggerheads with each other, wanting to trample on each other. Every time, the two of them would engage in a True Meaning Sword Battle. Hehe … they don't put anyone else in their eyes." Qiu Siyuan smiled faintly, but there was a hint of loneliness in his eyes.
Everyone hoped to become the most dazzling genius, but Qiu Siyuan also knew that it would be difficult for him to reach that height. In his heart, other than jealousy, there was a deep sense of helplessness.
"We just have to take it slow. As long as we don't stop and take one step at a time, we can always catch up." Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Qiu Siyuan shrugged his shoulders and did not refute Luo Zheng. For the first time, he opened the translation in his hands and began to chant the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate in a low voice.
…
…
On the platform.
After the Grand Sword Master activated the great array, he looked at the towering sword with his hands behind his back.
At this time, the various Palace Lords also rushed over.
"This batch of disciples all started at the same time. Qiu Yi and Yue Bai Cheng have already left the others far behind. I'm afraid the other disciples won't be able to catch up." A petite woman looked at the towering sword and said. This woman was the Palace Lord of the Upper Sword Palace. As she spoke, she looked at Qiu Yi and Yue Bai Cheng with a hint of regret in her eyes. Her biggest mistake was not taking Qiu Yi into the Upper Sword Palace.
"What's wrong with the Condensing Sword Academy today? Why are there so many people here? " The Palace Lord of the Angry Sword Palace asked curiously. As he spoke, he looked around. "Qiu Sheng Shui didn't come …"

However, just as she finished speaking, two graceful figures floated over from the distance.

Chapter 2657
Genius
The Autumn Academy was only a title. The thirteen academies were under her direct control and she was the direct superior of the palace masters of these academies.
Her real name was Qiu Hanyan and her position in the Dao Sword Palace was second only to the three great elders and the current palace master of the Dao Sword Palace.
When the palace masters saw her, they all bowed.
The palace master of the Upper Sword Palace smiled and asked, "How are the negotiations between the Autumn Academy and Elder Qi?"
Qiu Hanyan glanced at her indifferently and replied coldly, "Nothing much …"
"I would like to know if the Dao Sword Palace really intends to honor the reward of finding the cenotaph?" The palace master of the Burial Sword Palace, Yue Baili, asked.
"The reputation of the Dao Sword Palace is gilded in gold and silver in the Seven Stars Province … What should be honored will naturally be honored … But what does this have to do with you?" Qiu Hanyan replied.
After Qiu Bolan went missing, the Dao Sword Palace had made an agreement that if the former palace master Qiu Bolan's cenotaph was found, they would elect a new palace master of the Dao Sword Palace.
In the blink of an eye, the cenotaph had not been found and the current palace master had already taken the position.
No one would have thought that Qiu Hanyan would really be able to bring her father's cenotaph back. Logically speaking, a new palace master should be elected.
The Dao Sword Palace was now divided into two factions and even the three great elders did not see eye to eye with each other.
Two of the three great elders supported the current palace master and did not agree with the election of a new palace master. Because of this, there was a dispute within the Dao Sword Palace …
Therefore, Qiu Hanyan was not in a good mood.
After she said this to the palace masters, her gaze fell on the balcony where the Condensing Sword Palace was located and she faintly revealed a look of concern.
Yue Baili smiled and said, "Is the Autumn Academy here today to see the new batch of disciples of the Condensing Sword Palace?"
Qiu Hanyan did not reply and the palace master of the Demon Sword Palace continued, "What is there to pay attention to about the ordinary divine citizens of the Tiandu Province? They have no foundation and no talent. Staying in the Dao Sword Palace is purely a waste of resources … "
"The Condensing Sword Palace is a place to pretend to be useless. After wasting so many years, is there a need to be afraid of wasting a little?" Yue Baili said.
Hearing this, Qiu Shengshui glanced at Yue Baili and chuckled.
Yue Baili's eyes flashed as he asked, "Brother Shengshui, did I say something wrong?"
Qiu Shengshui raised his eyebrows and laughed: "Your Bewitching Sword Palace and Burial Sword Palace are blessed by the heavens and have the most resources, but the number of disciples you've nurtured are few. In the past two hundred years, almost all of the disciples that have been promoted to Tian Luo Guard are trash. Now you have the face to talk about my Condensing Sword Academy?"
"You …" Yue Baili's face sank.
"What about me? In these hundred years, have any of your disciples been sent to Greater One Heavenly Palace? Has any disciple ever entered Tai Yiwei? Not a single one, yet you are all so proud. Why don't you all go and die? "Qiu Shengshui continued to mock.
"You, you …" Yue Baili's face turned red.
"What about me? If you can't win the argument, do you want to fight? You, you, and all of you. Why don't all of you come up together and spar with me? " Qiu Shengshui pointed arrogantly at the palace masters and said, "All of you are trash, yet you want to nurture geniuses. What a joke …"
The palace masters were reprimanded by Qiu Shengshui and their faces turned red and white.
Qiu Shengshui was qualified to enter Greater One Heavenly Palace. Although he had been silent for many years, how could these palace masters be his match?
Qiu Hanyan smiled and her originally gloomy mood seemed to have improved.
The Grand Sword Master beside her said: "Qiu Shengshui, this is a place to observe swords. Watch your words!"
Qiu Shengshui rubbed his nose and said: "I've long found these guys an eyesore!"
"The competition between academies is about the talent and cultivation of the disciples, not the strength of the palace masters." The Grand Sword Master said fairly.
"The Grand Sword Master is right! We are focused on nurturing geniuses for Dao Sword Palace and this guy is talking about himself. How vulgar! "Yue Baili shook his head and said disdainfully.
Qiu Shengshui smiled and glanced at the rooftop of Condensing Sword Palace. His gaze landed on Luo Zheng and a look of anticipation appeared on his face. "The disciples that you nurtured can be called geniuses. Really … hehe."
Qiu Shengshui saw a shadow of himself in Luo Zheng.
He came with Qiu Hanyan for no other reason than to see Luo Zheng.
Yue Baili suddenly understood something and said: "So, Condensing Sword Palace seems to have good luck and found a genius in a small place like Tiandu Region?"
Qiu Shengshui smiled faintly and said: "This person's talent is slightly stronger than mine!"
Hearing this, the palace masters and the Grand Sword Master's expression turned serious.
Dao Sword Palace divided the talents of its disciples into eight grades. Those with sixth-grade talents would be able to enter Great Yi Heavenly Palace. Qiu Shengshui's talent back then was sixth-grade. Such a person was rarely seen in Dao Sword Palace for thousands of years. These palace masters found it unbelievable.
"Who is Brother Shengshui talking about?" Yue Baili asked.
Qiu Shengshui shrugged his shoulders. "You'll know when you see!"
Therefore, all the palace masters' gazes were focused on the rooftop of Condensing Sword Palace. They also wanted to know if Qiu Shengshui was boasting. After all, there were too few grade six talents.
…
When Luo Zheng first cultivated the "True Meaning of Nature", he did not borrow any external help and comprehended it forcefully. It was still a little difficult.
Now that he was facing this towering sword, Luo Zheng finally understood what Lai Huabei and the others meant.
Facing this towering sword, he could indeed achieve twice the results with half the effort.
"Absolute Beginning Sword Land, Ancient Seeking …"
When he began to recite the words in the translation, a strange feeling rose in his mind.
He felt his consciousness fusing into the sword pattern on the towering sword!
As if his soul had left his body, he himself had turned into a sword!
"Zizi Sword Heart, for Everlasting Prosperity …"
As he continued to recite, the towering sword also reflected the True Meaning of Eternity of Sword Fate, permeating Luo Zheng's soul.
Because Luo Zheng had just started, the True Meaning of Eternity of Sword Fate from the towering sword was also very shallow.
Like this, Luo Zheng felt as if someone was leading the way, leading Luo Zheng to comprehend the True Meaning of Dao. His recitation was extremely smooth!
In less than half an incense stick's worth of time, Luo Zheng had already recited 500 words, and the sword-shaped pattern on the towering sword continued to grow larger and larger, constantly climbing upwards. Now, the sword-shaped pattern was already half a foot long and as thick as an arm …
Many palace masters focused their gazes on the rooftop of Condensing Sword Palace and quickly discovered Luo Zheng's sword pattern.

Chapter 2658
Comprehension
The Palace Lord of the Upper Sword Palace also added, "Let's not talk about the Buried Sword Palace and the Demon Sword Palace. There are more than ten people in our Upper Sword Palace who can do this!"
The Palace Lord of the Demon Sword Palace laughed, "That's normal. After all, the Condensing Sword Palace is a bunch of trash. Suddenly, someone who looks like he's a treasure …"
The other Palace Masters also laughed in agreement.
Qiu Shengshui only glanced at them coldly. His gaze was still focused on Luo Zheng.
He didn't believe that Luo Zheng's potential was only so little.
…
On the surface of the towering sword, all the sword-shaped diagrams were slowly rising and enlarging.
If a disciple encountered a bottleneck and was stuck at a certain point, the towering sword would reflect the Eternal Sword Dao's true meaning. Under the influence of the Eternal Sword Dao's true meaning, it would often have the effect of enlightening the disciple!
Therefore, the cultivation speed of the Dao Sword Palace's disciples was much faster than the self-comprehending True Gods in the outside world.
Luo Zheng's eyes glowed as he chanted in a low voice.
Normally, the further one went, the slower one's comprehension speed. However, Luo Zheng's chanting speed didn't slow down. Instead, it became faster and faster!
Actually, with his talent, he didn't need the help of the towering sword for less than two thousand words! Under the baptism of the sword's true meaning, Luo Zheng was like a tiger that had grown wings!
Six hundred words …
Eight hundred words …
One thousand words …
In the eyes of these Palace Lords, one thousand words was a small accomplishment.
Luo Zheng had reached a thousand words in less than an incense's time. This kind of talent could be considered top-grade.
"Reaching a thousand words in an incense's time is not bad in the Upper Sword Palace. However, in our Buried Sword Palace, it's only normal," Yue Baili said, pretending to be calm.
Qiu Shengshui still didn't say anything. If Luo Zheng was only at this level, he wasn't worthy of his attention. However, he had a faint premonition that Luo Zheng's first cultivation wouldn't stop here.
"But this kid's chanting speed seems to be increasing …" The Broken Sword Palace Lord said.
Not only did this Palace Master notice this, but the other Palace Masters also noticed this.
The chanting speed went from fast to slow. This was the case for most people who cultivated the True Meaning of Dao. However, Luo Zheng actually did the opposite and became faster and faster. This performance was extremely abnormal.
From 500 words to 1000 words, Luo Zheng had used half an incense stick's worth of time. However, from 1000 words to 1500 words, Luo Zheng had only used 20 to 30 breaths of time. The True Meaning of Dao, which was originally extremely difficult to recite, had been spoken out loud and powerfully like a lotus flower.
His voice was not loud, but the many Palace Masters could hear him clearly in the noisy illusion!
"By your order, Bai Ru's Bone and Blood, return …" Luo Zheng's lips opened slightly without any hesitation.
One thousand five hundred words...
One thousand seven hundred words …
One thousand nine hundred words …
At this time, even the Great Sword Priest was attracted by this scene. He said, "For someone to reach 2000 words on their first try, that means he is a third grade talent."
Yue Bai Li's expression changed greatly. He said seriously, "A third grade talent, although not as good as our Burial Sword Palace's Qiu Yi, it is still very rare. I didn't think that a barren Sky Capital Region could produce such a person …"
"Don't speak too early," Qiu Shengshui said, "I said before that this kid's talent is not inferior to mine!"
Yue Bai Li was still somewhat unsatisfied. He said, "I think Brother Shengshui is speaking too early. Everyone has cultivated the True Meaning of Dao. Qiu Yi once broke 4300 words in one go!"
"Wait and see," Qiu Shengshui said calmly. He seemed very confident.
Luo Zheng's eyes shone brighter and brighter, like a ball of fire.
He felt that his voice had formed an echo with the towering sword. Every word he said, the towering sword responded with a word!
Of course, what the towering sword responded to Luo Zheng was not its voice, but the True Meaning of Eternal Sword …
"I don't know if it is because I am extremely suitable for cultivating the True Meaning of Eternal Sword, or because of the towering sword, but why is my speed of comprehending the True Meaning of Eternal Sword so much faster than the True Meaning of Nature?"
When he reached three thousand words, Luo Zheng felt a trace of confusion in his heart, and he actually stopped …
Yue Bai Li saw that Luo Zheng stopped and sighed in relief. He said to Qiu Shengshui with a smile, "It seems that the Condensing Sword Academy will not be able to produce a genius on par with Brother Shengshui!"
Qiu Shengshui and Qiu Hanyan also stared at Luo Zheng. Just now, Luo Zheng was reciting at an extremely fast speed. Now that he suddenly stopped, they were naturally puzzled.
If one was stuck at a certain point in the True Meaning of Dao, their soul would be unable to bear it. It would be very painful and even cause them to faint. However, Luo Zheng's face showed only a trace of confusion. Qiu Shengshui and the rest naturally found it strange.
However, Luo Zheng only stopped for three breaths of time …
He could not explain the confusion in his heart. He was too lazy to think about it, so he increased the speed of his recitation.
But this time, once he started, his chanting speed actually sped up once more, and he'd already fallen into a state where he'd completely forgotten himself. His voice that was originally chanting in a low voice had become resounding, resounding, and extremely penetrating!
As a result, it immediately attracted the attention of the disciples who were cultivating.
Lai Huabei recited a few dozen words with the help of the towering sword. However, he had already reached the limit of what he could bear. Every time he recited a word, he felt as if his soul was being pierced by thousands of arrows. Waves of dizziness came over him.
When he could no longer bear it, he could only stop for a while. Just as he stopped, he heard Luo Zheng's voice.
With Brother Luo Zheng's talent, I'm afraid he has already recited two to three hundred words? He looked at the strand of hair in front of Luo Zheng's forehead and found the sword-shaped pattern on the towering sword. His body suddenly trembled.
"Luo Zheng's sword-shaped pattern … is already half a person's height?" Lai Huabei's face was filled with disbelief.
Although Lai Huabei always thought that Luo Zheng was a genius, he did not expect Luo Zheng to be able to reach this level. It was only his first time, and he had already surpassed most of the students present!
Lai Huabei was not the only one who was attracted. Qiu Siyuan, Yue Baihao, and the other hedonistic disciples were also shocked.
Previously, Qiu Siyuan still had a trace of pride in his heart. As a disciple of the academy, he was still ahead of these newcomers. At least he could recite three hundred words, and his sword-shaped pattern was already the size of a palm.
However, his sense of superiority only lasted for two or three incense sticks of time … Now, Luo Zheng had already left him in the dust.
"This guy … He really shouldn't belong to the Condensing Sword Academy …" Qiu Siyuan sighed in his heart.
As Luo Zheng's sword-shaped pattern continued to grow, it also successfully attracted the attention of the other disciples.
These disciples were all proud of their talent, so how could they allow Luo Zheng to grow so quickly?

Chapter 2659
The Disciples' Gains
The True Intent Sword Battle was a compulsory event in the Academia.
These sword-shaped diagrams represented the level of comprehension of the True Intent of the Dao. When disciples of the same level engaged in a True Intent Sword Battle, not only would it stimulate the disciples' desire to win, both sides would also gain new insights during the battle.
Many disciples had broken through during the True Intent Sword Battle.
For example, Qiu Yi and Yue Bai Cheng had been engaged in the True Intent Sword Battle since the beginning. They had been chasing each other, and in a short period of time, they had surpassed their peers by a large margin and became the most dazzling new stars in the Dao Sword Palace.
However, the disciples who were attacking now naturally had a lot of hostility towards Luo Zheng.
As the two most outstanding academies in the Thirteen Academia, the Demon Sword Palace and the Burial Sword Palace naturally had to protect their authority.
If Luo Zheng was from the other academies, it would be fine. However, he was a disciple of the Condensing Sword Academy. In the eyes of the other academies, he was simply trash. Furthermore, Luo Zheng was not from the Qiu Clan or the Yue Bai Clan. How could they allow such a person to emerge?
"Stomp him down!"
"Slow him down!"
"If he wants to continue, he will have to pass through me first!"
These people had also comprehended over three thousand words of the Eternal Sword Intent. Their sword-shaped diagrams were similar to Luo Zheng's, and were even slightly stronger.
The diagrams surrounded him aggressively, and the True Intent aura on the towering sword instantly became several times denser!
"Zeng! Ding ding ding … "
When the sword-shaped diagrams struck Luo Zheng's sword-shaped diagram, there was a crisp sound of metal clashing. It was as though real swords and spears were clashing. The True Intent aura was transmitted to his soul through the black strand of hair.
If a normal disciple was attacked by so many people at the same time, they would not be able to withstand it. They would probably have a splitting headache and would have no choice but to stop.
However, Luo Zheng was only slightly obstructed by these people. He held the translation with one hand, and his voice became louder and louder. It was like a huge bell that constantly reverberated.
As for Luo Zheng's sword-shaped pattern, the true intent aura it emitted became increasingly pure. When the other three or four sword-shaped patterns collided, they were directly swept away!
When these sword-shaped diagrams were swept away, Luo Zheng's true intent was transmitted into the minds of the few disciples through the black strands. Following that, screams of agony could be heard coming from the rooftops of the Burial Sword Palace and Demon Sword Palace!
The disciples squatted down with their hands covering their heads, their faces filled with pain.
"This guy …"
"He's practically unharmed!"
"Is this really his first time cultivating the Sword Fate Eternal True Intent?"
"That's for sure. The towering sword can't be wrong!"
The sword-shaped pattern that appeared on the towering greatsword directly reflected the cultivator's comprehension of the True Intent. Everyone had seen Luo Zheng's sword-shaped pattern grow rapidly. How could this be wrong?
"Let's attack too!"
"If this fellow's comprehension of the Sword Fate Eternal Sword Intent is so thorough, it will be of great benefit to us!"
"The pain will only last for a while! Who knows, I might even be able to break through my bottleneck! "
When the disciples at the 3,000-plus word level saw this scene, their faces burned with passion.
The reason why the Dao Sword Palace encouraged sword battles of True Intent was that during the process of sword battles, they could often absorb the opponent's true intent aura. From there, the opponent's comprehension would be imprinted in their minds, and they would often be able to break through faster!
Moreover, the fiercer the opponent's counterattack, the better the effect.
Many disciples of the Dao Sword Palace had been stuck at the 3,000-plus word level for a long time. However, it was very difficult to break through the bottleneck of Dao Sword Intent with the help of external forces.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's image in their eyes had changed. The thoughts of suppressing Luo Zheng had completely disappeared. They seemed to have treated Luo Zheng as a great tonic elixir. With fanatical thoughts, they swarmed towards Luo Zheng.
When Luo Zheng was reciting the translation, he glanced sideways and saw a large sword-shaped pattern rushing towards him. He was shocked.
Were these guys crazy?
However, these disciples were challenging him to a sword battle of True Intent, so Luo Zheng had no choice.
He could only quickly comprehend the True Intent of Dao and allow his sword-shaped pattern to rise quickly and leave the 3,000-word area. However, his recitation speed was already at his limit. How could he not avoid the dense sword-shaped pattern?
Ding, ding, ding, ding …
Dozens of sword-shaped patterns flew towards Luo Zheng's sword-shaped pattern one after another. Almost every time there was a crisp "ding" sound, it was followed by a disciple's blood-curdling scream.
As a result, a spectacle suddenly appeared on the rooftop of the Twelve Academies.
Disciples constantly clutched their heads and screamed. Some even directly fainted while holding their heads. Among them, two disciples even flipped off the rooftop and fell into a bottomless abyss.
Of course, the Palace Masters naturally wouldn't let their disciples fall …
When the Grand Sword Master of the Dao Sword Palace saw this scene, he didn't know whether to laugh or cry. "This disciple called Luo Zheng seems to have a natural affinity with the Sword Dao Eternal True Intent …"
"It's not just … affinity … his soul … is also very strong. After all, he has entered the Sea of True Intent …" Qiu Hanyan revealed the truth.
The sword battle of True Intent initiated by the other disciples might not cause much trouble for Luo Zheng, but with so many disciples, it was hard to ignore. However, Luo Zheng was someone who had entered the Sea of True Intent. He could rely on his own soul to survive.
Qiu Shengshui nodded and said, "A strong soul is only one aspect. A strong soul alone cannot easily withstand a sword battle of True Intent. Both are indispensable …"
Just as he finished speaking, a surprised voice suddenly came from the rooftop of the Burial Sword Palace. "Hahaha, I understand now. I understand now. So that's how it is …"
The person who spoke was called Yue Baineng. He was also the first disciple to initiate a sword battle of True Intent against Luo Zheng.
When he clashed with Luo Zheng just now, the aura of True Intent invaded his mind and produced a pain that was comparable to the tearing of his soul. But after the pain subsided, he suddenly felt a sense of enlightenment in his mind!
He had been stuck at the stage of 3100 words for two years. But at this moment, he felt that he could easily cross over. Naturally, he was extremely excited! He almost couldn't wait to sit down cross-legged. Holding the translation in his hand, he silently recited. The sword pattern on his towering sword really started to rise slowly!
Not only him, but the other two disciples also had the same reaction. They were extremely excited and started to verify the enlightenment in their minds.
"This …"
When the Palace Masters saw the reactions of these three disciples, they were all stunned.
In order to let these academia disciples step into the Sea of True Intent as soon as possible, these Palace Lords had spent a lot of effort in private. But after spending so much effort and using so many methods, why not just have a True Intent Sword Duel with Luo Zheng?

Chapter 2660
Translator: Nyoi-Bo Studio Editor: Nyoi-Bo Studio
Breaking the record
But not long after Yue Baili finished speaking, the second batch of disciples who challenged Luo Zheng to a Sword Intent Duel also reacted.
Their reactions were exactly the same as the first three disciples. First, they were pleasantly surprised. Then, without any distractions, they began to recite the True Meaning of Dao to verify their own thoughts …
The second batch of disciples who challenged the Sword Intent Duel consisted of 20 to 30 disciples. These disciples all began to break through their own shackles. It was hard to say that this was a coincidence.
"After the disciples sparred with Luo Zheng, they were able to break through their bottlenecks immediately?"
"The True Meaning of Dao that he imbued was so miraculous?"
"This can only mean that the way he comprehended the Sword Intent of Eternity is very unique … or very accurate!"
At this moment, even the Grand Sword Master was shocked. His mouth was slightly agape as he said, "This, how could this be …"
The Grand Sword Master had been in charge of the "sword observation" for so long, but he had never seen such a bizarre thing!
Those Palace Lords were also shocked and secretly delighted. Luo Zheng had helped dozens of disciples break through at once. This was naturally a great help to the school!
"It should be that Luo Zheng himself is very compatible with the Sword Intent of Eternity. His True Meaning aura is very perfect. The aura left in the minds of the other disciples during the Sword Intent Duel is very pure, so the effect is naturally very good. This situation … I have heard of it once or twice, but it has never happened in our Dao Sword Palace," Qiu Hanyan said lightly.
The Towering Sword could also reflect the aura of the Sword Intent of Eternity to the disciples, but after all, the Towering Sword was giving guidance from the overall situation and was ultimately a dead object.
But Luo Zheng was completely in sync with the other disciples. His True Meaning was what the other disciples needed the most, so the effect was naturally much stronger than the Towering Sword.
The Palace Lord of the Demon Sword Palace blinked and an idea came to his mind. He said, "If this Luo Zheng really has it, should we stop Luo Zheng from cultivating and let him stay within the range of 3,000 words?"
Three thousand words was a bottleneck that was neither too big nor too small. Many disciples of the academy would be stuck at this stage. If Luo Zheng stayed at this stage, the cultivation speed of the academy disciples would naturally increase greatly in the future.
But these words were a bit too shameless. Who would be willing to sacrifice for others and delay their own cultivation?
Even Yue Baili felt that this suggestion was unreasonable and did not respond.
Qiu Sheng Shui sneered and said, "Qiu Yun 'er, your Demon Sword Palace's disciples are humans, but my Condensing Sword Academy's disciples aren't humans? Just the bunch of trash from your Demon Sword Palace added together wouldn't even be worth a single Luo Zheng! "

In the end, he's still an outsider. I'm doing this for the sake of the Qiu Clan, the Yuebai Clan, and even the entire Dao Sword Palace! "

At this moment, the towering sword that was seventy thousand feet tall suddenly trembled.

"Could it be related to Luo Zheng's Dao Enlightenment …" Someone guessed in a low voice.
At this moment, the Grand Sword Master seemed to have thought of something. He clapped his hands and nodded. "Yes! Indeed! "

The ability of this sound transmission array was similar to the Xuanyuan Gouyu. It could transmit sound over a long distance.

The palace masters were still stunned. They never thought that there would be a sound transmission array in the towering sword.
However, when Qiu Hanyan and the Grand Sword Master heard this voice, their bodies suddenly trembled. The two of them already knew the identity of the other party from the voice.
It really was the voice of the person who had planted the towering sword, Qiu Yinhe. The Dao Sword Palace was also created by this person.
Not only that, but Qiu Yinhe was also the ancestor of the Qiu Clan and the Bai Clan …
However, it was impossible for someone like Qiu Yinhe to stay in the Dao Sword Palace forever as he roamed the Motherworld.
Just as Qiu Yinhe had said, he had planted seven of these towering swords and created seven sects similar to the Dao Sword Palace. Moreover, they had all challenged states where demonic beasts ran rampant.
However, to this day, only two of the towering swords still existed.
One was the Dao Sword Palace, and the other was the Blood Sword City. Not long after the other five sects were established, they were attacked by peerless demonic beasts. After being defeated, they disappeared into history. Even the towering swords were destroyed by those peerless demonic beasts.
Now that he suddenly heard Qiu Yinhe's voice, the Grand Sword Master was the most excited. He had controlled the towering sword for so many years, but this was the first time he had seen the sound transmission array open!
"Senior Qiu, Senior Qiu …"

Chapter 2663
The Orthodox Human Race
Qiu Yinhe was a legendary existence in the Dao Sword Palace and even the entire Seven Stars State.
The Seven Stars State was not once called the Seven Stars State, but the Fierce Hou State. The race that occupied this state was the Fierce Hou Race.
There were only a small number of human beings in this province. These human beings had yet to be civilized, and were struggling to survive in the form of tribes.
At that time, the humans were at the bottom of the food chain. They could die at any moment from the mouths of the Fierce Hou Race's Monster Demons. These Monster Demons were extremely cruel and the divine citizens could not resist them.
It was Qiu Yinhe's appearance that changed this situation.
The Mirror Grasshopper that he killed was the fiercest Monster Demon in the Seven Stars State. It had occupied the Seven Stars State for millions of years and was infamous.
Thus, everyone in the Seven Stars State was familiar with the legends of Qiu Yinhe. Even the Tiandu State was the same, but the vast majority of people had never seen him in person.
Qiu Hanyan, as well as the Grand Swordsman and the Headmaster of the Academia who were present, could easily imagine how they felt when they heard this voice.
"Luo Zheng actually managed to lure Senior Qiu out," Qiu Shengshui smacked his lips.
Everyone was unable to contain their excitement. Only Luo Zheng remained calm.
He did not know what level Qiu Yinhe was at, but he had seen the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods, Fuxi, and Chiyou. No matter how powerful Qiu Yinhe was, he could not be above these people.
Qiu Yinhe ignored the Grand Sword Saint and said to Luo Zheng, "Disciple of the Dao Sword Palace, please come forward!"
Luo Zheng blinked and did not move from his spot.
The Grand Sword Saint panicked and said, "Luo Zheng, Senior Qiu wants you to come forward."
"Oh," Luo Zheng calmly replied.
Then, he lightly tapped the ground, and a gentle wind wrapped around him. His entire body floated toward the towering sword.
Just as he approached the towering sword, fine patterns appeared on the sword. These patterns immediately covered the entire sword, and a circular arched door appeared in the middle of the sword.
Seeing this arched door, the Grand Swordsman was also stunned.
The Grand Sword Arbiter was the person chosen by the Dao Sword Palace to control the towering sword. As a result, the Grand Sword Arbiter possessed great authority in the Dao Sword Palace. He was also the person who understood the towering sword the best. However, he had no idea that there was actually a door within the towering sword.
When Luo Zheng arrived at the entrance of the arched door, he paused for a moment and revealed a helpless smile before going in.
In fact, Luo Zheng didn't expect things to develop to this point.
It wasn't his idea to enter the Dao Sword Palace, but he thought that since he was already here, he might as well take things as they came. It wouldn't be a bad idea to master another Eternal Sword Luck True Intent.
However, he did not expect himself to be so compatible with the Eternal Sword Intent. Throughout the entire process, he did not feel the slightest bit of pressure and cultivated the complete version of the True Intent of Dao in one go.
Although Luo Zheng knew that this wasn't a bad thing, he couldn't help but feel a little hesitant.
The interior of the towering sword was not solid, and there was a relatively large space after entering through the arched door.
Luo Zheng floated in the center of the space, and the door behind him instantly disappeared. Then, Qiu Yinhe's voice sounded again, "What's your name?"
Luo Zheng looked around and answered very calmly, "My name is Luo Zheng."
"Luo?" Qiu Yinhe was slightly stunned.
When Qiu Yinhe took over the Seven Stars State, he bestowed the surnames of two major clans, "Qiu" and "Yue Bai". Qiu Yinhe thought that Luo Zheng should be a disciple born from the Qiu Clan and Yue Bai Clan. When he heard this surname, he naturally felt strange and asked again, "This surname is rare. Are you not a native of the Seven Stars State?"
As expected, he met another person who wanted to get to the bottom of things …
Luo Zheng cupped his hands and said, "I am not a native of the Seven Stars State. I was brought here by the Qiu Academy from the Tiandu State."
If Qiu Yinhe continued to ask about Luo Zheng's true origins, Luo Zheng could only make up a story.
The most important thing for him to do in his mother world was to not reveal the divine realm. That was the last hope and foundation of Jiuli.
Fortunately, Qiu Yinhe did not continue to ask. He changed the topic and said, "Since you joined the Dao Sword Palace, you must know the origin of the Dao Sword Palace?"
"I've heard a thing or two," Luo Zheng answered.
"Very good. I am the Tian Jiedushi of the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace. Back then, I laid down this towering sword to open up a path to the primal chaos. Since you can comprehend the Eternal Sword Fate with a single thought, it means that you have extraordinary talent in the way of the sword. My Tai Yi Heavenly Palace is the orthodox way of the sword in the world, and the Eternal Sword Fate was created by the Eastern Emperor. Are you willing to join my Tai Yi Heavenly Palace?" Qiu Yinhe said calmly.
The Dao Sword Palace was famous in the Seven Stars State, but in the mother world, it was only a small and remote power.
The towering sword was indeed powerful, but the other races were not afraid of the towering sword itself. They were afraid of the power behind the towering sword, the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace!
Qiu Yinhe had left behind a set of criteria for selecting disciples in the Dao Sword Palace. It was to divide the talents of the disciples into eight grades. Those who reached the sixth grade could be selected to enter the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace.
Over the years, the Dao Sword Palace had sent out many disciples with sixth grade talents.
Qiu Shengshui also had a sixth grade talent, but because of some other reasons, he missed this opportunity.
In the past hundred years, the Dao Sword Palace did not have enough manpower. They did not have a sixth grade talent, so they seemed to have withered. Who would have thought that Luo Zheng would suddenly appear?
A person who could comprehend the Dao with a single thought had already surpassed the criteria set by Qiu Yinhe. After all, comprehending the Dao with a single thought was rare even in the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace. Qiu Yinhe had never thought that such a genius would appear in such a small place.
He invited Luo Zheng in, which was a personal invitation to Luo Zheng.
"Just accept him. The Tai Yi Heavenly Palace's conditions are much better than this small place. It is one of the four orthodoxies of the human race ….. Wrong, I'm afraid there are only three orthodoxies left. Hehe, and Donghuang Taiyi is no weaker than the Dihong Clan," 9527 said.
"What nonsense are you talking about? I already have a master," Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Luo Zheng did not know what the three orthodoxies of the human race were, but he understood what 9527 meant.
The people of Jiuli were one of the orthodoxies of the human race. But now that Jiuli was destroyed, there was naturally one less orthodoxies.
Qiu Yinhe was very patient. He did not say anything and just quietly waited for Luo Zheng's response.
9527 had urged Luo Zheng to join the Dao Sword Palace in the hope that he would rely on a certain power to quickly improve himself. But he had no choice but to choose the Dao Sword Palace. After all, with Luo Zheng's strength, it was not realistic for him to travel across the continent.
Joining the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace through the Dao Sword Palace was beyond his expectations, but it was indeed a good choice.
After thinking it through, Luo Zheng replied, "I am willing to join the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace."
"Very good!" Qiu Yinhe said with a trace of a smile, "In that case, I will bestow upon you an opportunity as a greeting gift."
As his voice fell, a light blue ancient longsword appeared in the space inside the towering sword. This ancient longsword was illusory and emitted a trace of a unique aura.

Imperial Sword Seal
Qiu Yinhe smiled faintly and said, "This is the Body Enlightenment Sword, my Nirvana Token. Of course, what you see is only the projection of the Body Enlightenment Sword in the towering sword."
The Nirvana Token was very important to a Nirvana Realm master. Of course, Qiu Yinhe wouldn't place his own Nirvana Token in the Seven Stars State.
However, through special means, the Nirvana Token could be projected into a treasure.
For example, the Lingxiao Flying Boat relied on a Nirvana Token to allow the flying boat to have the speed to travel through the void.
The towering sword was a treasure that Qiu Yinhe created using his own Nirvana Token …
Luo Zheng looked at the pale golden ancient sword with a hint of confusion in his eyes. No matter how powerful the projection of the Nirvana Token was, it was impossible for it to be given to him. He hadn't stepped into the Nirvana Realm, so his inner world couldn't bear the Nirvana Token.
"The Body Enlightenment Sword is also a rare treasure in the Nirvana Realm. Its power isn't outstanding, but it can give you a 'Imperial Sword Seal,'" Qiu Yinhe said.
"Imperial Sword Seal? What's that? "Luo Zheng asked.
"You'll know soon enough. There's only one Imperial Sword Seal in this world. There's no other, hehe …" Qiu Yinhe said with a hint of pride.
The Body Enlightenment Sword floating in the air started to hum. After a few rounds, the tip of the Body Enlightenment Sword was aimed at Luo Zheng's chest!

Just as 9527 moved away, the ancient sword was already shooting towards Luo Zheng!
It was so fast that Luo Zheng didn't have time to react.
In the blink of an eye, the golden sword pierced through Luo Zheng's chest and came out of Luo Zheng's back.
Although Luo Zheng's body had been pierced by the sword, it was not a real sword, so it did not leave any wounds.

Luo Zheng unbuttoned his robe and saw a faint sword mark on his chest.
"This Imperial Sword Seal is my innate ability to comprehend the Body Sword. Even those old geezers are begging me to give them the Imperial Sword Seal. This meeting gift is very good, hehe …" Qiu Yinhe laughed again.
Luo Zheng secretly curled his lips. Although he didn't dare to deny Qiu Yinhe's strength, was it really good to boast like this?
"Senior Qiu hasn't told me the function of this Sword Control Seal …" Luo Zheng said.
"Do you have any high-grade swords?" Qiu Yinhe asked.
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly.

"This kind of scrap metal …" Qiu Yinhe was a little speechless.

Then he felt a burning sensation in his chest. A dark golden light shot out from the Imperial Sword Seal in his chest. "This power …" Luo Zheng's pupils shrank slightly.
