"Weng weng …"
Streaks of light golden filaments rushed in front of Luo Zheng. These filaments were moving in an irregular direction, but each one of them was unusually sharp.
"Slash, slash …"
These golden threads cut into the inner wall of the towering sword, leaving irregular grooves on the inner wall.
However, Luo Zheng didn't manage to cut open the towering sword. This towering sword had the ability to repair itself. In the blink of an eye, those grooves disappeared completely.
"The power is not bad," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile. He was faintly disappointed. Those faint golden threads were sharp, but there was still a certain gap between them and his Dao Fusion energy.
Qiu Yinhe seemed to have heard the disappointment in Luo Zheng's heart. He snorted faintly and said, "Just not bad? Now you're using this scrap to activate my Imperial Sword Seal. You'll understand the benefits of the Imperial Sword Seal in the future! "
Luo Zheng's heart jumped slightly. He immediately asked, "Senior Qiu, are you saying that this Imperial Sword Seal doesn't rely on my Dao True Intent to activate it?"
He just felt that the Imperial Sword Seal absorbed his Dao True Intent aura. He thought that this was just a Dao True Intent Divine Ability. In addition, its power was inferior to his Dao Fusion energy. Naturally, he wasn't too surprised.
"Of course not! It's related to the grade of the sword you're using, "Qiu Yinhe said.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's heart suddenly jumped wildly.
The magic treasures in the mother world should have another set of grades. As the standard sword of the Dao Sword Palace, this Azure Edge Sword was completely inferior. It wasn't even as good as some of the treasured swords Luo Zheng brought from the God Realm, including the "Kunluan Sword" and other top magic treasures from the God Realm.
Most importantly, the Sword of Tengshe was still with Luo Zheng …
If the Azure Edge Sword, which Qiu Yinhe called "scrap metal," could display such power, then what about the Sword of Tengshe?
Qiu Yinhe sensed Luo Zheng's emotions and chuckled. "It seems that you're carrying some amazing treasures?"
Luo Zheng's expression changed slightly. He wanted to say something to cover it up.
However, Qiu Yinhe continued, "Don't worry. How could I, Qiu Yinhe, have any ideas about a junior!"
Of course, he didn't know that Luo Zheng was holding Chiyou's former sword. If he knew, he would have a different idea.
"I've already given you the opportunity. You can leave now. Stay in the Dao Sword Palace obediently. I'll send people to the Seven Stars Province!" After Qiu Yinhe finished speaking, the circular arched door that had disappeared opened again.

City Extermination
Star Plucking City was the smallest city south of the Seven Stars Province. The most eye-catching building in the city was the thousand-foot-tall Star Plucking Tower.
This Star Plucking Tower was an outpost built by the Dao Sword Palace, and the Tianluo Guards were stationed there year-round.
Many of the Gods in Star Plucking City tried their luck, hoping to be accepted as disciples by the Tianluo Guards. Even if they didn't accept disciples, they could at least give them a few pointers.
This annoyed the Tianluo Guards to no end, so they could only lock down the Star Plucking Tower.
At the top of the tower, two azure-robed men were leaning against a wide cushion in boredom. One of them complained, "It's only been a month, and I can't take it anymore. I have to stand guard here for three years! Is the life of a Tianluo Guard so boring? "
The speaker was called Qiu Chen. Half a year ago, he had passed the Dao Sword Palace's test and become a one-star Tianluo Guard.
When Qiu Chen thought that he could go to other provinces and slay demons with the other Tianluo Guards, he was assigned this mission: stand guard over Star Plucking City for three years.
"You should be content. You only got three years, but I got five! After staying in this godforsaken place for so long, I don't even remember what Seven Stars City looks like, "the other youth replied.
This youth was called Yue Baihe, and he was also a one-star Tianluo Guard.
When a disciple of the Dao Sword Palace became a Tianluo Guard, their first mission was to stand guard over the major cities of the Seven Stars Province.
Under the protection of the towering sword, the Seven Stars Province was rarely invaded by Great Demons. This sort of mission was completely meaningless and extremely boring.
"Rumble …"
As the two were speaking, the ground began to rumble, and even the Star Plucking Tower began to sway.
Yue Baihe sensed the tremors and curiously asked, "What's going on?"
Qiu Chen frowned. "Is it an earthquake?"
The ground shook more and more violently, and the Star Picking Tower shook more and more violently. Many of the gods in Star Picking City also ran out. They all looked at each other, not knowing what was going on.
"Earthquake, my ass! What's that!? "
A massive fissure suddenly appeared in front of Star Plucking City.
This fissure rapidly expanded, and a thick vine emerged from it. Arabian Demonic Flowers took root in this vine.
In the blink of an eye, this vine had reached the skies of Plucking City!
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Hundreds of thousands of Arabian Flowers left their vines and plummeted to the ground.
Qiu Chen's mouth dropped open in disbelief at the sight of the Arabian Demonic Flowers. "Damn … it's the monster plants of the Tiandu Province!"
"How dare they invade the Seven Stars State! Are those Sand Nation people and Arabian demonic flowers seeking death? "Yue Baihe yelled.
Each Arabian Demonic Flower was more than one hundred feet tall, and in the first wave, all the buildings in Star Seizing City had collapsed.
The divine citizens madly fled, but the vast majority of them fell into the maws of the Arabian flowers. Even though a few True Gods were doing their best to resist, the Arabian Flowers were simply too numerous. How could they possibly be a match for them?
When Qiu Chen saw this, he pulled out his Qingfeng Sword. Strands of Dao Intent spread out from his sword.
He had become a Heavenly Net Guard precisely so that he could slay demons and devils like the seniors of the Dao Sword Palace. Not only was he fearless in the face of this apocalyptic scene, his blood even began to boil with excitement!
"What are you doing?" Yue Baihe stood in front of Qiu Chen and asked.
"Of course I'm going to kill these Arabian Demonic Flowers!"
Qiu Chen raised his sword and leaped into the air. Under the Sword Fate's Eternal Sword Intent, streaks of greenish-purple sword light slashed out.
This sword glow was so powerful that it was practically unstoppable. In the blink of an eye, several dozen Arabian Demonic Flowers had been cleaved in two.
These Arabian Demonic Flowers could barely deal with Upper True Gods, so how could they resist a Heavenly Net Guard like Qiu Chen?
Seeing that Qiu Chen was in the mood to kill, Yue Baihe shot to his side like a ghost and grabbed the hilt of his sword. "There are so many demonic flowers. Can you kill them all? That Demonic Flower King is a Fifteenth Heaven Great Monster. With its true body here, you will die without a grave. We need to retreat to the Dao Sword Palace! "
"I am a Heavenly Net Guard of the Dao Sword Palace. How can I …" Qiu Chen righteously said.

Just as he condensed the spatial ripple, a thick vine suddenly whipped towards the two of them!
Whoosh …
The Star Seizing Tower was smashed to pieces by the vine, and the two of them immediately felt an irresistible force hit them.
All the bones in their bodies shattered in an instant. The two Heaven's Net Guardians flew through the air like kites with their strings cut and fell into Star picking City.
The thick vine didn't stop there. It made a small arc and whipped the two of them from top to bottom!
Yue Baihe lay limp on the ground, his face covered in blood. He glared at the unconscious Qiu Chen and cursed, "Damn it! You killed me just like that!"
It had not been easy to become a Heavenly Net Guard, but he had died in the Seven Stars Province before he could even venture out. One could easily imagine the unwillingness in his heart.
"Boom …"
The vines came crashing down, and the terrifying power crushed everything below into dust.
The hundreds of thousands of divine citizens of Star Seizing City had been devoured in less than five minutes, and then the Arabian flowers once more took root in the massive vines and burrowed into the ground. They began to travel north, leaving behind a city of ghastly craters and ruins.
The massive sword tens of thousands of li away seemed crude, but it was actually Qiu Yinhe's painstaking creation.

The Great Maelstrom

Luo Zheng originally knew about this. After all, he had heard that the Arabian Demonic Flower King had crossed the Paramita and entered the Fifteenth Heaven.

When the topic of compatibility was mentioned, Luo Zheng thought of his Eternal Sword Fate and asked, "Does everyone have a True Intent of Dao that they are compatible with?"

If the compatibility is higher, then the speed of comprehending the True Intent of the Dao will be faster. Just like my compatibility with the Eternal Sword Dao is 90%! Ling Shuang smiled. "That's Dao Enlightenment! There are only a few who can do it. Those who can comprehend the Dao with a single thought will have great achievements in the future! "
So even the compatibility of the True Intent of Dao could be measured. Luo Zheng sighed softly in his heart. Then his compatibility with the Eternal Sword Dao should be 100%.
In the Divine Territory, it was extremely difficult for him to comprehend the True Intent of Nature. Presumably, his compatibility with the True Intent of Nature was not high. However, at that time, there was only one True Intent of Dao for him to comprehend.
"You're quite impressive to have grasped another True Intent of Dao in such a short period of time … I've even prepared a True Intent of Nature Dao for you. Now, it seems like I should get you another True Intent of Sword Fate Divine Ability?" Ling Shuang smiled and brought the topic back. She was still interested in Luo Zheng's two True Intents of Dao.
It should not be difficult for Luo Zheng to obtain the True Intent of Sword Dao Enlightenment after entering Great Yi Heaven Palace.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to decline, another two true intent small boats appeared not far to the right. These two true intent small boats were much larger in size. Each of them was 60 meters long, and standing on the true intent small boats was a massive body.
From the silhouette of the body, it could be seen that these were two huge mutated beasts.
These two mutated beasts sat on the wide True Intent boats, not making a sound and emitting a sinister aura.
"What's going on?"
At this time, even Ling Shuang realized that something was wrong.
The human race did not have a complete understanding of the Sea of True Intent. Hearing this, Luo Zheng felt an inexplicable sense of sadness.
Yesterday, the Dao Sword Palace was still prosperous. Today, it was ruined and had to flee.
The human race was really going to be small …
Qiu Shengshui and Qiu Hanyan stood at the front of the airship. They shook their heads when they saw this.
Their feelings for the Dao Sword Palace were much deeper than these young people. Naturally, they felt more sad.

Request for Help

In front of the palace gate.

Ming Xuan was not an unreasonable person. It was just that after falling into despair, he had been repeatedly disappointed. His personality had become very stubborn, and he was naturally unwilling to cooperate.

"Have you heard of the Dao Sword Palace?" Luo Zheng asked.

Before Luo Zheng could react, Ling Shuang's boat of True Intent dissipated in front of Luo Zheng.

Only this family could save Ming Xuan. There was no other family.

Sensing Luo Zheng's gaze, Qiu Junshan began to tremble. Then, Luo Zheng pointed at him. "He, and all of these Outsiders, deserve to die …."
Seeing that he was about to be sentenced to death, Qiu Junshan was about to use Greater Teleportation to escape before Luo Zheng could even finish his sentence.
Xie Shan and the rest of the Dark Rong people had the same thought. This was their last chance to escape ….
Lin Zhanting clenched his fist and punched towards the sky.
Streaks of formless fist prints distorted the space, erupting with a series of muffled booms.
Qiu Junshan and the rest had just condensed a space wave, and it was shattered by the fist prints. Their bodies, energy centers, and souls were also shattered.
Seeing this scene, the disciples of the academy were shocked.
That was the Palace Master of the Dao Sword Palace, but he was like a chicken in front of this strong man. He died in the same way as the rest of the Dark Rong people who had just entered the Nirvana Realm.
"Lin Zhanting" did not seem to have used all his strength, and he looked like he had everything under control.
This was the real almighty of the human race!
After Lin Zhanting killed these people, He Chi slowly came down. He stared at Qiu Hanyan and asked, "I wonder what happened to the Dao Sword Palace that it was destroyed after so many years?"
Lin Zhanting did not care about the Dao Sword Palace, but the Dao Sword Palace was a subordinate force of the Greater One Heavenly Palace. He Chi could not ignore it.
Qiu Hanyan said, "The person who was killed by Lord Lin …. was our Palace Master …."
Qiu Hanyan told him what had happened in the Dao Sword Palace these few days.
He Chi thought for a while with his hands behind his back. He said, "The Seven Stars Region is desolate. Qiu Yinhe was forced to open up a demon territory. It was very difficult. It was not easy for you to hold on for so long …."
"The value of this place is not as high as you think. Moreover, the trade route you opened up made the merchant alliance rich. I think the remaining people should leave this place and send them back to the Greater One Heavenly Palace for Qiu Yinhe to deal with," Lin Zhanting said.
Hearing this, the eyes of the disciples lit up.
In the eyes of genius disciples like Qiu Yi and Yue Baicheng, the Greater One Heavenly Palace was their lifelong goal.
It was just that with their talent, it was very difficult for them to be chosen by the Greater One Heavenly Palace. That was why Qiu Yi was jealous of Luo Zheng. If they could really go to the Greater One Heavenly Palace, it would be a blessing in disguise.
He Chi stared at Qiu Hanyan and said, "This is one way, but it depends on their choice."
Qiu Hanyan and Qiu Shengshui looked at each other. Their eyes were filled with determination.
They also yearned for the Greater One Heavenly Palace, especially Qiu Shengshui. With his talent and temperament, even if he went to the Greater One Heavenly Palace, he could become Tai Yiwei.
But they were born in the Dao Sword Palace. Now that the Dao Sword Palace was in trouble, how could they just leave?
Although the Seven Stars City was destroyed, there were still dozens of big cities in the Seven Stars Region. If they lost them and left, the tens of millions of people in these dozens of big cities would eventually be swallowed by the demons and the Seven Stars Region would return to desolation.
Only then did Qiu Hanyan tell Guru He Chi her thoughts.
Master He Chi did not force Qiu Hanyan. Instead, he helped Qiu Hanyan control the towering sword.
Although the Dao Sword Palace was destroyed, under the protection of the towering sword, he believed that there would be a day when it would flourish again.
Lin Zhanting and He Chi only stayed in the Seven Stars Region for half a day. After half a day, Master He Chi put Luo Zheng and the other hundreds of disciples into a small world and drove the Guiding Sword to bring Lin Zhanting on the road.
In this small world, Yue Baicheng, Qiu Yi and the others were very excited.
As for Qiu Siyuan, Lai Huabei and the others, they felt like they were in a dream …

An unpleasant meeting.

This wall was grayish brown, faintly emitting a metallic luster.

However, Great Yi Heaven Palace can't conduct an assessment every day, so they erected a wall here. Crossing this sword wall is the first step to joining Great Yi Heaven Palace."
Luo Zheng stared at the wall and asked, "This wall isn't that easy to cross, right?"
"It's easy to say it's easy, but it's also not easy to say it's not easy," Yue Baicheng said with a solemn expression, "If your compatibility with the sword is high, you can naturally succeed in one step. If not, I'm afraid you won't be able to cross this wall in your lifetime …"
Compatibility?
Hearing this, Luo Zheng remembered that Ling Shuang had mentioned this before. So Great Yi Heaven Palace's disciples relied on this wall to test their compatibility?
But according to what Ling Shuang said, if one could comprehend the Dao with one thought, it would mean 100% compatibility. He was very compatible with the sword, so it shouldn't be a problem for him to cross this wall, but it might be difficult for others.
"Scram! This is our territory, trash like you can go to the back and die! "
Just then, a berating voice was heard.
Luo Zheng turned his head to look and saw over twenty young men coming over aggressively. The leading young man in black carried two crossed swords on his back, his eyes faintly revealing a cold aura. This person was actually berating the academy disciples.
"Your territory? Your territory is engraved with your name? "Qiu Yi heard this and his face darkened.
When had they ever been bullied in Dao Sword Palace?

Conflict

Chapter 2690
Bombardment
There was a reason why they were called pariahs.
The Greater One Heavenly Palace occupied the Center God Province, giving birth to countless branches, and among these countless branches, there were countless families.
Surrounding the Center God Province, there were also a large number of humans. These humans wanted to join the Greater One Heavenly Palace as if they were on a pilgrimage.
In fact, the sword wall was not the first checkpoint to enter the Greater One Heavenly Palace. There were several checkpoints outside this mountain range, and after a simple screening, eighty percent of the people were stopped outside.
Luo Zheng and these disciples came down directly from the Great Yi Mountain, so naturally they skipped those checkpoints and came to the sword wall.
In the eyes of the natives of the Center God Province, anyone outside the Center God Province was a pariah.
The other onlookers saw this situation and were faintly worried for Luo Zheng, including Yue Baicheng, Qiu Yi, and others. After all, this was their territory.

Luo Zheng ignored him and replied, "You might even be worse than trash."

"It seems that you don't intend to compromise." Wang Ze slowly drew his sword from behind his back. "Of course the Heavenly Palace has its reasons, but there is only one reason. Trash like you deserve to die!"

Piercing cold air bloomed in all directions. The cold air contained the Sword Will of Eternity. It turned into frost rhombus swords and kept compressing into his longsword.
Luo Zheng's face was still indifferent when he felt this powerful aura.
This Wang Ze's aura was similar to Dongfang Chunjun's. Perhaps it was slightly weaker, but not by much.
When those frost rhombus swords were compressed, Wang Ze's figure moved and turned into a gust of wind that swept towards Luo Zheng. Whether it was the timing or the posture, they were all precise.
"Whoosh!"
This sword was aimed at Luo Zheng's forehead. He really wanted to take Luo Zheng's life.
"Dao of nature …"
Wang Ze was not a Nirvana Realm elite. Luo Zheng did not need to use the Sword of Tengshe and the Imperial Sword Seal.
However, as someone from the Center God Province, Wang Ze's True Meaning Divine Ability was also extremely powerful!
What Luo Zheng lacked was a Divine Ability that could use the True Meaning of Dao!
Wang Ze and Luo Zheng retreated. The two swept past everyone's heads like a gust of wind chasing after strong grass.
In the blink of an eye, the two had circled around in such a large area. The people below were afraid of being affected, so they all hid.
"You'll die no matter where you run!"
Wang Ze looked at Luo Zheng with disdain as he kept retreating. The tip of his sword turned slightly.
The frost rhombus swords compressed in Wang Ze's longsword flew out and scattered in all directions. After rolling a few times, they surrounded Luo Zheng. This time, Luo Zheng had nowhere to run!
Seeing these frost rhombus swords stabbing towards him, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and the energy of Dao Fusion dissipated.
At this moment, he did not retreat. Instead, he charged towards Wang Ze!
"After giving up on running, you want to fight to the death?" Wang Ze sneered. "Then I'll grant you death! Sword Annihilation Force! "
Facing Luo Zheng's sudden attack, Wang Ze suddenly waved his longsword. The frost rhombus swords that were shooting towards Luo Zheng suddenly exploded into tens of thousands of sharp fragments. An extremely cold aura mixed with the fragments of the rhombus swords enveloped Luo Zheng.
This "Sword Annihilation Force" was his Wang family's ultimate technique. Once it was successfully activated, it would take the opponent's life.
Within this area, it was a dead zone!
When Lai Huabei and the others saw Luo Zheng submerged in the white frost, the corners of their mouths couldn't help but tremble.
Even Qiu Yi's eyes were filled with sorrow. Even though Qiu Yi was jealous of Luo Zheng's talent, he had shown great loyalty on Taiyi Mountain. At the very least, he was fully qualified to stay on Taiyi Mountain. He had only left for the sake of these students who didn't want to be locked up.
"That kid is dead, right?" a white-robed disciple said calmly.
"The Sword Annihilation Force can be considered a true legacy of the Wang family. Its power is not bad," another white-robed disciple sighed.
A carefree smile appeared on Wang Shaoqi's face. He could imagine Luo Zheng being sliced to pieces by the frost.
Within the white mist, the temperature plummeted. Luo Zheng could feel the murderous intent coming from all directions.
Not only did his speed slow down, but the sharp shards hidden in the cold intent were constantly cutting at his body. Even the True Meaning of Nature could not avoid these shards.
If it had been anyone else, they would have been sliced to pieces in the blink of an eye. However, Luo Zheng was no ordinary person. His body glowed with a faint golden light, exuding a mysterious aura, and shattered the fragments of the rhombus sword into powder!
"Whoosh!"
In the next moment, Luo Zheng rushed out of the white mist.
Outside the white mist, Wang Ze's expression stiffened when he saw Luo Zheng come out unscathed.
He could sense Luo Zheng's form in the white mist. He was wondering why Luo Zheng's body hadn't been sliced to pieces. Then, he saw Luo Zheng come out unscathed. There wasn't even a trace of blood on his body. This was completely beyond Wang Ze's expectations.
When Wang Ze saw Luo Zheng's long sword flickering with the luster of Dao Fusion energy, his pupils suddenly shrank.
With his experience, he naturally couldn't recognize Dao Fusion energy. However, the violent aura contained in this energy reminded him of the power of this sword …
"I wonder who gave who the death sentence is?"
Luo Zheng said indifferently.

Stop!
"I wonder who's the one who's going to die?"
Luo Zheng's voice echoed in Wang Ze's ears.
At this moment, there was fear in Wang Ze's heart …
From the moment he came out of the wall, Wang Ze didn't put anyone in his eyes.
He had crossed the eighth stage, and the Heavenly Palace had given him many soul pills.
It wouldn't be long before he could enter the Nirvana Realm! He still had a bright future ahead of him!
How could Wang Ze know that death was so close to him?
"No! You can't kill me! "
He almost gave up on escaping, and just roared with all his might.
The white-robed youths saw this scene, and their faces changed greatly. They didn't expect such a sudden change.
"Stop!"
"If you kill Wang Ze, you'll die without a burial place!"
"You're just a lowly commoner, yet you dare to rebel!"
These threatening words were as light as a feather in Luo Zheng's ears, and his sword blasted towards Wang Ze without any suspense.
"Stop!"
At this moment, a white-robed old man appeared above the sword wall. This white-robed old man was a Nirvana Realm expert.
His roar made everyone dizzy, and even Luo Zheng's mood fluctuated a little.
But the sword in his hand still blasted forward without hesitation!
"Boom!"
In everyone's eyes, Wang Ze's whole body emitted a dazzling luster, and his whole body was annihilated under Luo Zheng's sword light.
Wang Ze was dead!
Wang Shaoqi looked at his brother's miserable state, and the corner of his mouth trembled.
The white-robed disciples' faces were also very ugly. They didn't expect Luo Zheng to be so bold, and actually dared to kill someone here!
"We're in big trouble …" Yue Baicheng said with a frown.
The Academia disciples also looked at each other, but Qiu Yi said, "Hmph, good kill!"
"Whoosh!"
The white-robed old man on the sword wall flew over, pointing at Luo Zheng, and said with a face full of anger, "How dare you, to dare to kill my Tai Yi Heavenly Palace's people under the sword wall, you! You're courting death! "
The white-robed youths also moved closer to the old man. This white-robed old man's status was clearly not low. The white-robed youths all had respectful looks on their faces.
Wang Shaoqi also flew over. With a mournful expression, he said with his toothless mouth, "Senior, you must seek justice for my brother, Wang Ze!"
The hearts of everyone shook when they saw a Paramita Realm expert make an appearance.
Although Luo Zheng had killed Wang Ze, it seemed that he would not be able to escape death …
Luo Zheng stood in the air and looked up at the white-robed old man. His face was full of indifference as he said, "As you said, the one courting death is not me, but Wang Ze!"
"What a joke! Wang Ze has already passed the Sword Wall, so he can barely be considered a member of the Heavenly Palace. He guards the Sword Wall for the Heavenly Palace and maintains order and rules, "a white-robed youth said coldly.
Luo Zheng's eyes were cold and sharp like a sword. "Maintain order and rules? Our group was waiting here, and Wang Ze's younger brother came to find trouble with us. All I did was teach him a lesson, and Wang Ze wanted to use the word 'rules' to take my life. Maintaining order and rules, and using that as an excuse to kill someone. How sharp! He was biased and wanted to take my life. Was I supposed to just stand there and let him kill me? Since he wanted to kill me, he should be prepared to be killed! Wang Ze was not strong enough, so he was naturally courting death! "
His words spread to the surroundings, and the True Gods present whispered to each other.
They had seen the entire process. It was indeed Wang Ze who was not strong enough and courted death!
"What a sharp tongue!" The white robed old man sneered, "But you are not a member of my Heavenly Palace. The difference between your status and Wang Ze's is like the difference between heaven and earth. Status is reason, strength is reason. Daring to kill a disciple of my Heavenly Palace is the same as courting death! You … deserve to die! "
The white-robed old man's surname was also Wang, and he came from the same clan as Wang Ze. How could he let Luo Zheng go with just a few words?
After speaking, the white-robed old man did not hesitate. He stretched out his hand, and a longsword appeared. Pink flowers bloomed from it.
"You're being unreasonable …"
Luo Zheng frowned.
The scene in front of him confirmed Luo Zheng's guess.
In the Realm of the Gods, there were a great number of rules that restricted disciples from killing each other.
Unless there was a life or death grudge between the Realm of the Gods, there were very few deaths from fighting.
The two most intense places were the forbidden area and the Arena of the Gods. But in the Arena of the Gods, no one would truly die. The forbidden area was mainly used to fight against vicious beasts …
In the end, the Realm of the Gods was dominated by the human race. There was not much of a sense of crisis.
But the Origin World was completely different. The human race was not the mainstream, and they had to contend with countless foreign races and monster races. If they wanted to become stronger, if they wanted to conquer other races, the internal disputes would be even more intense!
In the Dao Sword Palace, Luo Zheng had faintly felt this, but he didn't think that it would be even worse in the Greater One Heavenly Palace!
Seeing the white-robed old man about to kill him, Luo Zheng quickly retreated. His fingers were already on his spatial ring. At the same time, the Eternal True Meaning of Sword Fate began to brew.
The last time he used the Imperial Sword Seal, the Eternal True Meaning of Sword Fate within his body had disappeared without a trace. But after three or four days, it had slowly recovered.
If this old man really wanted to kill him, he would have no choice but to use this trump card and risk his life!
Besides this trump card, Luo Zheng had another life-saving talisman in his heart. Only he could save that fellow called Mingxuan. Would Lin Zhanting just watch as he was killed?
This was another reason why Luo Zheng was so unscrupulous.
"Play!"
Seeing the white-robed old man activate the pink flower, a gentle aura spread out from his sword.
Others might be bewitched by this gentle aura, and some might even be infatuated as if they had taken a drug. However, Luo Zheng saw the terrifying killing intent hidden in this aura with a single glance. The moment he sensed the killing intent, a thought appeared in Luo Zheng's mind. He couldn't resist this sword strike at all.
"The Sword of Tengshe … if I use it now, there will be no end to it. But there's no other way!"
Just as Luo Zheng's fingers were on his spatial ring and he was about to take out the Sword of Tengshe, a cool breeze gently blew.
This breeze was very light, but when it hit them in the face, it immediately made them feel refreshed!
Everyone who was originally in a daze felt a cool breeze on their faces, and their minds suddenly became clear.
At the same time, a faint voice sounded, "Wang Xiao, you can't kill this person …"
Along with the voice, another young man appeared. This young man wore an azure robe and had an indifferent expression.
"Why! Wang Ze can't die in vain! "Wang Xiao said stubbornly.
The long sword in his hand was still swung towards Luo Zheng, and a wisp of pink wind swept towards Luo Zheng.
Seeing that Wang Xiao was so disobedient, the indifferent young man's face suddenly twisted. His originally calm body suddenly moved, and the originally calm young man suddenly exploded with a terrifying power, smashing a fist on the white-robed old man.
"Boom!"
In the next moment, the white-robed old man was embedded into the mountain wall.

Wang Xiao was already a god-like figure in everyone's heart. After being punched out of the wall by this youth, the sword wind that was originally heading towards Luo Zheng also dissipated.
Seeing this scene, everyone was shocked. At the same time, they were also quite curious.
From this youth's tone, he seemed to be acquainted with Luo Zheng? Did he want to save Luo Zheng?
Who would have thought that after doing all this, the youth would only coldly glance at Luo Zheng before flying towards the top of the sword wall.
Wang Xiao struggled in the mountain wall for a while, and then came out.
His wrinkled face was filled with a fierce aura. He fiercely glared at Luo Zheng before leaving with a flick of his sleeves.
It was obvious that Wang Xiao wanted to swallow Luo Zheng whole. However, that youth had already warned him. Wang Xiao also knew the consequences of attacking Luo Zheng now, so he could only temporarily give up.
Anyone could see that Wang Xiao would definitely not let this matter go.
Those white-robed youths did not leave with Wang Xiao. The last white-robed youth floated towards Luo Zheng. He stared at Luo Zheng with a strange smile on his face. "Do you know that you're finished? If you leave now, Banner Lord Wang will definitely hunt you down. If you cross the sword wall, you'll also die. Heh heh heh … "
This white-robed youth was the one who had just made fun of Wang Ze.
Luo Zheng coldly glanced at the white-robed youth and said, "Since you're going to die anyway, what's the point of saying such nonsense to me?"
"Because if you enter the sword wall, I might have to personally deal with you. I'm not trash like Wang Ze. You'll rather die than fall into my hands. You might as well kill yourself." The white-robed youth laughed.
The corners of Luo Zheng's mouth curled up slightly. These people were really arrogant. He sneered and said, "My life has always been here. If you have the ability, come and take it away. However, I think trash like you and Wang Ze are just ordinary trash. You're not qualified!"
The white-robed youth's eyes flashed with a cold light, but when he thought of Wang Xiao's fate, he restrained himself in the end. "An arrogant lowly commoner like you won't be able to survive more than three days in the sword wall. I hope you can come in as soon as possible. Remember my name, my name is Zhao Hao!"
After saying that, his sleeves fluttered, and he was about to leave with the other white-robed youths. Those white-robed youths looked at Luo Zheng with indifferent eyes. In their hearts, Luo Zheng was no different from a dead man. It was only a matter of time before he died.
"Wait!" Luo Zheng suddenly shouted.
Zhao Hao turned his head and gave Luo Zheng a strange look. "Do you have any last words? There's no need for you to beg for mercy. Banner Lord Wang won't let you go, so we won't let you go either. "
"What's your name?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Zhao Hao," Zhao Hao replied.
"What?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"My name is Zhao …" Zhao Hao replied halfway. He finally understood that Luo Zheng was playing with him.
Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of apology. "I'm sorry, my memory is bad. I usually can't remember the names of trash."
The crowd below the sword wall immediately burst into laughter.
Yue Baicheng, Qiu Yi, and the other disciples also didn't dare to laugh out loud. Lai Huabei laughed in a low voice. "I didn't know that brother Luo Zheng would play with people like this …"
"You, you're looking for death!" Zhao Hao's face was dyed with a dark red color.
The other white-robed youths also revealed a murderous look in their eyes. Every time they came to the sword wall to maintain order, they were always in the limelight. This time, not only was Wang Ze killed by this person, he was also humiliated. How could they tolerate this?
Seeing that Zhao Hao's aura was increasing rapidly and was about to attack, the white-robed youth beside him pulled him back and said, "Zhao Hao, Master Lu just attacked. We can't kill him here."
Master Lu, who had attacked Wang Xiao before, had a high status. He was definitely not someone these white-robed youths could provoke.
"Don't be impulsive. When he enters the sword wall, we have a hundred ways to kill him." Another white-robed youth glanced at Luo Zheng and then sneered.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng looked at the white-robed youth with a smile and asked, "What's your name?"
The white-robed youth was about to answer, but thinking of Luo Zheng's previous teasing, his face darkened slightly. He snorted, and the white-robed youths flew away.
After these people left, Luo Zheng slowly descended from the air.
Wang Shaoqi saw that his brother was killed and Wang Xiao retreated. Now he also had a disheartened look. He was about to secretly leave with his people when Luo Zheng suddenly said, "You, wait a minute."
Wang Shaoqi's shoulders shook. He turned his head and asked vaguely, "Wh, what's the matter …"
The people he brought were also trembling with fear. Luo Zheng, this monster, could even casually kill people like Wang Ze. How could these people be qualified to challenge Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng stared at Wang Shaoqi and asked, "What's your compatibility with the Eternal Sword Intent?"
Wang Shaoqi thought that Luo Zheng wouldn't let him go. He didn't expect Luo Zheng to ask this. He answered without hesitation, "Twenty, twenty percent …"
"Only twenty percent?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
This compatibility was very low. Although the Towering Sword couldn't measure the compatibility, it could be roughly estimated based on the first recitation of the True Meaning of Dao.
For example, Luo Zheng comprehended the Dao with a single thought, and his compatibility was 100%. For example, Qiu Yi and Yue Baicheng, who could recite more than four thousand words at a time, their compatibility was about 40% or even higher.
This Wang Family Young Master's talent was really low. It was only 20%.
Even if Wang Shaoqi was placed in the Dao Sword Palace, he could only be considered a mediocre talent.
When the other disciples heard this, their hearts skipped a beat. They immediately understood what Luo Zheng meant.
With Luo Zheng's talent, crossing the Sword Wall was a sure thing. The disciples didn't doubt it at all. But what about the others? Qiu Yi and Yue Baicheng might be able to pass, but the other dozens of disciples would have a hard time.
However, Wang Shaoqi's compatibility was only 20%. If he wanted to cross the Sword Wall, it meant that the Sword Wall's difficulty was very low, or that there were other ways to make up for the compatibility …
"If it's only 20%, it should be very difficult to cross the Sword Wall. I think you're very confident?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Wang Shaoqi nodded. "I spent many years cultivating the Eternal True Meaning of Sword Fate. With my compatibility, it is indeed very difficult to cross the Sword Wall. But to be recognized by the Sword Wall, compatibility isn't the only way."
The Sword Wall had stood here for so long, and as a screening barrier, there would eventually be many people who were unwilling to come here to study it. But Greater One Heavenly Palace also turned a blind eye to this, and so there were many crooked and evil ways.
"What other ways are there?" Luo Zheng stared at Wang Shaoqi and asked.
